# PetCall 2.0 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Zbudować PetCall 2.0 — nowy rdzeń bez Ace3 i nowy natywny interfejs — jako jedno wydanie, z pełną zgodnością danych i funkcji 1.0.x.

**Architecture:** Rdzeń (`Core/`) to moduły bez zależności od UI, z publicznym API i callbackami opisanymi w spec §13; testowany automatycznie w czystym Lua 5.1 z atrapą API WoW. Interfejs (`UI/`) to ramki tworzone w Lua na szablonach Blizzarda, wołające wyłącznie API rdzenia; edytory warunków i reguł rysują się ze schematów typów. Prace idą pionowymi przekrojami (rdzeń + odpowiadający mu kawałek UI), więc niezgodność kontraktu wychodzi w trakcie, a nie po wydaniu.

**Tech Stack:** Lua 5.1 (WoW 12.1.5, Interface 120105), szablony/mixiny Blizzarda (`ButtonFrameTemplate`, `ScrollBox`, `MenuUtil`, `Settings`), `C_EncodingUtil`, LibStub + CallbackHandler-1.0 + LibDataBroker-1.1 + LibDBIcon-1.0, własny mini-framework testów (Lua 5.1; lokalnie przez `lupa`, w CI przez `lua5.1`), luacheck.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-petcall-2-ui-design.md` (interfejs) + `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-15-petcall-12.1.5-update.md` (etap 2: wymagania, architektura, lista błędów A–D do naprawy). Wykonawca czyta oba.

## Global Constraints

- `## Interface: 120105`; zero wywołań usuniętych/deprecated API — addon działa z `/console loadDeprecationFallbacks 0`.
- Biblioteki runtime wyłącznie: LibStub, CallbackHandler-1.0 (wymagany przez LDB i LibDBIcon), LibDataBroker-1.1, LibDBIcon-1.0. Żadnego Ace3, żadnego LibPetJournal.
- `PetCall3DB` czytany bez utraty: `profileKeys`, `profiles`, `namespaces.SpecialItems`, `_petleashMigrated`; pola zestawów `name`, `enabled`, `priority`, `defaultValue`, `immediate`, `pets`, `filter`, `trigger`. Rozszerzenia wyłącznie addytywne.
- Stringi `PetCall:1:` (AceSerializer) importują się; nowy eksport ma prefiks `PetCall:2:`.
- Wagi: `0, 1/64, 1/8, 1, 8, 64` (W=8, potęgi −2…2); priorytety zestawów: te same wartości bez 0; zestaw `$Default` ma priorytet −1.
- Nazwy zachowane dla zgodności: global `PetCall`, metody `PetCall:ResummonPet()`, `:DismissPet()`, `:TogglePet()`, `:OpenOptions()` (z `Bindings.xml`), bindingi `PETCALL_SUMMON/DESUMMON/TOGGLE/CONFIG`, komendy `/petcall` i `/pcall` z podkomendami `config resummon dismiss desummon togglePet enable dismissWhenStealthed debugstate migrate` + nowe `settings help`.
- Każdy tekst widoczny dla gracza przez `L[...]` z kluczem w `Locales/enUS.lua`.
- `## OptionalDeps: EllesmereUI`; integracja wyłącznie przez `EllesmereUI.RegisterSkin`.
- Komentarze w kodzie po angielsku, zwięzłe, jak w obecnym repo; wcięcia 4 spacje.
- Praca na gałęzi `petcall-2.0` (z `main`); commit po każdym zadaniu; nic nie jest wypychane bez zgody użytkownika.

## Review Focus

1. **Profil 1.0.x z wartościami domyślnymi wyciętymi przez AceDB** (np. zestaw bez pola `enabled`, `pets`, `priority`) — musi działać jak w 1.0.x (`enabled=true`, `priority=1`, `defaultValue=0`). Test: Task 4 „wildcard defaults”.
2. **Stare drzewo triggerów z mieszanką AND/OR na kilku poziomach** — po wczytaniu `Check` zestawu daje dokładnie ten sam wynik co 1.0.x w każdym stanie świata. Test: Task 13 „equivalence over all world states”.
3. **Konto bez petów / tylko pety „fanfare”** — żadnych błędów, siatka i menu pokazują pusty stan, `Ready` blokuje przywołanie (`have_pets`). Test: Task 6 i Task 11 (model siatki).
4. **Import stringa z kodem Lua i nieznanymi typami** — kod wyłączony, nieznane typy usunięte, brak wykonania kodu podczas importu. Test: Task 19.
5. **Zmiana profilu w trakcie gry** — zestawy, warunki (watchery) i namespace SpecialItems przeładowują się, stare watchery nie zostają zarejestrowane (brak podwójnych eventów). Test: Task 14 „profile switch frees watchers”.

## Doprecyzowania kontraktu ze spec §13

Plan realizuje kontrakt ze spec z tymi doprecyzowaniami (wykonawca trzyma się nazw z planu):

- `set:SetAutoRules(rules)` → edycja przez `ns.AutoRules.AddChip/RemoveChip/SetChipWeight/AddCriterion/RemoveCriterion/SetMatch/SetField/Simplify` na `set.settings.filter` + `set:FiltersChanged()` (chipy mają kształt zapisu 1.0.x, więc osobny model nie jest potrzebny).
- `Profiles:*` = `ns.DB:List/Current/Set/Copy/Reset/Delete` (`ns.Profiles` to alias `ns.DB`).
- `Ready:GetBlockers()` zwraca `{key, label}` (bez `detail` — szczegóły pokazuje dymek paska stanu z pełną listą).
- Dodatkowe callbacki: `AutoSummonChanged`, `SummonedPetChanged`, `SelectedSetChanged`, `WindowBuilt`, `WindowShown`, `WindowHidden`, `WindowResized`, `SpecialItemsChanged`.
- „O dodatku” jest podkategorią Ustawień (Task 21 aktualizuje spec).

### Task 1: Gałąź, framework testów, lokalne narzędzia, CI

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/lib/minitest.lua`, `tests/wowstub.lua`, `tests/run.lua`, `tests/smoke_spec.lua`, `tools/lua.py`, `tools/luacheck.py`
- Modify: `.github/workflows/lint.yml`, `.pkgmeta`, `.luacheckrc`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `describe(name, fn)`, `it(name, fn)`, `before_each(fn)`, `assert.equal(expected, actual, msg?)`, `assert.same(expected, actual)` (głębokie porównanie), `assert.is_true(v)`, `assert.is_false(v)`, `assert.is_nil(v)`, `assert.is_not_nil(v)`, `assert.has_error(fn)`, `assert.has_no_error(fn)`.
  - `local W = dofile("tests/wowstub.lua")`; `W.reset()` — świeży świat i świeży `_G` addonu; `W.load(opts)` — ładuje pliki z `PetCall.toc` (pomija `Libs\LibDBIcon*`, `UI\*` poza `opts.ui` = lista plików UI do załadowania) i zwraca `ns`; `W.fire(event, ...)` — wysyła event do ramek; `W.login()` — `ADDON_LOADED("PetCall")` + `PLAYER_LOGIN` + `PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD`; `W.world` — tabela stanu świata czytana przez stuby (patrz kod); `W.advance(seconds)` — przesuwa `GetTime()` i odpala timery `C_Timer`.
  - `python -I tools/lua.py <plik.lua> [args]` — uruchamia Lua 5.1 lokalnie; `python -I tools/luacheck.py [pliki…]` — luacheck z `.luacheckrc`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Lokalne narzędzia (Windows, bez kompilatora)**

`tools/lua.py`:

```python
"""Run a Lua 5.1 script via lupa (dev only). Usage: python -I tools/lua.py file.lua [args...]"""
import os
import sys

try:
    import lupa.lua51 as lupa
except ImportError:
    sys.exit("lupa is required: python -m pip install --user lupa")

root = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
os.chdir(root)
lua = lupa.LuaRuntime(unpack_returned_tuples=True)
lua.globals().arg = lua.table_from(sys.argv[2:])
run = lua.eval("function(p) local f = assert(loadfile(p)); return f() end")
code = run(sys.argv[1].replace("\\", "/"))
sys.exit(code if isinstance(code, int) else 0)
```

`tools/luacheck.py` — luacheck jest czystym Lua; ten skrypt uruchamia go pod lupa z podstawionym `lfs`:

```python
"""Run luacheck (pure Lua) under lupa with a Python-backed lfs (dev only).
Needs: python -m pip install --user lupa ; git clone https://github.com/lunarmodules/luacheck tools/.luacheck
       git clone https://github.com/luarocks/argparse tools/.argparse
Usage: python -I tools/luacheck.py [files...]   (default: Core UI tests)"""
import os
import sys
import lupa.lua51 as lupa

root = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
os.chdir(root)
args = sys.argv[1:] or ["Core", "UI", "Locales/enUS.lua"]
files = []
for a in args:
    if os.path.isdir(a):
        for d, _, fs in os.walk(a):
            files += [os.path.join(d, f).replace("\\", "/") for f in fs if f.endswith(".lua")]
    else:
        files.append(a)

lua = lupa.LuaRuntime(unpack_returned_tuples=True)

def attributes(path, attr=None):
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        return None
    info = {"mode": "directory" if os.path.isdir(path) else "file",
            "modification": int(os.stat(path).st_mtime), "size": os.stat(path).st_size}
    return info.get(attr) if attr else lua.table_from(info)

setup = lua.eval("""
function(py, lc, ap)
    package.path = lc .. "/src/?.lua;" .. lc .. "/src/?/init.lua;" .. ap .. "/src/?.lua;" .. package.path
    package.loaded.lfs = {
        currentdir = function() return py.cwd() end,
        attributes = function(p, a) return py.attributes(p, a) end,
        mkdir = function() return true end,
        dir = function(p) local t, i = py.listdir(p), 0; return function() i = i + 1; return t[i] end end,
    }
end""")
setup(lua.table_from({
    "cwd": lambda: os.getcwd().replace("\\", "/"),
    "attributes": attributes,
    "listdir": lambda p: lua.table_from([".", ".."] + os.listdir(p)),
}), "tools/.luacheck", "tools/.argparse")
lua.globals().arg = lua.table_from(files + ["--no-cache"])
run = lua.eval("""
function()
    local code = 0
    local exit = os.exit
    os.exit = function(c) code = (c == true and 0) or (c == false and 1) or c or 0; error("__exit__") end
    local ok, err = pcall(require, "luacheck.main")
    os.exit = exit
    if not ok and not tostring(err):find("__exit__") then io.stderr:write(tostring(err), "\\n"); return 99 end
    return code
end""")
sys.exit(run())
```

Dopisz do `.gitignore`:

```
tools/.luacheck/
tools/.argparse/
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Mini-framework testów** — `tests/lib/minitest.lua`:

```lua
-- Minimal busted-like test runner for plain Lua 5.1.
local M = { results = { passed = 0, failed = 0, errors = {} } }
local stack = {}

local function deepEqual(a, b, seen)
    if a == b then return true end
    if type(a) ~= "table" or type(b) ~= "table" then return false end
    seen = seen or {}
    if seen[a] == b then return true end
    seen[a] = b
    for k, v in pairs(a) do
        if not deepEqual(v, b[k], seen) then return false end
    end
    for k in pairs(b) do
        if a[k] == nil then return false end
    end
    return true
end

local function dump(v, depth)
    depth = depth or 0
    if type(v) ~= "table" then return type(v) == "string" and string.format("%q", v) or tostring(v) end
    if depth > 3 then return "{...}" end
    local parts = {}
    for k, x in pairs(v) do
        parts[#parts + 1] = "[" .. dump(k, depth + 1) .. "]=" .. dump(x, depth + 1)
    end
    table.sort(parts)
    return "{" .. table.concat(parts, ",") .. "}"
end

local function fail(msg) error({ minitest = true, msg = msg }, 3) end

assert = setmetatable({
    equal = function(e, a, m) if e ~= a then fail((m or "") .. " expected " .. dump(e) .. ", got " .. dump(a)) end end,
    same = function(e, a, m) if not deepEqual(e, a) then fail((m or "") .. " expected " .. dump(e) .. ", got " .. dump(a)) end end,
    is_true = function(v, m) if v ~= true then fail((m or "") .. " expected true, got " .. dump(v)) end end,
    is_false = function(v, m) if v ~= false then fail((m or "") .. " expected false, got " .. dump(v)) end end,
    is_nil = function(v, m) if v ~= nil then fail((m or "") .. " expected nil, got " .. dump(v)) end end,
    is_not_nil = function(v, m) if v == nil then fail((m or "") .. " expected non-nil") end end,
    has_error = function(f, m) if pcall(f) then fail((m or "") .. " expected an error") end end,
    has_no_error = function(f, m)
        local ok, err = pcall(f)
        if not ok then fail((m or "") .. " unexpected error: " .. tostring(type(err) == "table" and err.msg or err)) end
    end,
}, { __call = function(_, v, m) if not v then fail(m or "assertion failed") end return v end })

function describe(name, fn)
    stack[#stack + 1] = { name = name, before = {} }
    fn()
    stack[#stack] = nil
end

function before_each(fn)
    local b = stack[#stack].before
    b[#b + 1] = fn
end

function it(name, fn)
    local names, befores = {}, {}
    for _, s in ipairs(stack) do
        names[#names + 1] = s.name
        for _, b in ipairs(s.before) do befores[#befores + 1] = b end
    end
    names[#names + 1] = name
    local full = table.concat(names, " > ")
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        for _, b in ipairs(befores) do b() end
        fn()
    end)
    if ok then
        M.results.passed = M.results.passed + 1
    else
        M.results.failed = M.results.failed + 1
        local msg = type(err) == "table" and err.msg or tostring(err)
        M.results.errors[#M.results.errors + 1] = full .. "\n    " .. msg
    end
end

return M
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Atrapa API WoW** — `tests/wowstub.lua`. Zasada: każda funkcja WoW używana przez `Core/` ma stub czytający `W.world`; gdy kod sięgnie po nieznany global, test pada z czytelnym błędem (`strict` na `_G` po załadowaniu).

```lua
-- WoW API stub for Core/ unit tests. Not loaded in game.
local W = {}

local function noop() end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------ frames
local frames, timers
local FrameMT = {}
FrameMT.__index = function(t, k) return FrameMT[k] or noop end
function FrameMT:RegisterEvent(e) self._events[e] = true end
function FrameMT:RegisterUnitEvent(e, ...) self._events[e] = { ... } end
function FrameMT:UnregisterEvent(e) self._events[e] = nil end
function FrameMT:UnregisterAllEvents() self._events = {} end
function FrameMT:IsEventRegistered(e) return self._events[e] ~= nil end
function FrameMT:SetScript(n, f) self._scripts[n] = f end
function FrameMT:GetScript(n) return self._scripts[n] end
function FrameMT:HookScript(n, f)
    local old = self._scripts[n]
    self._scripts[n] = function(...) if old then old(...) end f(...) end
end
function FrameMT:IsShown() return self._shown end
function FrameMT:Show() self._shown = true end
function FrameMT:Hide() self._shown = false end
function FrameMT:CreateTexture() return W.newFrame() end
function FrameMT:CreateFontString() return W.newFrame() end

function W.newFrame()
    local f = setmetatable({ _events = {}, _scripts = {}, _shown = true }, FrameMT)
    frames[#frames + 1] = f
    return f
end

function W.fire(event, ...)
    local unit = ...
    for _, f in ipairs(frames) do
        local reg = f._events[event]
        if reg and f._scripts.OnEvent then
            if reg == true or reg[1] == unit or reg[2] == unit then
                f._scripts.OnEvent(f, event, ...)
            end
        end
    end
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------ world
local function defaultWorld()
    return {
        time = 1000, gameHour = 12, gameMinute = 0,
        zone = "Stormwind City", subzone = "Trade District", mapID = 84, mapParents = { [84] = 37, [37] = 13 },
        resting = true, instanceType = "none", inGroup = false, inRaid = false, delve = false,
        class = "HUNTER", className = "Hunter", spec = 1, playerName = "Tester", realm = "Realm",
        combat = false, flying = false, falling = false, mounted = false, stealthed = false, dead = false,
        vehicle = false, afk = false, casting = false, fullControl = true,
        auras = {}, items = {}, equipped = {}, quests = {},
        pets = {}, summoned = nil, cooldowns = {}, favorites = {},
        macroResult = nil, locale = "enUS",
        printed = {},
    }
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------ install
function W.reset()
    frames, timers = {}, {}
    W.world = defaultWorld()
    local w = W.world
    -- WoW Lua extensions
    wipe = function(t) for k in pairs(t) do t[k] = nil end return t end
    tinsert, tremove, tconcat = table.insert, table.remove, table.concat
    format, strfind, strlower, strupper, strsub, strmatch, gsub, strsplit =
        string.format, string.find, string.lower, string.upper, string.sub, string.match, string.gsub,
        function(sep, s)
            local out = {}
            for piece in string.gmatch(s, "([^" .. sep .. "]+)") do out[#out + 1] = piece end
            return unpack(out)
        end
    strtrim = function(s) return (s:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")) end
    string.trim = strtrim
    floor, ceil, max, min, abs = math.floor, math.ceil, math.max, math.min, math.abs
    fastrandom = function() return W.random and W.random() or math.random() end
    CopyTable = function(t)
        local c = {}
        for k, v in pairs(t) do c[k] = type(v) == "table" and CopyTable(v) or v end
        return c
    end
    securecallfunction = function(f, ...) return f(...) end
    -- WoW's xpcall forwards extra args
    local xp = xpcall
    xpcall = function(f, h, ...)
        local n, a = select("#", ...), { ... }
        return xp(function() return f(unpack(a, 1, n)) end, h)
    end
    geterrorhandler = function() return function(e) error(e, 0) end end
    hooksecurefunc = function(t, k, f)
        if type(t) == "string" then t, k, f = _G, t, k end
        assert(type(t[k]) == "function", "hooksecurefunc: " .. tostring(k) .. " is not a function")
        local orig = t[k]
        t[k] = function(...) local r = { orig(...) } f(...) return unpack(r) end
    end
    CreateFrame = function() return W.newFrame() end
    UIParent = W.newFrame()
    DEFAULT_CHAT_FRAME = { AddMessage = function(_, m) w.printed[#w.printed + 1] = m end }
    StaticPopupDialogs = {}
    StaticPopup_Show = function(name) w.popup = name end
    SlashCmdList = {}
    UISpecialFrames = {}
    -- game state
    GetTime = function() return w.time end
    GetGameTime = function() return w.gameHour, w.gameMinute end
    GetLocale = function() return w.locale end
    GetBuildInfo = function() return "12.1.5", "70100", "Oct 13 2026", 120105 end
    IsLoggedIn = function() return true end
    UnitName = function() return w.playerName end
    GetRealmName = function() return w.realm end
    UnitClass = function() return w.className, w.class end
    UnitFactionGroup = function() return "Alliance" end
    GetCurrentRegion = function() return 3 end
    GetZoneText = function() return w.zone end
    GetSubZoneText = function() return w.subzone end
    GetRealZoneText = function() return w.zone end
    IsResting = function() return w.resting end
    IsInInstance = function() return w.instanceType ~= "none", w.instanceType end
    IsInGroup = function() return w.inGroup or w.inRaid end
    IsInRaid = function() return w.inRaid end
    InCombatLockdown = function() return w.combat end
    IsFlying = function() return w.flying end
    IsFalling = function() return w.falling end
    IsMounted = function() return w.mounted end
    IsStealthed = function() return w.stealthed end
    HasFullControl = function() return w.fullControl end
    UnitIsDeadOrGhost = function() return w.dead end
    UnitInVehicle = function() return w.vehicle end
    UnitIsAFK = function() return w.afk end
    UnitCastingInfo = function() return w.casting and "Spell" or nil end
    UnitChannelInfo = function() return nil end
    SecureCmdOptionParse = function() return w.macroResult end
    JumpOrAscendStart, SitStandOrDescendStart = noop, noop
    GetBindingKey = function() return nil end
    PlaySound = noop
    ReloadUI = function() w.reloaded = true end
    ERR_MUST_BE_STANDING = "You must be standing to do that"
    SPELL_FAILED_NOT_STANDING = "You must be standing to do that"
    DEFAULT, ZONE, PARTY, RAID, CLASS = "Default", "Zone", "Party", "Raid", "Class"
    CLASS_SORT_ORDER = { "HUNTER", "WARRIOR" }
    LOCALIZED_CLASS_NAMES_MALE = { HUNTER = "Hunter", WARRIOR = "Warrior" }
    Enum = { PetJournalFilter = {} }
    C_Timer = {
        After = function(sec, fn) timers[#timers + 1] = { at = w.time + sec, fn = fn } end,
        NewTimer = function(sec, fn)
            local t = { at = w.time + sec, fn = fn }
            t.Cancel = function() t.cancelled = true end
            t.IsCancelled = function() return t.cancelled end
            timers[#timers + 1] = t
            return t
        end,
        NewTicker = function(sec, fn)
            local t = { at = w.time + sec, fn = fn, every = sec }
            t.Cancel = function() t.cancelled = true end
            t.IsCancelled = function() return t.cancelled end
            timers[#timers + 1] = t
            return t
        end,
    }
    C_PetJournal = {
        GetOwnedPetIDs = function()
            local r = {}
            for id in pairs(w.pets) do r[#r + 1] = id end
            table.sort(r)
            return r
        end,
        GetPetInfoByPetID = function(id)
            local p = w.pets[id]
            if not p then return nil end
            return p.species, p.custom, p.level or 1, 0, 0, 0, w.favorites[id] or false,
                p.name, "icon" .. p.species, p.type or 1, p.creature or (1000 + p.species)
        end,
        GetPetStats = function(id) local p = w.pets[id] return 100, 100, 10, 10, p and (p.quality or 2) end,
        PetIsSummonable = function(id) local p = w.pets[id] return p ~= nil and not p.unsummonable end,
        PetNeedsFanfare = function(id) local p = w.pets[id] return p ~= nil and p.fanfare == true end,
        GetSummonedPetGUID = function() return w.summoned end,
        SummonPetByGUID = function(id)
            if w.summoned == id then w.summoned = nil else w.summoned = id end
            w.summonCalls = (w.summonCalls or 0) + 1
        end,
        GetPetCooldownByGUID = function(id) return w.cooldowns[id] or 0 end,
        SetPetLoadOutInfo = noop,
        SetCustomName = noop,
        SetFavorite = function(id, v) w.favorites[id] = v == 1 end,
        GetNumPetTypes = function() return 10 end,
        GetPetInfoBySpeciesID = function(s) return "Species" .. s, "icon", 1, 1000 + s, "Source " .. s end,
    }
    C_Item = {
        GetItemCount = function(id) return w.items[id] or 0 end,
        GetItemInfo = function(id) return "Item" .. id end,
        IsEquippedItem = function(id) return w.equipped[id] == true end,
        RequestLoadItemDataByID = noop,
        GetItemQualityColor = function() return 1, 1, 1 end,
    }
    C_ChatInfo = { SendChatMessage = noop }
    C_SpecializationInfo = {
        GetSpecialization = function() return w.spec end,
        GetSpecializationInfo = function(i) return i, "Spec" .. i end,
    }
    C_UnitAuras = { GetPlayerAuraBySpellID = function(id) return w.auras[id] and { spellId = id } or nil end }
    C_Map = {
        GetBestMapForUnit = function() return w.mapID end,
        GetMapInfo = function(id) return { mapID = id, name = "Map" .. id, parentMapID = w.mapParents[id] or 0 } end,
    }
    C_PartyInfo = { IsDelveInProgress = function() return w.delve end }
    C_QuestLog = {
        GetNumQuestLogEntries = function() return #w.quests end,
        GetInfo = function(i) return w.quests[i] and { questID = w.quests[i], isHeader = false } or nil end,
        GetTitleForQuestID = function(q) return "Quest" .. q end,
    }
    C_Spell = { GetSpellInfo = function(id) return { name = "Spell" .. id } end }
    C_AddOns = { GetAddOnMetadata = function(_, k) return k == "Version" and "2.0.0-test" or nil end }
    C_EncodingUtil = {
        SerializeCBOR = function(v) W._cbor = W._cbor or {} W._cbor[#W._cbor + 1] = v return "CBOR" .. #W._cbor end,
        DeserializeCBOR = function(s) local i = tonumber(s:match("^CBOR(%d+)$")) return i and W._cbor[i] end,
        CompressString = function(s) return "Z" .. s end,
        DecompressString = function(s) return s:match("^Z(.*)$") end,
        EncodeBase64 = function(s) return "B" .. s end,
        DecodeBase64 = function(s) return s:match("^B(.*)$") end,
    }
    PetCall3DB, PetLeash3DB, PetCall = nil, nil, nil
end

function W.advance(seconds)
    local w = W.world
    local target = w.time + seconds
    while true do
        table.sort(timers, function(a, b) return a.at < b.at end)
        local t = timers[1]
        if not t or t.at > target then break end
        table.remove(timers, 1)
        w.time = t.at
        if not t.cancelled then
            t.fn(t)
            if t.every and not t.cancelled then
                t.at = w.time + t.every
                timers[#timers + 1] = t
            end
        end
    end
    w.time = target
end

-- Reads PetCall.toc and returns the addon files in load order.
function W.tocFiles(opts)
    opts = opts or {}
    local uiAllowed = {}
    for _, f in ipairs(opts.ui or {}) do uiAllowed[f] = true end
    local list = {}
    for line in io.lines("PetCall.toc") do
        line = line:gsub("\r", "")
        if line ~= "" and not line:match("^#") then
            local path = line:gsub("\\", "/")
            local skip = path:match("^Libs/LibDBIcon") or path:match("^Libs/LibDataBroker")
                or (path:match("^UI/") and not uiAllowed[path])
            if not skip then list[#list + 1] = path end
        end
    end
    return list
end

function W.load(opts)
    local ns = {}
    for _, path in ipairs(W.tocFiles(opts)) do
        local chunk = assert(loadfile(path))
        chunk("PetCall", ns)
    end
    return ns
end

function W.login()
    W.fire("ADDON_LOADED", "PetCall")
    W.fire("PLAYER_LOGIN")
    W.fire("PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD", true, false)
    W.advance(1)
end

W.reset()
return W
```

> Ścieżki w TOC od Task 2 używają `\` (np. `Core\Addon.lua`); `W.tocFiles` zamienia je na `/`.

```lua
    "tests/smoke_spec.lua",
}
for _, s in ipairs(arg or {}) do specs = { s } break end

    if not ok then

end
```

### Task 2: Szkielet addonu — moduły, eventy, timery, callbacki

**Files:**
- Create: `Core/Addon.lua`, `tests/addon_spec.lua`
- Modify: `PetCall.toc` (nowa lista ładowania), `tests/run.lua` (dopisz spec)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 (`W.load`, `W.fire`, `W.advance`).
- Produces (`ns` = tabela addonu, global `PetCall = ns`):
  - `ns.name = "PetCall"`, `ns.version` (z TOC)
  - `ns:NewModule(name) → module`; `ns:GetModule(name) → module`; moduły dostają `OnInitialize()` (po `ADDON_LOADED` i `ns.DB:Load()`) i `OnEnable()` (po `PLAYER_LOGIN`), w kolejności tworzenia.
  - metody modułu: `:RegisterEvent(event, handler?)`, `:RegisterUnitEvent(event, unit, handler?)` (tylko `"player"`), `:UnregisterEvent(event)`, `:UnregisterAllEvents()`; `handler` = nazwa metody lub funkcja, domyślnie metoda o nazwie eventu; wywołanie `handler(self, event, ...)`.
  - eventy można też rejestrować na dowolnym obiekcie: `ns.Events.Register(obj, event, handler)`, `ns.Events.RegisterUnit(obj, event, unit, handler)`, `ns.Events.Unregister(obj, event)`, `ns.Events.UnregisterAll(obj)`.
  - `ns:RegisterCallback(owner, name, fn)`, `ns:UnregisterCallback(owner, name)`, `ns:UnregisterAllCallbacks(owner)`, `ns:Fire(name, ...)`; `fn(owner, ...)`.
  - `ns:After(sec, fn) → handle`, `ns:NewTicker(sec, fn) → handle`; `handle:Cancel()`.
  - `ns.Debounce(delay, fn) → trigger()` — wielokrotne `trigger()` w oknie `delay` wywołuje `fn` raz.
  - `ns:Print(msg)`, `ns:Printf(fmt, ...)` — prefiks `|cff33ff99PetCall|r:`.

```lua
local W = dofile("tests/wowstub.lua")

describe("Addon core", function()
    local ns
    before_each(function()
        W.reset()
        ns = W.load()
    end)

    it("exposes the global", function()
        assert.equal(ns, PetCall)
    end)

    it("runs OnInitialize then OnEnable in creation order", function()
        local log = {}
        local a = ns:NewModule("A")
        function a:OnInitialize() log[#log + 1] = "Ai" end
        function a:OnEnable() log[#log + 1] = "Ae" end
        local b = ns:NewModule("B")
        function b:OnInitialize() log[#log + 1] = "Bi" end
        W.login()
        assert.same({ "Ai", "Bi", "Ae" }, log)
        assert.equal(a, ns:GetModule("A"))
    end)

    it("dispatches events to methods and functions", function()
        local m = ns:NewModule("M")
        local got = {}
        function m:ZONE_CHANGED(event, x) got[#got + 1] = event .. x end
        m:RegisterEvent("ZONE_CHANGED")
        m:RegisterEvent("PLAYER_DEAD", function(self, event) got[#got + 1] = event end)
        W.fire("ZONE_CHANGED", "!")
        W.fire("PLAYER_DEAD")
        m:UnregisterEvent("ZONE_CHANGED")
        W.fire("ZONE_CHANGED", "?")
        assert.same({ "ZONE_CHANGED!", "PLAYER_DEAD" }, got)
    end)

    it("delivers unit events only for the player", function()
        local m = ns:NewModule("U")
        local n = 0
        m:RegisterUnitEvent("UNIT_AURA", "player", function() n = n + 1 end)
        W.fire("UNIT_AURA", "raid3")
        W.fire("UNIT_AURA", "player")
        assert.equal(1, n)
    end)

    it("fires callbacks and unregisters owners", function()
        local owner, got = {}, nil
        ns:RegisterCallback(owner, "SetsChanged", function(o, name) got = name end)
        ns:Fire("SetsChanged", "X")
        assert.equal("X", got)
        ns:UnregisterAllCallbacks(owner)
        ns:Fire("SetsChanged", "Y")
        assert.equal("X", got)
    end)

    it("debounces", function()
        local n = 0
        local trigger = ns.Debounce(0.5, function() n = n + 1 end)
        trigger() trigger() trigger()
        W.advance(0.6)
        assert.equal(1, n)
    end)
end)
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implementacja** — `Core/Addon.lua`:

```lua
local ADDON, ns = ...
_G.PetCall = ns

ns.name = ADDON
local tocVersion = C_AddOns.GetAddOnMetadata(ADDON, "Version")
ns.version = (tocVersion and not tocVersion:find("@")) and tocVersion or "dev"

local pairs, ipairs, type, select, tostring = pairs, ipairs, type, select, tostring

--
-- Events
--

local Events = {}
ns.Events = Events

local handlers = {}        -- event -> { [obj] = handler }
local unitHandlers = {}    -- event -> { [obj] = handler }
local frame = CreateFrame("Frame")
local unitFrame = CreateFrame("Frame")

local function dispatch(map, event, ...)
    local list = map[event]
    if not list then return end
    for obj, handler in pairs(list) do
        if type(handler) == "string" then
            obj[handler](obj, event, ...)
        else
            handler(obj, event, ...)
        end
    end
end

frame:SetScript("OnEvent", function(_, event, ...) dispatch(handlers, event, ...) end)
unitFrame:SetScript("OnEvent", function(_, event, ...) dispatch(unitHandlers, event, ...) end)

function Events.Register(obj, event, handler)
    handlers[event] = handlers[event] or {}
    if not next(handlers[event]) then frame:RegisterEvent(event) end
    handlers[event][obj] = handler or event
end

function Events.RegisterUnit(obj, event, unit, handler)
    assert(unit == "player", "only player unit events are supported")
    unitHandlers[event] = unitHandlers[event] or {}
    if not next(unitHandlers[event]) then unitFrame:RegisterUnitEvent(event, "player") end
    unitHandlers[event][obj] = handler or event
end

function Events.Unregister(obj, event)
    for _, map in ipairs({ handlers, unitHandlers }) do
        local list = map[event]
        if list and list[obj] then
            list[obj] = nil
            if not next(list) then
                (map == handlers and frame or unitFrame):UnregisterEvent(event)
            end
        end
    end
end

function Events.UnregisterAll(obj)
    for _, map in ipairs({ handlers, unitHandlers }) do
        for event, list in pairs(map) do
            if list[obj] then Events.Unregister(obj, event) end
        end
    end
end

--
-- Callbacks
--

local callbacks = {}   -- name -> { [owner] = fn }

function ns:RegisterCallback(owner, name, fn)
    callbacks[name] = callbacks[name] or {}
    callbacks[name][owner] = fn
end

function ns:UnregisterCallback(owner, name)
    if callbacks[name] then callbacks[name][owner] = nil end
end

function ns:UnregisterAllCallbacks(owner)
    for _, list in pairs(callbacks) do list[owner] = nil end
end

function ns:Fire(name, ...)
    local list = callbacks[name]
    if not list then return end
    -- copy: a callback may (un)register while we iterate
    local snapshot = {}
    for owner, fn in pairs(list) do snapshot[owner] = fn end
    for owner, fn in pairs(snapshot) do
        local ok, err = pcall(fn, owner, ...)
        if not ok then geterrorhandler()(err) end
    end
end

--
-- Timers
--

function ns:After(sec, fn)
    return C_Timer.NewTimer(sec, fn)
end

function ns:NewTicker(sec, fn)
    return C_Timer.NewTicker(sec, fn)
end

function ns.Debounce(delay, fn)
    local pending
    return function()
        if pending then return end
        pending = C_Timer.NewTimer(delay, function()
            pending = nil
            fn()
        end)
    end
end

--
-- Output
--

function ns:Print(msg)
    DEFAULT_CHAT_FRAME:AddMessage("|cff33ff99PetCall|r: " .. tostring(msg))
end

function ns:Printf(fmt, ...)
    self:Print(format(fmt, ...))
end

--
-- Modules
--

local ModuleMixin = {}
function ModuleMixin:RegisterEvent(event, handler) Events.Register(self, event, handler) end
function ModuleMixin:RegisterUnitEvent(event, unit, handler) Events.RegisterUnit(self, event, unit, handler) end
function ModuleMixin:UnregisterEvent(event) Events.Unregister(self, event) end
function ModuleMixin:UnregisterAllEvents() Events.UnregisterAll(self) end
ns.ModuleMixin = ModuleMixin

ns.modules, ns.moduleOrder = {}, {}

function ns:NewModule(name)
    assert(not self.modules[name], "module exists: " .. name)
    local m = { name = name }
    for k, v in pairs(ModuleMixin) do m[k] = v end
    self.modules[name] = m
    self.moduleOrder[#self.moduleOrder + 1] = m
    return m
end

function ns:GetModule(name)
    return self.modules[name]
end

local function callAll(method)
    for _, m in ipairs(ns.moduleOrder) do
        if m[method] then
            local ok, err = pcall(m[method], m)
            if not ok then geterrorhandler()(err) end
        end
    end
end

local lifecycle = {}
Events.Register(lifecycle, "ADDON_LOADED", function(_, _, name)
    if name ~= ADDON then return end
    Events.Unregister(lifecycle, "ADDON_LOADED")
    if ns.DB then ns.DB:Load() end
    callAll("OnInitialize")
end)
Events.Register(lifecycle, "PLAYER_LOGIN", function()
    Events.Unregister(lifecycle, "PLAYER_LOGIN")
    callAll("OnEnable")
end)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implementacja** — `Core/Locale.lua`:

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = setmetatable({}, { __index = function(_, key) return key end })
ns.L = L

local writerDefault = setmetatable({}, {
    __newindex = function(_, key, value)
        rawset(L, key, value == true and key or value)
    end,
})
local writerClient = setmetatable({}, {
    __newindex = function(_, key, value)
        if value ~= true then rawset(L, key, value) end
    end,
})

function ns.NewLocale(locale, isDefault)
    if isDefault then return writerDefault end
    if locale == GetLocale() then return writerClient end
    return nil
end
```

Każdy plik `Locales/xxXX.lua` (poza enUS): zamień dwie linie

```lua
local L = LibStub("AceLocale-3.0"):NewLocale("PetCall", "deDE")
if not L then return end
```

na

```lua
local _, ns = ...
local L = ns.NewLocale("deDE")
if not L then return end
```

(odpowiedni kod języka w każdym pliku; linia `@localization(...)@` zostaje). `Locales/enUS.lua`: `local _, ns = ...` + `local L = ns.NewLocale("enUS", true)` + `if not L then return end`; klucze bez zmian.

TOC — dopisz po `Core\Addon.lua`:

```
Core\Locale.lua
Locales\enUS.lua
Locales\deDE.lua
Locales\frFR.lua
Locales\koKR.lua
Locales\ruRU.lua
Locales\zhCN.lua
```

Atrapa testów nie potrafi skompilować linii `@localization(...)@`, a w grze robi to packager. W `W.tocFiles` dodaj pomijanie: `or (path:match("^Locales/") and path ~= "Locales/enUS.lua")`.

### Task 4: Baza danych zgodna z AceDB

**Files:**
- Create: `Core/DB.lua`, `tests/db_spec.lua`
- Modify: `PetCall.toc` (po `Locales\zhCN.lua` dopisz `Core\DB.lua`), `tests/run.lua`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ns:Fire`.
- Produces:
  - `ns.DB:Load()` (wołane przez Addon przy `ADDON_LOADED`), `ns.db.sv` (= `PetCall3DB`), `ns.db.profile`, `ns.db.global`, `ns.db.char` (= `sv.char[charKey]`).
  - `ns.DB.defaults` — domyślne wartości profilu (poniżej), `ns.DB.globalDefaults`.
  - `ns.DB:Namespace(name, defaults) → ns` gdzie `ns.profile`/`ns.global` śledzą bieżący profil.
  - Profile (`ns.Profiles = ns.DB`): `:List() → {names}`, `:Current() → name`, `:Set(name) → ok, err`, `:Copy(from) → ok, err`, `:Reset()`, `:Delete(name) → ok, err`; po zmianie `ns:Fire("ProfileChanged")`.
  - `ns.DB.ApplyDefaults(tbl, defaults)` — uzupełnia brakujące klucze (rekurencyjnie), obsługuje klucz `"*"` (szablon dla każdego innego klucza w tej tabeli).
  - `ns.DB.charKey` = `UnitName("player") .. " - " .. GetRealmName()`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Test** — `tests/db_spec.lua`:

```lua
local W = dofile("tests/wowstub.lua")

local function fixture()
    return {
        profileKeys = { ["Tester - Realm"] = "Default", ["Alt - Realm"] = "Alt" },
        profiles = {
            Default = {
                waitTimer = 5,
                sets = {
                    ["$Default"] = { pets = { ["BattlePet-0-1"] = 8 } },
                    ["City"] = { name = "City", trigger = { { "specialLocation", loc = "city" } } },
                },
                autoSwitch = { onZone = true },
            },
            Alt = { enable = false },
        },
        namespaces = { SpecialItems = { profiles = { Default = { enable = false } }, global = { localeCache = { enUS = { ["item:1"] = "X" } } } } },
        _petleashMigrated = true,
    }
end

describe("DB", function()
    local ns
    before_each(function()
        W.reset()
        PetCall3DB = fixture()
        ns = W.load()
        W.login()
    end)

    it("selects the character profile and fills defaults", function()
        assert.equal("Default", ns.DB:Current())
        assert.equal(5, ns.db.profile.waitTimer)
        assert.equal(true, ns.db.profile.enable)
        assert.equal(true, ns.db.profile.autoSwitch.onZone)
        assert.equal(false, ns.db.profile.autoSwitch.timer)
        assert.equal(30 * 60, ns.db.profile.autoSwitch.timerValue)
    end)

    it("wildcard defaults: sets stripped by AceDB behave like 1.0.x", function()
        local city = ns.db.profile.sets.City
        assert.equal(true, city.enabled)
        assert.equal(1, city.priority)
        assert.equal(0, city.defaultValue)
        assert.same({}, city.pets)
        local def = ns.db.profile.sets["$Default"]
        assert.equal(1, def.defaultValue)
        assert.equal(-1, def.priority)
        assert.equal(8, def.pets["BattlePet-0-1"])
    end)

    it("keeps unknown top-level keys", function()
        assert.equal(true, PetCall3DB._petleashMigrated)
    end)

    it("namespaces follow the profile", function()
        local si = ns.DB:Namespace("SpecialItems", { profile = { enable = true, items = { ["*"] = true } } })
        assert.equal(false, si.profile.enable)
        assert.equal(true, si.profile.items["item:5"])
        assert.equal("X", si.global.localeCache.enUS["item:1"])
        ns.DB:Set("Fresh")
        assert.equal(true, si.profile.enable)
    end)

    it("profile operations", function()
        local fired = 0
        ns:RegisterCallback({}, "ProfileChanged", function() fired = fired + 1 end)
        assert.same({ "Alt", "Default" }, ns.DB:List())
        assert.is_true((ns.DB:Set("Alt")))
        assert.equal(false, ns.db.profile.enable)
        assert.equal("Alt", PetCall3DB.profileKeys["Tester - Realm"])
        assert.is_true((ns.DB:Copy("Default")))
        assert.equal(5, ns.db.profile.waitTimer)
        ns.DB:Reset()
        assert.equal(3, ns.db.profile.waitTimer)
        local ok = ns.DB:Delete("Alt")
        assert.is_false(ok)                       -- cannot delete the current profile
        ns.DB:Set("Default")
        assert.is_true((ns.DB:Delete("Alt")))
        assert.is_nil(PetCall3DB.profiles.Alt)
        assert.equal(5, fired)
    end)

    it("creates a fresh database", function()
        W.reset()
        local ns2 = W.load()
        W.login()
        assert.equal("Default", ns2.DB:Current())
        assert.equal(true, ns2.db.profile.sets["$Default"].enabled)
        assert.is_not_nil(PetCall3DB.profiles.Default)
    end)
end)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implementacja** — `Core/DB.lua`:

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local pairs, type, next, sort = pairs, type, next, table.sort

local DB = {}
ns.DB, ns.Profiles = DB, DB
ns.db = {}

DB.defaults = {
    enable = true,
    enableInBattleground = true,
    dismissInBattleground = false,
    disableOutsideCities = false,
    dismissWhenStealthed = false,
    disableForQuestItems = true,          -- unused since 1.x; kept so old data round-trips
    enableInPVE = true,
    dismissInPVE = false,
    nearPEWOnly = false,
    overrideSetLoadout = false,
    waitTimer = 3,
    verbose = true,
    autoSwitch = { timer = false, timerValue = 30 * 60, citiesOnly = false, onZone = false },
    minimap = { hide = true },
    sets = {
        ["$Default"] = { name = DEFAULT, enabled = true, defaultValue = 1, priority = -1, pets = {}, filter = {} },
        ["*"] = { enabled = true, defaultValue = 0, priority = 1, pets = {}, filter = {}, trigger = {} },
    },
}

DB.globalDefaults = {
    window = { scale = 1, width = 820, height = 560, setsCollapsed = nil },
    recentPets = {},
}

local function copy(v)
    if type(v) ~= "table" then return v end
    local c = {}
    for k, x in pairs(v) do c[k] = copy(x) end
    return c
end

function DB.ApplyDefaults(tbl, defaults)
    local wildcard = defaults["*"]
    for k, v in pairs(defaults) do
        if k ~= "*" then
            if type(v) == "table" then
                if type(tbl[k]) ~= "table" then tbl[k] = {} end
                DB.ApplyDefaults(tbl[k], v)
            elseif tbl[k] == nil then
                tbl[k] = v
            end
        end
    end
    if wildcard then
        for k, v in pairs(tbl) do
            if defaults[k] == nil and type(v) == "table" and type(wildcard) == "table" then
                DB.ApplyDefaults(v, wildcard)
            end
        end
        -- new keys created later get the wildcard too
        setmetatable(tbl, {
            __index = function(t, k)
                if type(k) ~= "string" then return nil end
                local new = copy(wildcard)
                rawset(t, k, new)
                return new
            end,
        })
    end
end

local namespaces = {}

local function profileName(sv)
    sv.profileKeys = sv.profileKeys or {}
    local name = sv.profileKeys[DB.charKey] or "Default"
    sv.profileKeys[DB.charKey] = name
    return name
end

local function bindProfile()
    local sv = ns.db.sv
    local name = profileName(sv)
    sv.profiles[name] = sv.profiles[name] or {}
    DB.ApplyDefaults(sv.profiles[name], DB.defaults)
    ns.db.profile = sv.profiles[name]
    for nsName, n in pairs(namespaces) do
        local nsv = sv.namespaces[nsName]
        nsv.profiles = nsv.profiles or {}
        nsv.profiles[name] = nsv.profiles[name] or {}
        if n.defaults.profile then DB.ApplyDefaults(nsv.profiles[name], n.defaults.profile) end
        n.profile = nsv.profiles[name]
    end
end

function DB:Load()
    DB.charKey = UnitName("player") .. " - " .. GetRealmName()
    PetCall3DB = PetCall3DB or {}
    local sv = PetCall3DB
    sv.profiles = sv.profiles or {}
    sv.namespaces = sv.namespaces or {}
    sv.global = sv.global or {}
    sv.char = sv.char or {}
    sv.char[DB.charKey] = sv.char[DB.charKey] or {}
    DB.ApplyDefaults(sv.global, DB.globalDefaults)
    ns.db.sv, ns.db.global, ns.db.char = sv, sv.global, sv.char[DB.charKey]
    bindProfile()
end

function DB:Namespace(name, defaults)
    local sv = ns.db.sv
    sv.namespaces[name] = sv.namespaces[name] or {}
    local nsv = sv.namespaces[name]
    nsv.global = nsv.global or {}
    if defaults.global then DB.ApplyDefaults(nsv.global, defaults.global) end
    local n = { defaults = defaults, global = nsv.global }
    namespaces[name] = n
    bindProfile()
    return n
end

function DB:Current()
    return profileName(ns.db.sv)
end

function DB:List()
    local out, seen = {}, {}
    for name in pairs(ns.db.sv.profiles) do
        out[#out + 1], seen[name] = name, true
    end
    local cur = self:Current()
    if not seen[cur] then out[#out + 1] = cur end
    sort(out)
    return out
end

local function changed()
    bindProfile()
    ns:Fire("ProfileChanged")
end

function DB:Set(name)
    if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" then return false, ns.L["Profile name cannot be empty."] end
    ns.db.sv.profileKeys[DB.charKey] = name
    changed()
    return true
end

function DB:Copy(from)
    local src = ns.db.sv.profiles[from]
    if not src or from == self:Current() then return false, ns.L["Unknown profile."] end
    local cur = self:Current()
    ns.db.sv.profiles[cur] = copy(src)
    for _, nsv in pairs(ns.db.sv.namespaces) do
        if nsv.profiles and nsv.profiles[from] then
            nsv.profiles[cur] = copy(nsv.profiles[from])
        end
    end
    changed()
    return true
end

function DB:Reset()
    local cur = self:Current()
    ns.db.sv.profiles[cur] = {}
    for _, nsv in pairs(ns.db.sv.namespaces) do
        if nsv.profiles then nsv.profiles[cur] = {} end
    end
    changed()
end

function DB:Delete(name)
    if name == self:Current() then return false, ns.L["Cannot delete the active profile."] end
    if not ns.db.sv.profiles[name] then return false, ns.L["Unknown profile."] end
    ns.db.sv.profiles[name] = nil
    for _, nsv in pairs(ns.db.sv.namespaces) do
        if nsv.profiles then nsv.profiles[name] = nil end
    end
    for key, prof in pairs(ns.db.sv.profileKeys) do
        if prof == name then ns.db.sv.profileKeys[key] = nil end
    end
    ns:Fire("ProfileChanged")
    return true
end
```

> `DB.ApplyDefaults` na `sets` wypełnia brakujące pola istniejących zestawów szablonem `"*"` — dokładnie tak, jak AceDB czytał zestawy, których domyślne pola zostały wycięte przy wylogowaniu. Metatabela tworzy nowy zestaw z szablonu przy pierwszym odczycie nieznanej nazwy — `Sets` (Task 8) używa `rawget`, żeby sprawdzać istnienie.

Dodaj klucze do `Locales/enUS.lua`:

```lua
L["Profile name cannot be empty."] = true
L["Unknown profile."] = true
L["Cannot delete the active profile."] = true
```

```lua
-- Realistic 1.0.x saved variables + pet collection + world states.
local pets = {
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Generator referencji** — `tools/reference_1_0.lua` ładuje **stary** kod 1.0.15 (z gałęzi `main` przed Task 23, pliki w katalogu głównym) z bibliotekami Ace3 i atrapą `tests/wowstub.lua`, i dla każdego świata zapisuje wynik:

```lua
-- Usage (from repo root, on main or any commit that still has 1.0.x files):
--   git worktree add ../petcall-ref v1.0.15
--   python -I tools/lua.py tools/reference_1_0.lua ../petcall-ref > tests/fixtures/reference_1_0.lua
local refRoot = arg[1] or error("path to a 1.0.x checkout required")
local W = dofile("tests/wowstub.lua")
local F = dofile("tests/fixtures/PetCall3DB_1_0.lua")

local function deepcopy(t)
    if type(t) ~= "table" then return t end
    local c = {} for k, v in pairs(t) do c[k] = deepcopy(v) end return c
end

local function load1x()
    W.reset()
    for id, p in pairs(F.pets) do W.world.pets[id] = p end
    for id in pairs(F.favorites) do W.world.favorites[id] = true end
    PetCall3DB = deepcopy(F.db)
    LE_PET_JOURNAL_FILTER_COLLECTED, LE_PET_JOURNAL_FILTER_NOT_COLLECTED = 1, 2
    C_PetJournal.GetNumPets = function() local n = 0 for _ in pairs(W.world.pets) do n = n + 1 end return n, n end
    C_PetJournal.GetPetInfoByIndex = function(i)
        local ids = C_PetJournal.GetOwnedPetIDs()
        local id = ids[i]
        local p = W.world.pets[id]
        return id, p.species, true, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, p.creature or (1000 + p.species)
    end
    for _, k in ipairs({ "IsFilterChecked", "IsPetTypeChecked", "IsPetSourceChecked" }) do C_PetJournal[k] = function() return true end end
    for _, k in ipairs({ "SetFilterChecked", "SetAllPetTypesChecked", "SetAllPetSourcesChecked", "SetSearchFilter", "ClearSearchFilter", "SetPetTypeFilter", "SetPetSourceChecked" }) do C_PetJournal[k] = function() end end
    C_PetJournal.GetNumPetSources = function() return 10 end
    GetSpecialization = C_SpecializationInfo.GetSpecialization
    GetSpecializationInfo = C_SpecializationInfo.GetSpecializationInfo
    local libs = { "LibStub/LibStub.lua", "CallbackHandler-1.0/CallbackHandler-1.0.lua", "AceAddon-3.0/AceAddon-3.0.lua",
        "AceEvent-3.0/AceEvent-3.0.lua", "AceTimer-3.0/AceTimer-3.0.lua", "AceDB-3.0/AceDB-3.0.lua",
        "AceLocale-3.0/AceLocale-3.0.lua", "AceConsole-3.0/AceConsole-3.0.lua", "AceSerializer-3.0/AceSerializer-3.0.lua",
        "LibPetJournal-2.0/LibPetJournal-2.0.lua" }
    for _, l in ipairs(libs) do assert(loadfile(refRoot .. "/Libs/" .. l))() end
    for _, n in ipairs({ "AceConfigRegistry-3.0", "AceConfig-3.0", "AceConfigDialog-3.0", "AceGUI-3.0", "AceDBOptions-3.0", "LibDBIcon-1.0" }) do
        setmetatable(LibStub:NewLibrary(n, 1), { __index = function() return function() return setmetatable({}, { __index = function() return function() end end }) end end })
    end
    local addon = {}
    assert(loadfile(refRoot .. "/Locales/enUS.lua"))("PetCall", addon)
    for _, f in ipairs({ "PetCall.lua", "Triggers.lua", "Filters.lua", "Ready.lua", "SwitchTimer.lua" }) do
        assert(loadfile(refRoot .. "/" .. f))("PetCall", addon)
    end
    W.fire("ADDON_LOADED", "PetCall")
    W.fire("PLAYER_LOGIN")
    W.fire("PET_JOURNAL_LIST_UPDATE")
    W.advance(1)
    return addon
end

local out = { "-- Generated by tools/reference_1_0.lua from 1.0.x. Do not edit.", "return {" }
for wi, state in ipairs(F.worlds) do
    local addon = load1x()
    for k, v in pairs(state) do W.world[k] = v end
    for _, e in ipairs({ "ZONE_CHANGED_NEW_AREA", "GROUP_ROSTER_UPDATE", "PLAYER_SPECIALIZATION_CHANGED", "PLAYER_FLAGS_CHANGED" }) do W.fire(e, "player") end
    W.advance(1)
    local cur = addon:GetCurrentSet()
    out[#out + 1] = string.format("  [%d] = { current = %q, sets = {", wi, cur and cur.name or "")
    for name, set in addon:IterateSets() do
        local active = set:IsActive()
        -- build weights directly so inactive sets are captured too
        local tbl = addon:BuildSetWeights(set.settings, {})
        local weights, total = {}, tbl.total or 0
        for i = 1, #tbl, 2 do
            local id = tbl[i + 1]
            weights[id] = (weights[id] or 0) + tbl[i]
        end
        local parts = {}
        for id, w in pairs(weights) do parts[#parts + 1] = string.format("[%q]=%.10g", id, w) end
        table.sort(parts)
        out[#out + 1] = string.format("    [%q] = { active = %s, value = %s, total = %.10g, weights = { %s } },",
            name, tostring(active), tostring(set.value), total, table.concat(parts, ", "))
    end
    out[#out + 1] = "  } },"
end
out[#out + 1] = "}"
print(table.concat(out, "\n"))
```

### Task 6: PetCache

**Files:**
- Create: `Core/PetCache.lua`, `tests/petcache_spec.lua`
- Modify: `PetCall.toc` (po `Core\DB.lua`: `Core\PetCache.lua`), `tests/run.lua`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ns:NewModule`, `ns:Fire`, `ns.Debounce`.
- Produces (`ns.PetCache`):
  - `:Rescan()`; `:QueueRescan()` (debounce 0,5 s)
  - `:IteratePetIDs() → ipairs` po GUID-ach posiadanych petów bez „fanfare”, posortowanych po kluczu sortowania
  - `:NumPets() → number`, `:IsLoaded() → bool`
  - `:GetInfo(petID) → info|nil`, `info = { petID, speciesID, customName, level, name, icon, petType, creatureID, isFavorite, quality, sortKey }` (`quality` 1–6 z `GetPetStats`, `nil` → 2)
  - `:GetSpeciesID(petID)`, `:GetCreatureID(petID)`, `:GetSortKey(petID)` (`""` gdy brak)
  - `:Contains(petID) → bool`
  - Callback `ns:Fire("PetsChanged", listChanged)` — `listChanged = true`, gdy zmienił się zbiór posiadanych petów.

- [ ] **Step 1: Test** — `tests/petcache_spec.lua`:

```lua
local W = dofile("tests/wowstub.lua")
describe("PetCache", function()
    local ns
    before_each(function()
        W.reset()
        W.world.pets = {
            a = { species = 39, name = "Mechanical Squirrel", level = 5 },
            b = { species = 40, name = "Bombay Cat", custom = "Mruczek", level = 25, quality = 4 },
            f = { species = 41, name = "Box", fanfare = true },
        }
```

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local C_PetJournal, ipairs, format, strlower, sort, wipe = C_PetJournal, ipairs, format, strlower, table.sort, wipe

local PetCache = ns:NewModule("PetCache")
ns.PetCache = PetCache

local ids, infos, loaded = {}, {}, false

local function readInfo(petID)
    local speciesID, customName, level, _, _, _, isFavorite, name, icon, petType, creatureID =
        C_PetJournal.GetPetInfoByPetID(petID)
    if not speciesID then return nil end
    local _, _, _, _, quality = C_PetJournal.GetPetStats(petID)
    return {
        petID = petID, speciesID = speciesID, customName = customName, level = level or 1,
        name = name or "?", icon = icon, petType = petType, creatureID = creatureID,
        isFavorite = isFavorite and true or false, quality = quality or 2,
        sortKey = format("%s\0%s\0%03d", strlower(name or ""), strlower(customName or ""), level or 0),
    }
end

function PetCache:Rescan()
    local owned = C_PetJournal.GetOwnedPetIDs()
    if not owned then return end
    local old = {}
    for _, id in ipairs(ids) do old[id] = true end
    wipe(ids)
    wipe(infos)
    local listChanged = false
    for _, id in ipairs(owned) do
        if not C_PetJournal.PetNeedsFanfare(id) then
            local info = readInfo(id)
            if info then
                ids[#ids + 1], infos[id] = id, info
                if not old[id] then listChanged = true end
                old[id] = nil
            end
        end
    end
    if next(old) then listChanged = true end
    sort(ids, function(a, b)
        local ka, kb = infos[a].sortKey, infos[b].sortKey
        if ka == kb then return a < b end
        return ka < kb
    end)
    loaded = true
    ns:Fire("PetsChanged", listChanged)
end

PetCache.QueueRescan = ns.Debounce(0.5, function() PetCache:Rescan() end)

function PetCache:IteratePetIDs() return ipairs(ids) end
function PetCache:NumPets() return #ids end
function PetCache:IsLoaded() return loaded end
function PetCache:Contains(petID) return infos[petID] ~= nil end
function PetCache:GetInfo(petID) return infos[petID] end
function PetCache:GetSpeciesID(petID) local i = infos[petID] return i and i.speciesID end
function PetCache:GetCreatureID(petID) local i = infos[petID] return i and i.creatureID end
function PetCache:GetSortKey(petID) local i = infos[petID] return i and i.sortKey or "" end

function PetCache:OnEnable()
    self:RegisterEvent("PET_JOURNAL_LIST_UPDATE", "QueueRescan")
    self:RegisterEvent("NEW_PET_ADDED", "QueueRescan")
    self:RegisterEvent("PET_JOURNAL_PET_DELETED", "QueueRescan")
    self:Rescan()
end
```

> `QueueRescan` dostaje `(self, event)` z dispatchera — debounce ignoruje argumenty. `next` jest globalem Lua.

### Task 7: Wagi i reguły automatyczne

**Files:**
- Create: `Core/Weights.lua`, `Core/AutoRules.lua`, `tests/weights_spec.lua`, `tests/autorules_spec.lua`
- Modify: `PetCall.toc` (po `Core\PetCache.lua`: `Core\Weights.lua`, `Core\AutoRules.lua`), `tests/run.lua`, `Locales/enUS.lua`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ns.PetCache`.
- Produces:
  - `ns.Weights.LEVELS = { {value=0,key="NEVER"}, {value=1/64,key="EXTRA_LOW"}, {value=1/8,key="LOW"}, {value=1,key="NORMAL"}, {value=8,key="HIGH"}, {value=64,key="EXTRA_HIGH"} }`
  - `ns.Weights.NEVER = 0`, `ns.Weights.NORMAL = 1`
  - `ns.Weights.Label(value) → string` (np. „Extra High Priority”), `ns.Weights.Short(value) → string|nil` (`"+2"`, `"+1"`, `"-1"`, `"-2"`, `"Never"`, `nil` dla Normal), `ns.Weights.Nearest(value) → value` (dla wartości spoza listy)
  - `ns.Weights.IGNORE_SPECIES = { [114]=true, [280]=true, [281]=true, [282]=true, [283]=true }`
  - `ns.Weights.PetWeight(settings, petID) → number` — wpis ręczny (`rawget(settings.pets, petID)`), w przeciwnym razie 0 dla gatunków z `IGNORE_SPECIES`, w przeciwnym razie `settings.defaultValue`
  - `ns.Weights.Build(settings, out?) → out` — `out` = tablica naprzemienna `w, petID, w, petID…` + `out.total`, kolejność petów jak `PetCache:IteratePetIDs()`; pety niespełniające `C_PetJournal.PetIsSummonable` pomijane; dla każdego wpisu `settings.filter` pasującego do peta dopisywana para `(weight, petID)` (semantyka 1.0.x)
  - `ns.Weights.Pick(tbl, r) → petID|nil` (`r` ∈ [0,1)), `ns.Weights.Chance(tbl, petID) → 0..1`, `ns.Weights.Sum(tbl, petID) → number`
  - `ns.AutoRules.Types() → { {key="favorites", fields={}}, {key="petType", fields={ {key="petTypes", kind="multiChoice", values=fn} }}, {key="level", fields={ {key="operator", kind="operator"}, {key="level", kind="number", min=1, max=25} }} }`, `ns.AutoRules.Label(key)`
  - `ns.AutoRules.Match(entry, petID) → bool` — rekurencyjnie na formacie zapisu (`AND`, `OR`, liście), `entry.negate` odwraca wynik
  - `ns.AutoRules.Weight(entry) → number` (`entry[2] or 1`)
  - `ns.AutoRules.ToChips(filterList) → { {index=i, entry=e, legacy=bool, match="all"|"any", criteria={leafEntries}} }`
  - `ns.AutoRules.Describe(entry) → string`
  - Edycja (mutują `filterList`, zwracają `true`): `AddChip(list, typeKey, weight)`, `RemoveChip(list, index)`, `SetChipWeight(list, index, w)`, `AddCriterion(list, index, typeKey)`, `RemoveCriterion(list, index, criterionIndex)`, `SetMatch(list, index, "all"|"any")`, `SetField(leafEntry, key, value)`, `Simplify(list, index) → bool`

Format zapisu (zgodny z 1.0.x, rozszerzony addytywnie): liść `{ "favorites", w }`, `{ "petType", petType = "8", w }` lub nowy `{ "petType", petTypes = {8, 10}, w }`, `{ "level", operator = ">=", level = "25", w }`; węzły `{ "AND"|"OR", items = { liście bez wagi }, w }`. Chip = wpis najwyższego poziomu. Chip z jednym kryterium = liść; chip z ≥ 2 kryteriami = `AND`/`OR` z liśćmi w `items`. Wpis, który nie pasuje do tego kształtu (zagnieżdżone węzły), jest chipem `legacy = true`.

```lua
describe("AutoRules", function()

        W.reset()

        }

        ns = W.load()

        AR = ns.AutoRules
    end)

        assert.is_false(AR.Match({ "favorites", 8 }, "a"))
        assert.is_true(AR.Match({ "petType", petType = "8" }, "a"))
        assert.is_true(AR.Match({ "level", operator = ">=", level = "25" }, "a"))
        assert.is_false(AR.Match({ "level", operator = ">=", level = "25" }, "b"))
    end)
    it("matches new multi petTypes and nodes", function()
        assert.is_true(AR.Match({ "petType", petTypes = { 9, 10 } }, "b"))
        assert.is_true(AR.Match({ "OR", items = { { "favorites" }, { "petType", petType = "8" } } }, "a"))
        assert.is_false(AR.Match({ "AND", items = { { "favorites" }, { "petType", petType = "8" } } }, "a"))
    end)
    it("builds chips and marks deep trees legacy", function()
        local list = {
            { "favorites", 64 },
            { "OR", items = { { "petType", petType = "8" }, { "favorites" } }, 8 },
            { "AND", items = { { "OR", items = { { "favorites" } } } }, 1 },
        }
        local chips = AR.ToChips(list)
        assert.equal(3, #chips)
        assert.same({ false, "all", 1 }, { chips[1].legacy, chips[1].match, #chips[1].criteria })
        assert.same({ false, "any", 2 }, { chips[2].legacy, chips[2].match, #chips[2].criteria })
        assert.is_true(chips[3].legacy)
    end)
    it("edits chips keeping the 1.0 storage shape", function()
        local list = {}
        AR.AddChip(list, "favorites", 8)
        assert.same({ { "favorites", 8 } }, list)
        AR.AddCriterion(list, 1, "level")
        assert.equal("AND", list[1][1])
        assert.equal(8, list[1][2])
        assert.equal(2, #list[1].items)
        AR.SetMatch(list, 1, "any")
        assert.equal("OR", list[1][1])
        AR.RemoveCriterion(list, 1, 2)
        assert.same({ { "favorites", 8 } }, list)
        AR.RemoveChip(list, 1)
        assert.same({}, list)
    end)
    it("simplifies a nested single-child node", function()
        local list = { { "AND", items = { { "OR", items = { { "favorites" } } } }, 1 } }
        assert.is_true(AR.Simplify(list, 1))
        assert.same({ { "favorites", 1 } }, list)
    end)
end)
```

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
local C_PetJournal, rawget, abs = C_PetJournal, rawget, math.abs

local Weights = {}
ns.Weights = Weights

local W8 = 8
Weights.LEVELS = {
    { value = 0,       key = "NEVER",      label = L["Never"],               short = L["Never"] },
    { value = W8 ^ -2, key = "EXTRA_LOW",  label = L["Extra Low Priority"],  short = "-2" },
    { value = W8 ^ -1, key = "LOW",        label = L["Low Priority"],        short = "-1" },
    { value = 1,       key = "NORMAL",     label = L["Normal"],              short = nil },
    { value = W8,      key = "HIGH",       label = L["High Priority"],       short = "+1" },
    { value = W8 ^ 2,  key = "EXTRA_HIGH", label = L["Extra High Priority"], short = "+2" },
}
Weights.NEVER, Weights.NORMAL = 0, 1
Weights.IGNORE_SPECIES = { [114] = true, [280] = true, [281] = true, [282] = true, [283] = true }

function Weights.Nearest(value)
    local best, dist = 1, math.huge
    for _, lvl in ipairs(Weights.LEVELS) do
        local d = abs(lvl.value - (value or 0))
        if d < dist then best, dist = lvl.value, d end
    end
    return best
end

local function level(value)
    local v = Weights.Nearest(value)
    for _, lvl in ipairs(Weights.LEVELS) do
        if lvl.value == v then return lvl end
    end
end

function Weights.Label(value) return level(value).label end
function Weights.Short(value) return level(value).short end

function Weights.PetWeight(settings, petID)
    local v = rawget(settings.pets, petID)
    if v ~= nil then return v end
    local species = ns.PetCache:GetSpeciesID(petID)
    if species and Weights.IGNORE_SPECIES[species] then return 0 end
    return settings.defaultValue or 0
end

function Weights.Build(settings, out)
    out = out or {}
    wipe(out)
    out.total = 0
    local filters = settings.filter
    local hasFilters = filters and #filters > 0
    for _, petID in ns.PetCache:IteratePetIDs() do
        local value = Weights.PetWeight(settings, petID)
        if value > 0 and C_PetJournal.PetIsSummonable(petID) then
            out[#out + 1], out[#out + 2] = value, petID
            out.total = out.total + value
        end
        if hasFilters then
            for _, entry in ipairs(filters) do
                if ns.AutoRules.Match(entry, petID) then
                    local w = ns.AutoRules.Weight(entry)
                    out[#out + 1], out[#out + 2] = w, petID
                    out.total = out.total + w
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return out
end

function Weights.Pick(tbl, r)
    if not tbl.total or tbl.total <= 0 then return nil end
    local remaining = r * tbl.total
    for i = 1, #tbl, 2 do
        remaining = remaining - tbl[i]
        if remaining <= 0 then return tbl[i + 1] end
    end
    return tbl[#tbl]
end

function Weights.Sum(tbl, petID)
    local s = 0
    for i = 1, #tbl, 2 do
        if tbl[i + 1] == petID then s = s + tbl[i] end
    end
    return s
end

function Weights.Chance(tbl, petID)
    if not tbl.total or tbl.total <= 0 then return 0 end
    return Weights.Sum(tbl, petID) / tbl.total
end
```

> Zachowane z 1.0.x: filtry dodają wagę także petom, które nie są „summonable” (1.0.x tego nie sprawdzało dla filtrów). Test referencyjny to pilnuje — nie „poprawiaj” tego.

`Core/AutoRules.lua`:

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
local tonumber, tinsert, tremove, ipairs, type = tonumber, tinsert, tremove, ipairs, type

local AutoRules = {}
ns.AutoRules = AutoRules

local ops = {
    ["=="] = function(a, b) return a == b end,
    [">="] = function(a, b) return a >= b end,
    [">"] = function(a, b) return a > b end,
    ["<="] = function(a, b) return a <= b end,
    ["<"] = function(a, b) return a < b end,
}
AutoRules.OPERATORS = { "==", ">=", ">", "<=", "<" }

local function petTypeValues()
    local out = {}
    for i = 1, C_PetJournal.GetNumPetTypes() do
        out[#out + 1] = { value = i, label = _G["BATTLE_PET_NAME_" .. i] or tostring(i) }
    end
    return out
end

local types = {
    favorites = {
        key = "favorites", label = L["Favorites"], fields = {},
        match = function(e, info) return info.isFavorite end,
        describe = function() return L["Favorites"] end,
        new = function() return { "favorites" } end,
    },
    petType = {
        key = "petType", label = L["Pet Type"],
        fields = { { key = "petTypes", kind = "multiChoice", values = petTypeValues } },
        match = function(e, info)
            if e.petTypes then
                for _, t in ipairs(e.petTypes) do if t == info.petType then return true end end
                return false
            end
            return info.petType == tonumber(e.petType)
        end,
        describe = function(e)
            local names = {}
            local list = e.petTypes or { tonumber(e.petType) }
            for _, t in ipairs(list) do names[#names + 1] = _G["BATTLE_PET_NAME_" .. t] or tostring(t) end
            return L["Type"] .. ": " .. table.concat(names, ", ")
        end,
        new = function() return { "petType", petTypes = { 1 } } end,
    },
    level = {
        key = "level", label = L["Level"],
        fields = { { key = "operator", kind = "operator" }, { key = "level", kind = "number", min = 1, max = 25 } },
        match = function(e, info)
            local cmp, n = ops[e.operator or "?"], tonumber(e.level)
            return cmp ~= nil and n ~= nil and cmp(info.level, n)
        end,
        describe = function(e) return L["Level"] .. " " .. (e.operator or "?") .. " " .. tostring(e.level) end,
        new = function() return { "level", operator = ">=", level = "25" } end,
    },
}
local order = { "favorites", "petType", "level" }

function AutoRules.Types()
    local out = {}
    for _, k in ipairs(order) do out[#out + 1] = types[k] end
    return out
end

function AutoRules.Label(key) return types[key] and types[key].label or key end

local function matchEntry(e, info)
    local kind = e[1]
    local result
    if kind == "AND" or kind == "OR" then
        result = kind == "AND"
        for _, sub in ipairs(e.items or {}) do
            local m = matchEntry(sub, info)
            if kind == "AND" and not m then result = false break end
            if kind == "OR" and m then result = true break end
        end
    elseif types[kind] then
        result = types[kind].match(e, info) and true or false
    else
        result = false
    end
    if e.negate then result = not result end
    return result
end

function AutoRules.Match(entry, petID)
    local info = ns.PetCache:GetInfo(petID)
    if not info then return false end
    return matchEntry(entry, info)
end

function AutoRules.Weight(entry) return entry[2] or 1 end

local function isLeaf(e) return types[e[1]] ~= nil end

function AutoRules.ToChips(list)
    local chips = {}
    for i, e in ipairs(list or {}) do
        local chip = { index = i, entry = e, legacy = false, match = "all", criteria = {} }
        if isLeaf(e) then
            chip.criteria[1] = e
        elseif e[1] == "AND" or e[1] == "OR" then
            chip.match = e[1] == "AND" and "all" or "any"
            for _, sub in ipairs(e.items or {}) do
                if isLeaf(sub) then chip.criteria[#chip.criteria + 1] = sub else chip.legacy = true end
            end
            if #chip.criteria == 0 then chip.legacy = true end
        else
            chip.legacy = true
        end
        chips[#chips + 1] = chip
    end
    return chips
end

function AutoRules.Describe(e)
    local kind = e[1]
    local text
    if kind == "AND" or kind == "OR" then
        local parts = {}
        for _, sub in ipairs(e.items or {}) do parts[#parts + 1] = AutoRules.Describe(sub) end
        text = "(" .. table.concat(parts, kind == "AND" and (" " .. L["and"] .. " ") or (" " .. L["or"] .. " ")) .. ")"
    elseif types[kind] then
        text = types[kind].describe(e)
    else
        text = L["Unknown rule"]
    end
    if e.negate then text = L["not"] .. " " .. text end
    return text
end

-- Editing helpers. Chips keep the 1.0 shape: a leaf, or AND/OR of leaves.

function AutoRules.AddChip(list, typeKey, weight)
    local e = types[typeKey].new()
    e[2] = weight or 1
    tinsert(list, e)
    return true
end

function AutoRules.RemoveChip(list, index)
    tremove(list, index)
    return true
end

function AutoRules.SetChipWeight(list, index, w)
    list[index][2] = w
    return true
end

function AutoRules.AddCriterion(list, index, typeKey)
    local e = list[index]
    local new = types[typeKey].new()
    if isLeaf(e) then
        local w = e[2]
        e[2] = nil
        list[index] = { "AND", items = { e, new }, w }
    else
        tinsert(e.items, new)
    end
    return true
end

function AutoRules.RemoveCriterion(list, index, ci)
    local e = list[index]
    if isLeaf(e) then
        tremove(list, index)
        return true
    end
    tremove(e.items, ci)
    if #e.items == 1 then
        local only = e.items[1]
        only[2] = e[2]
        list[index] = only
    elseif #e.items == 0 then
        tremove(list, index)
    end
    return true
end

function AutoRules.SetMatch(list, index, match)
    local e = list[index]
    if not isLeaf(e) then e[1] = match == "any" and "OR" or "AND" end
    return true
end

function AutoRules.SetField(leaf, key, value)
    leaf[key] = value
    if key == "petTypes" then leaf.petType = nil end
    return true
end

-- Collapses single-child AND/OR chains; only when the meaning stays identical.
local function collapse(e)
    if (e[1] == "AND" or e[1] == "OR") and not e.negate and e.items and #e.items == 1 then
        return collapse(e.items[1])
    end
    return e
end

function AutoRules.Simplify(list, index)
    local e = list[index]
    local w = e[2]
    local c = collapse(e)
    if c == e then return false end
    c = { unpack(c) }
    for k, v in pairs(collapse(e)) do if type(k) ~= "number" then c[k] = v end end
    c[2] = w
    list[index] = c
    return true
end
```

> `collapse` zwraca ten sam obiekt, jeśli nic nie upraszcza; kopia w `Simplify` przenosi pola nazwane (`petType`, `operator` …) i ustawia wagę chipa.

Klucze do `Locales/enUS.lua`: `Favorites`, `Pet Type`, `Type`, `Level`, `and`, `or`, `not`, `Unknown rule` (wartość `true`).

### Task 8: Zestawy (bez warunków)

**Files:**
- Create: `Core/Sets.lua`, `tests/sets_spec.lua`
- Modify: `PetCall.toc` (po `Core\AutoRules.lua`: `Core\Conditions.lua`, `Core\ConditionTypes.lua`, `Core\Sets.lua` — dwa pierwsze powstają w Task 13; do tego czasu dodaj tylko `Core\Sets.lua`), `tests/run.lua`, `Locales/enUS.lua`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ns.db.profile.sets`, `ns.Weights`, `ns:Fire`, `ns.PetCache`.
- Produces (`ns.Sets`, obiekty `set`):
  - `Sets.DEFAULT = "$Default"`; `Sets:List() → {set}` (Default pierwszy, potem priorytet malejąco, nazwa rosnąco), `Sets:Get(name) → set|nil`, `Sets:Create(name) → set, ok, err`, `Sets:ValidateName(name, except?) → ok, err`, `Sets:Reload()` (po zmianie profilu), `Sets:GetCurrent() → set|nil`, `Sets:Update()` (przelicza aktywny zestaw)
  - `set.name`, `set.settings`, `set:IsDefault()`, `set:DisplayName()` (`L["Default"]` dla `$Default`)
  - `set:Rename(name) → ok, err`, `set:Delete() → ok, err`, `set:SetEnabled(b)`, `set:SetPriority(w)`, `set:SetImmediate(b)`, `set:SetDefaultWeight(w)`
  - `set:GetPetWeight(petID)`, `set:SetPetWeight(petID, w)`, `set:SetPetWeights(petIDs, w)`, `set:ClearPetWeights()`, `set:HasManualWeight(petID) → bool`, `set:GetAutoWeight(petID) → number` (suma wag z reguł automatycznych)
  - `set:GetWeightTable() → tbl` (leniwie), `set:GetChance(petID) → 0..1, reason?` (`reason` = `L["On cooldown"]` / `L["Cannot be summoned"]` / nil), `set:PickPet() → petID|nil`, `set:Contains(petID)`
  - `set:GetAutoRules() → chips` (= `ns.AutoRules.ToChips(settings.filter)`), `set:FiltersChanged()` (po edycji reguł)
  - `set:ConditionsMet() → bool` (w Task 8: zawsze `true`; Task 14 podpina watcher), `set:IsActive() → bool` (włączony, warunki spełnione, `total > 0`), `set:GetStatus() → "active"|"waiting"|"empty"|"noConditions"|"disabled"`
  - Callbacki: `ns:Fire("SetsChanged", name|nil)`, `ns:Fire("CurrentSetChanged", set|nil)`

Status: `disabled` gdy `not enabled`; `empty` gdy `total == 0`; `noConditions` gdy zestaw nie-domyślny bez warunków (aktywny zawsze — informacyjnie); `active` gdy jest bieżącym zestawem; `waiting` w pozostałych przypadkach.

```lua
local W = dofile("tests/wowstub.lua")
local F = dofile("tests/fixtures/PetCall3DB_1_0.lua")

local function deepcopy(t)
    if type(t) ~= "table" then return t end
    local c = {} for k, v in pairs(t) do c[k] = deepcopy(v) end return c
end

describe("Sets", function()
    local ns
    before_each(function()
        W.reset()
        for id, p in pairs(F.pets) do W.world.pets[id] = p end
        PetCall3DB = deepcopy(F.db)
        ns = W.load()
        W.login()
        W.advance(1)
    end)

    it("lists Default first, then by priority and name", function()
        local names = {}
        for _, s in ipairs(ns.Sets:List()) do names[#names + 1] = s.name end
        assert.same({ "$Default", "RaidOrZone", "City", "Deep", "Disabled", "NoTrigger" }, names)
    end)

    it("validates names", function()
        assert.is_false((ns.Sets:ValidateName("")))
        assert.is_false((ns.Sets:ValidateName("$x")))
        assert.is_false((ns.Sets:ValidateName("City")))
        assert.is_true((ns.Sets:ValidateName("City", "City")))
        local set, ok = ns.Sets:Create("  New  ")
        assert.is_true(ok)
        assert.equal("New", set.name)
        assert.equal(true, set.settings.enabled)
        assert.equal(1, set.settings.priority)
    end)

    it("renames without leaving stale entries and refuses duplicates", function()
        local city = ns.Sets:Get("City")
        assert.is_false((city:Rename("Deep")))
        assert.is_true((city:Rename("Town")))
        assert.equal(city, ns.Sets:Get("Town"))
        assert.is_nil(ns.Sets:Get("City"))
        assert.is_nil(rawget(ns.db.profile.sets, "City"))
        assert.equal("Town", ns.db.profile.sets.Town.name)
    end)

    it("default set cannot be renamed, deleted or disabled", function()
        local d = ns.Sets:Get("$Default")
        assert.is_false((d:Rename("X")))
        assert.is_false((d:Delete()))
        d:SetEnabled(false)
        assert.equal(true, d.settings.enabled)
    end)

    it("weights and chance", function()
        local d = ns.Sets:Get("$Default")
        d:SetPetWeight("BattlePet-0-0005", 64)
        assert.equal(64, d:GetPetWeight("BattlePet-0-0005"))
        assert.is_true(d:HasManualWeight("BattlePet-0-0005"))
        local c = d:GetChance("BattlePet-0-0005")
        assert.is_true(c > 0 and c < 1)
        d:ClearPetWeights()
        assert.is_false(d:HasManualWeight("BattlePet-0-0005"))
        W.world.cooldowns["BattlePet-0-0001"] = 30
        local _, reason = d:GetChance("BattlePet-0-0001")
        assert.equal("On cooldown", reason)
    end)

    it("fires SetsChanged on edits", function()
        local got = {}
        ns:RegisterCallback({}, "SetsChanged", function(_, n) got[#got + 1] = n end)
        ns.Sets:Get("City"):SetPriority(64)
        assert.same({ "City" }, got)
    end)

    it("status reflects enabled/empty/noConditions", function()
        assert.equal("disabled", ns.Sets:Get("Disabled"):GetStatus())
        assert.equal("noConditions", ns.Sets:Get("NoTrigger"):GetStatus())
        local e = ns.Sets:Create("Empty")
        assert.equal("empty", e:GetStatus())
    end)

    it("reloads on profile change", function()
        ns.DB:Set("Other")
        assert.is_nil(ns.Sets:Get("City"))
        assert.is_not_nil(ns.Sets:Get("$Default"))
    end)
end)
```

> Kolejność w `List()`: priorytety z fixture — `RaidOrZone`=64, `City`=8, `Deep`=1, `Disabled`=1 (domyślny), `NoTrigger`=1/8. `Deep` i `Disabled` mają równy priorytet → alfabetycznie.
