- [ ] **Step 3: Implementacja** — `Core/Sets.lua`:

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
local pairs, ipairs, rawget, wipe, sort, strfind, strtrim = pairs, ipairs, rawget, wipe, table.sort, strfind, strtrim

local Sets = ns:NewModule("Sets")
ns.Sets = Sets
Sets.DEFAULT = "$Default"

local cache = {}          -- name -> set object
local current

local SetMixin = {}
SetMixin.__index = SetMixin

local function changed(set)
    set.dirty = true
    ns:Fire("SetsChanged", set and set.name)
    Sets:Update()
end

function SetMixin:IsDefault() return self.name == Sets.DEFAULT end
function SetMixin:DisplayName() return self:IsDefault() and L["Default"] or self.name end

function SetMixin:Rename(name)
    if self:IsDefault() then return false, L["The default set cannot be renamed."] end
    local ok, err = Sets:ValidateName(name, self.name)
    if not ok then return false, err end
    name = strtrim(name)
    if name == self.name then return true end
    local sets = ns.db.profile.sets
    sets[name] = self.settings
    sets[self.name] = nil
    cache[self.name] = nil
    cache[name] = self
    self.name = name
    self.settings.name = name
    changed(self)
    return true
end

function SetMixin:Delete()
    if self:IsDefault() then return false, L["The default set cannot be deleted."] end
    ns.db.profile.sets[self.name] = nil
    cache[self.name] = nil
    if self.watcher then self.watcher:Free() self.watcher = nil end
    self.deleted = true
    ns:Fire("SetsChanged", self.name)
    Sets:Update()
    return true
end

function SetMixin:SetEnabled(v)
    if self:IsDefault() then return end
    self.settings.enabled = v and true or false
    changed(self)
end

function SetMixin:SetPriority(w) if not self:IsDefault() then self.settings.priority = w changed(self) end end
function SetMixin:SetImmediate(v) self.settings.immediate = v and true or nil changed(self) end
function SetMixin:SetDefaultWeight(w) self.settings.defaultValue = w changed(self) end

function SetMixin:GetPetWeight(petID) return ns.Weights.PetWeight(self.settings, petID) end
function SetMixin:HasManualWeight(petID) return rawget(self.settings.pets, petID) ~= nil end
function SetMixin:SetPetWeight(petID, w) self.settings.pets[petID] = w changed(self) end
function SetMixin:SetPetWeights(petIDs, w)
    for _, id in ipairs(petIDs) do self.settings.pets[id] = w end
    changed(self)
end
function SetMixin:ClearPetWeights() wipe(self.settings.pets) changed(self) end

function SetMixin:GetAutoWeight(petID)
    local sum = 0
    for _, e in ipairs(self.settings.filter or {}) do
        if ns.AutoRules.Match(e, petID) then sum = sum + ns.AutoRules.Weight(e) end
    end
    return sum
end

function SetMixin:GetAutoRules() return ns.AutoRules.ToChips(self.settings.filter) end
function SetMixin:FiltersChanged() changed(self) end

function SetMixin:GetWeightTable()
    if self.dirty or not self.weights then
        self.weights = ns.Weights.Build(self.settings, self.weights)
        self.dirty = false
    end
    return self.weights
end

function SetMixin:GetChance(petID)
    local chance = ns.Weights.Chance(self:GetWeightTable(), petID)
    local reason
    if (C_PetJournal.GetPetCooldownByGUID(petID) or 0) > 0 then
        reason = L["On cooldown"]
    elseif not C_PetJournal.PetIsSummonable(petID) then
        reason = L["Cannot be summoned"]
    end
    return chance, reason
end

function SetMixin:PickPet() return ns.Weights.Pick(self:GetWeightTable(), fastrandom()) end
function SetMixin:Contains(petID) return ns.Weights.Sum(self:GetWeightTable(), petID) > 0 end

function SetMixin:HasConditions()
    return not self:IsDefault() and self.settings.trigger ~= nil and #self.settings.trigger > 0
end

function SetMixin:ConditionsMet()
    if self:IsDefault() or not self.watcher then return true end
    return self.watcher.value
end

function SetMixin:IsActive()
    if self.deleted or not self.settings.enabled then return false end
    if not self:ConditionsMet() then return false end
    return self:GetWeightTable().total > 0
end

function SetMixin:GetPriority()
    return self:IsDefault() and -1 or (self.settings.priority or 1)
end

function SetMixin:GetStatus()
    if not self.settings.enabled then return "disabled" end
    if self:GetWeightTable().total <= 0 then return "empty" end
    if current == self then return "active" end
    if not self:IsDefault() and not self:HasConditions() then return "noConditions" end
    return "waiting"
end

--
-- Collection
--

local function newSet(name)
    local set = setmetatable({ name = name, settings = ns.db.profile.sets[name], dirty = true }, SetMixin)
    cache[name] = set
    if ns.Conditions and not set:IsDefault() then
        set.watcher = ns.Conditions.NewWatcher(set.settings.trigger, function() Sets:Update() end)
    end
    return set
end

function Sets:Get(name)
    if cache[name] then return cache[name] end
    if rawget(ns.db.profile.sets, name) then return newSet(name) end
end

function Sets:List()
    local out = {}
    for name in pairs(ns.db.profile.sets) do
        out[#out + 1] = self:Get(name)
    end
    sort(out, function(a, b)
        if a:IsDefault() ~= b:IsDefault() then return a:IsDefault() end
        if a:GetPriority() ~= b:GetPriority() then return a:GetPriority() > b:GetPriority() end
        return a.name < b.name
    end)
    return out
end

function Sets:ValidateName(name, except)
    name = name and strtrim(name) or ""
    if name == "" or strfind(name, "^%$") then
        return false, L["Set name cannot be empty or begin with '$'."]
    end
    if name ~= except and rawget(ns.db.profile.sets, name) then
        return false, L["A set with this name already exists."]
    end
    return true
end

function Sets:Create(name)
    local ok, err = self:ValidateName(name)
    if not ok then return nil, false, err end
    name = strtrim(name)
    local settings = ns.db.profile.sets[name]   -- wildcard defaults create it
    settings.name = name
    local set = newSet(name)
    ns:Fire("SetsChanged", name)
    self:Update()
    return set, true
end

function Sets:Reload()
    for _, set in pairs(cache) do
        if set.watcher then set.watcher:Free() end
    end
    wipe(cache)
    current = nil
    rawget(ns.db.profile.sets, Sets.DEFAULT)
    ns:Fire("SetsChanged", nil)
    self:Update()
end

function Sets:GetCurrent() return current end

function Sets:Update()
    local best
    for _, set in ipairs(self:List()) do
        if set:IsActive() and (not best or set:GetPriority() > best:GetPriority()) then
            best = set
        end
    end
    if best ~= current then
        current = best
        ns:Fire("CurrentSetChanged", current)
        if current and current.settings.immediate and ns.Summon then
            ns.Summon:OnImmediateSet(current)
        end
    end
end

function Sets:OnInitialize()
    ns:RegisterCallback(self, "ProfileChanged", function() Sets:Reload() end)
    ns:RegisterCallback(self, "PetsChanged", function()
        for _, set in pairs(cache) do set.dirty = true end
        Sets:Update()
    end)
end

function Sets:OnEnable() self:Reload() end
```

> Rozstrzyganie remisu priorytetów: 1.0.x wybierał **ostatni** zestaw o równym priorytecie w kolejności `next()`, czyli w praktyce przypadkowo. 2.0 bierze pierwszy w kolejności `List()` (alfabetycznie) — deterministycznie. Test referencyjny w Task 14 porównuje `current` tylko dla światów bez remisu; jeśli referencja ma remis, zanotuj to w teście jako znaną, zamierzoną różnicę.

Klucze do `Locales/enUS.lua`: `Default`, `The default set cannot be renamed.`, `The default set cannot be deleted.`, `On cooldown`, `Cannot be summoned`, `Set name cannot be empty or begin with '$'.`, `A set with this name already exists.`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ns.db.global.window`, `ns.Sets`, `ns.L`.
- Produces:
  - `ns.Skin:Apply(kind, frame, ...)` — `kind` ∈ `"Shell","Inset","Panel","Tab","EditBox","Button","Dropdown","Checkbox","ScrollBar","CloseButton","SquareIcon"`; bez EllesmereUI no-op; z EllesmereUI woła `S[kind](frame, ...)`. Elementy zgłoszone przed callbackiem EllesmereUI są skórkowane, gdy callback nadejdzie. `ns.Skin:SetTabSelected(tab, selected)`, `ns.Skin:Accent() → r,g,b` (domyślnie złoty `1, 0.82, 0`), `ns.Skin:OnLooksChanged(fn)`.
  - `ns.Widgets.Button(parent, text, onClick) → button` (UIPanelButtonTemplate, szerokość = tekst + 24, min 80), `ns.Widgets.Checkbox(parent, label, get, set) → cb`, `ns.Widgets.Dropdown(parent, width, buildMenu) → dd` (WowStyle1DropdownTemplate + `SetupMenu`), `ns.Widgets.Search(parent, onChanged) → editbox` (SearchBoxTemplate, debounce 0,15 s), `ns.Widgets.Slider(parent, min, max, steps, value, onChange, formatter) → slider`, `ns.Widgets.Label(parent, text, font?) → fontstring`, `ns.Widgets.Inset(parent) → frame`, `ns.Widgets.Tooltip(frame, titleOrFn, body?)`.
  - `ns.UI.Main` — okno: `:Toggle()`, `:Show()`, `:Hide()`, `:SelectSet(name)`, `:SelectTab(key)` (`"pets"|"when"|"settings"`), `:GetSelectedSet() → set`, `:RegisterTab(key, label, factory, order)` (factory(contentFrame) → tabObject z `:Refresh(set)`; `order` ustala kolejność zakładek), `:SetLeftPanel(frame)`, `:SetStatusBar(frame)`, `:GetContentWidth()`; callback `ns:Fire("WindowResized", width, height)`.
  - `ns:OpenWindow()`; komenda `/pcall` i `/petcall` bez argumentu → `ns:OpenWindow()` (pełne podkomendy w Task 21).

- [ ] **Step 2: `UI/Skin.lua`**

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local Skin = {}
ns.Skin = Skin

local S                 -- EllesmereUI skin API once available
local pending = {}      -- { kind, frame, args } registered before EUI's callback
local accentListeners = {}

function Skin:Apply(kind, frame, ...)
    if not frame then return end
    if S then
        if S[kind] then S[kind](frame, ...) end
    else
        pending[#pending + 1] = { kind, frame, { ... } }
    end
end

function Skin:SetTabSelected(tab, selected)
    if S and S.SetTabSelection then S.SetTabSelection(tab, selected) end
end

function Skin:Accent()
    if S and S.GetAccentColor then return S.GetAccentColor() end
    return 1, 0.82, 0
end

function Skin:IsActive() return S ~= nil end

function Skin:OnLooksChanged(fn) accentListeners[#accentListeners + 1] = fn end

if EllesmereUI and EllesmereUI.RegisterSkin then
    EllesmereUI.RegisterSkin("PetCall", function(api)
        S = api
        for _, p in ipairs(pending) do
            if S[p[1]] then S[p[1]](p[2], unpack(p[3])) end
        end
        pending = nil
        if S.OnLooksChanged then
            S.OnLooksChanged(function()
                for _, fn in ipairs(accentListeners) do fn() end
            end)
        end
    end)
else
    -- No EllesmereUI: keep nothing queued.
    Skin.Apply = function() end
end
```

> Bez EllesmereUI `Apply` jest no-opem (nic nie trzymamy w pamięci). Z EllesmereUI elementy zgłoszone przed `PLAYER_LOGIN` czekają w `pending`.

- [ ] **Step 3: `UI/Widgets.lua`**

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local CreateFrame, C_Timer = CreateFrame, C_Timer
local Widgets = {}
ns.Widgets = Widgets

-- Generic confirmation: StaticPopup_Show("PETCALL_CONFIRM", text, nil, { onAccept = fn })
StaticPopupDialogs["PETCALL_CONFIRM"] = {
    text = "%s", button1 = YES, button2 = NO, timeout = 0, whileDead = true, hideOnEscape = true,
    OnAccept = function(_, data) data.onAccept() end,
}

function Widgets.Label(parent, text, font)
    local fs = parent:CreateFontString(nil, "OVERLAY", font or "GameFontHighlight")
    fs:SetJustifyH("LEFT")
    fs:SetText(text or "")
    return fs
end

function Widgets.Button(parent, text, onClick)
    local b = CreateFrame("Button", nil, parent, "UIPanelButtonTemplate")
    b:SetText(text)
    b:SetHeight(22)
    b:SetWidth(math.max(80, b:GetFontString():GetStringWidth() + 24))
    b:SetScript("OnClick", function(self, button) onClick(self, button) end)
    ns.Skin:Apply("Button", b)
    return b
end

function Widgets.Checkbox(parent, label, get, set)
    local cb = CreateFrame("CheckButton", nil, parent, "UICheckButtonTemplate")
    cb:SetSize(24, 24)
    cb.text:SetText(label)
    cb.text:SetFontObject("GameFontHighlight")
    cb:SetScript("OnShow", function(self) self:SetChecked(get() and true or false) end)
    cb:SetScript("OnClick", function(self)
        PlaySound(self:GetChecked() and SOUNDKIT.IG_MAINMENU_OPTION_CHECKBOX_ON or SOUNDKIT.IG_MAINMENU_OPTION_CHECKBOX_OFF)
        set(self:GetChecked())
    end)
    cb:SetChecked(get() and true or false)
    ns.Skin:Apply("Checkbox", cb)
    return cb
end

-- buildMenu(owner, rootDescription) uses MenuUtil description API (CreateRadio/CreateButton/...)
function Widgets.Dropdown(parent, width, buildMenu)
    local dd = CreateFrame("DropdownButton", nil, parent, "WowStyle1DropdownTemplate")
    dd:SetWidth(width)
    dd:SetupMenu(buildMenu)
    ns.Skin:Apply("Dropdown", dd)
    return dd
end

function Widgets.Search(parent, onChanged)
    local eb = CreateFrame("EditBox", nil, parent, "SearchBoxTemplate")
    eb:SetHeight(20)
    eb:SetAutoFocus(false)
    local timer
    eb:HookScript("OnTextChanged", function(self, userInput)
        if timer then timer:Cancel() end
        timer = C_Timer.NewTimer(userInput and 0.15 or 0, function()
            timer = nil
            onChanged(self:GetText())
        end)
    end)
    ns.Skin:Apply("EditBox", eb, { padInput = true })
    return eb
end

function Widgets.Slider(parent, minValue, maxValue, steps, value, onChange, formatter)
    local s = CreateFrame("Slider", nil, parent, "MinimalSliderWithSteppersTemplate")
    local formatters = formatter and { [MinimalSliderWithSteppersMixin.Label.Right] = formatter } or nil
    s:Init(value, minValue, maxValue, steps, formatters)
    s:RegisterCallback(MinimalSliderWithSteppersMixin.Event.OnValueChanged, function(_, v) onChange(v) end, s)
    return s
end

function Widgets.Inset(parent)
    local f = CreateFrame("Frame", nil, parent, "InsetFrameTemplate")
    ns.Skin:Apply("Inset", f)
    return f
end

function Widgets.Tooltip(frame, title, body)
    frame:HookScript("OnEnter", function(self)
        GameTooltip:SetOwner(self, "ANCHOR_RIGHT")
        local t = type(title) == "function" and title(self) or title
        if t then GameTooltip:SetText(t, 1, 1, 1) end
        if body then GameTooltip:AddLine(type(body) == "function" and body(self) or body, nil, nil, nil, true) end
        GameTooltip:Show()
    end)
    frame:HookScript("OnLeave", function() GameTooltip:Hide() end)
end
```

> **Weryfikacja API w grze:** przed implementacją sprawdź w kliencie 12.1.5 (`/dump MinimalSliderWithSteppersMixin.Event`, `/dump WowStyle1DropdownTemplate` przez `/fstack` na dowolnej liście w Ustawieniach), że nazwy szablonów i pól istnieją. Jeśli któraś nazwa się różni — popraw tylko w `Widgets.lua`, reszta UI jej nie zna.

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
local CreateFrame, UIParent = CreateFrame, UIParent

ns.UI = ns.UI or {}
local Main = {}
ns.UI.Main = Main

local MIN_W, MIN_H = 640, 440
local frame, tabs, tabOrder, tabButtons, selectedTab, selectedSet = nil, {}, {}, {}, nil, nil

local function saveGeometry()
    local g = ns.db.global.window
    g.width, g.height = frame:GetSize()
    local point, _, relPoint, x, y = frame:GetPoint(1)
    g.point, g.relPoint, g.x, g.y = point, relPoint, x, y
end

local function applyScale()
    frame:SetScale(ns.db.global.window.scale or 1)
end

function Main:Build()
    if frame then return end
    local g = ns.db.global.window
    frame = CreateFrame("Frame", "PetCallMainWindow", UIParent, "ButtonFrameTemplate")
    frame:SetTitle("PetCall")
    frame:SetPortraitToAsset("Interface\\AddOns\\PetCall\\Media\\icon")
    frame:SetSize(g.width or 820, g.height or 560)
    frame:SetPoint(g.point or "CENTER", UIParent, g.relPoint or "CENTER", g.x or 0, g.y or 0)
    frame:SetFrameStrata("HIGH")
    frame:SetToplevel(true)
    frame:SetMovable(true)
    frame:SetResizable(true)
    frame:SetResizeBounds(MIN_W, MIN_H)
    frame:SetClampedToScreen(true)
    frame:EnableMouse(true)
    frame:RegisterForDrag("LeftButton")
    frame:SetScript("OnDragStart", frame.StartMoving)
    frame:SetScript("OnDragStop", function(self) self:StopMovingOrSizing() saveGeometry() end)
    frame:Hide()
    tinsert(UISpecialFrames, "PetCallMainWindow")
    ButtonFrameTemplate_HideButtonBar(frame)
    applyScale()
    ns.Skin:Apply("Shell", frame, { bottomBar = 28 })
    ns.Skin:Apply("CloseButton", frame.CloseButton)
    ns.Skin:Apply("Inset", frame.Inset)

    local resize = CreateFrame("Button", nil, frame, "PanelResizeButtonTemplate")
    resize:SetPoint("BOTTOMRIGHT", -4, 4)
    resize:Init(frame, MIN_W, MIN_H, GetScreenWidth(), GetScreenHeight())
    resize:SetOnResizeStoppedCallback(saveGeometry)

    local gear = CreateFrame("Button", nil, frame)
    gear:SetSize(20, 20)
    gear:SetPoint("RIGHT", frame.CloseButton, "LEFT", -2, 0)
    gear:SetNormalAtlas("mechagon-projects")
    gear:SetHighlightAtlas("mechagon-projects", "ADD")
    gear:SetScript("OnClick", function() ns:OpenSettings() end)
    ns.Widgets.Tooltip(gear, L["Settings"])

    -- Layout regions: left panel (sets), content (tabs), status bar (bottom).
    frame.left = CreateFrame("Frame", nil, frame.Inset)
    frame.left:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 4, -4)
    frame.left:SetPoint("BOTTOMLEFT", 4, 4)
    frame.left:SetWidth(190)

    frame.content = CreateFrame("Frame", nil, frame.Inset)
    frame.content:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", frame.left, "TOPRIGHT", 6, -28)
    frame.content:SetPoint("BOTTOMRIGHT", -4, 4)

    frame.status = CreateFrame("Frame", nil, frame)
    frame.status:SetPoint("BOTTOMLEFT", 8, 6)
    frame.status:SetPoint("BOTTOMRIGHT", -24, 6)
    frame.status:SetHeight(22)

    frame:SetScript("OnSizeChanged", function(_, w, h)
        ns:Fire("WindowResized", w, h)
    end)
    frame:SetScript("OnShow", function()
        PlaySound(SOUNDKIT.IG_CHARACTER_INFO_OPEN)
        if not selectedSet or selectedSet.deleted then Main:SelectSet(ns.Sets.DEFAULT) end
        if not selectedTab then Main:SelectTab("pets") end
        ns:Fire("WindowShown")
    end)
    frame:SetScript("OnHide", function()
        PlaySound(SOUNDKIT.IG_CHARACTER_INFO_CLOSE)
        ns:Fire("WindowHidden")
    end)

    -- fade in
    local ag = frame:CreateAnimationGroup()
    local a = ag:CreateAnimation("Alpha")
    a:SetFromAlpha(0) a:SetToAlpha(1) a:SetDuration(0.15)
    frame:HookScript("OnShow", function() ag:Play() end)

    for _, key in ipairs(tabOrder) do Main:BuildTab(key) end
    ns:Fire("WindowBuilt", frame)
end

-- order: 1 = pets, 2 = when, 3 = settings (files load in a different order)
function Main:RegisterTab(key, label, factory, order)
    tabs[key] = { key = key, label = label, factory = factory, order = order or 99 }
    tabOrder[#tabOrder + 1] = key
    table.sort(tabOrder, function(a, b) return tabs[a].order < tabs[b].order end)
    assert(not frame, "register tabs at load time")
end

function Main:BuildTab(key)
    local t = tabs[key]
    if t.button then return end
    local index = #tabButtons + 1
    local b = CreateFrame("Button", nil, frame, "PanelTopTabButtonTemplate")
    b:SetID(index)
    b:SetText(t.label)
    b:SetScript("OnClick", function() Main:SelectTab(key) end)
    if index == 1 then
        b:SetPoint("BOTTOMLEFT", frame.content, "TOPLEFT", 4, 2)
    else
        b:SetPoint("LEFT", tabButtons[index - 1], "RIGHT", 2, 0)
    end
    PanelTemplates_TabResize(b, 12)
    tabButtons[index], t.button = b, b
    frame.Tabs = tabButtons
    PanelTemplates_SetNumTabs(frame, #tabButtons)
    ns.Skin:Apply("Tab", b)
    t.page = CreateFrame("Frame", nil, frame.content)
    t.page:SetAllPoints()
    t.page:Hide()
    t.object = t.factory(t.page)
end

function Main:SelectTab(key)
    local t = tabs[key]
    if not t or not t.button:IsShown() then key, t = "pets", tabs.pets end
    selectedTab = key
    for k, other in pairs(tabs) do
        local on = k == key
        other.page:SetShown(on)
        ns.Skin:SetTabSelected(other.button, on)
    end
    PanelTemplates_SetTab(frame, t.button:GetID())
    if t.object.Refresh then t.object:Refresh(selectedSet) end
end

function Main:SelectSet(name)
    selectedSet = ns.Sets:Get(name) or ns.Sets:Get(ns.Sets.DEFAULT)
    -- the "when" tab does not exist for the default set
    if tabs.when and tabs.when.button then
        tabs.when.button:SetShown(not selectedSet:IsDefault())
    end
    ns:Fire("SelectedSetChanged", selectedSet)
    self:SelectTab(selectedTab or "pets")
end

function Main:GetSelectedSet() return selectedSet end
function Main:GetFrame() self:Build() return frame end
function Main:GetContentWidth() return frame and frame.content:GetWidth() or 0 end

function Main:SetScale(scale)
    ns.db.global.window.scale = scale
    if frame then applyScale() end
end

function Main:Show() self:Build() frame:Show() end
function Main:Hide() if frame then frame:Hide() end end
function Main:Toggle()
    self:Build()
    frame:SetShown(not frame:IsShown())
end

function ns:OpenWindow() Main:Show() end

ns:RegisterCallback(Main, "SetsChanged", function(_, name)
    if selectedSet and selectedSet.deleted then Main:SelectSet(ns.Sets.DEFAULT) end
end)
ns:RegisterCallback(Main, "ProfileChanged", function()
    if frame and frame:IsShown() then Main:SelectSet(ns.Sets.DEFAULT) end
end)
```

> `ns:OpenSettings()` powstaje w Task 20; do tego czasu przycisk ⚙ wypisze błąd — dodaj na początku `UI/MainWindow.lua` tymczasowo `ns.OpenSettings = ns.OpenSettings or function() end` i usuń tę linię w Task 20.

`Core/Commands.lua` (wersja minimalna; pełna w Task 21):

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local Commands = ns:NewModule("Commands")
ns.Commands = Commands
Commands.handlers = {}

function Commands:Handle(input)
    input = strtrim(input or "")
    if input == "" then return ns:OpenWindow() end
    local cmd, rest = input:match("^(%S+)%s*(.-)$")
    local h = self.handlers[strlower(cmd)]
    if h then return h(rest) end
    ns:Printf(ns.L["Unknown command: %s. Type /pcall help."], cmd)
end

function Commands:OnInitialize()
    SLASH_PETCALL1, SLASH_PETCALL2 = "/petcall", "/pcall"
    SlashCmdList.PETCALL = function(msg) Commands:Handle(msg) end
end
```

```lua
describe("PetGridModel", function()
    local ns, set
    before_each(function()
        W.reset()
        W.world.pets = {
            a = { species = 1, name = "Alpha", level = 25, type = 8, quality = 4 },
            b = { species = 2, name = "Beta", custom = "Kitty", level = 3, type = 10, quality = 2 },

        assert.same({ "b" }, ns.PetGridModel.Filter(set, { show = "unselected" }))
        assert.same({ "a", "c" }, ns.PetGridModel.Filter(set, { types = { [8] = true } }))

    it("sorts", function()
        assert.same({ "a", "c", "b" }, ns.PetGridModel.Filter(set, { sort = "level" }))
        assert.same({ "a", "c", "b" }, ns.PetGridModel.Filter(set, { sort = "rarity" }))
    end)
    it("chunks rows", function()
        assert.same({ { "a", "b" }, { "c" } }, ns.PetGridModel.Rows({ "a", "b", "c" }, 2))
    end)
    it("empty collection", function()
        W.world.pets = {}
        ns.PetCache:Rescan()
        assert.same({}, ns.PetGridModel.Filter(set, {}))
    end)
end)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `UI/PetGridModel.lua`**

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local strfind, strlower, sort, ipairs = strfind, strlower, table.sort, ipairs
local M = {}
ns.PetGridModel = M

local function isSelected(set, id)
    return set:GetPetWeight(id) > 0 or set:GetAutoWeight(id) > 0
end

function M.Filter(set, state)
    state = state or {}
    local search = strlower(state.search or "")
    local out = {}
    for _, id in ns.PetCache:IteratePetIDs() do
        local info = ns.PetCache:GetInfo(id)
        local ok = true
        if search ~= "" and not strfind(info.sortKey, search, 1, true) then ok = false end
        if ok and state.show == "selected" and not isSelected(set, id) then ok = false end
        if ok and state.show == "unselected" and isSelected(set, id) then ok = false end
        if ok and state.types and next(state.types) and not state.types[info.petType] then ok = false end
        if ok and state.favoritesOnly and not info.isFavorite then ok = false end
        if ok then out[#out + 1] = id end
    end
    local key = state.sort or "name"
    if key ~= "name" then
        local tbl = key == "chance" and set:GetWeightTable() or nil
        local function value(id)
            local info = ns.PetCache:GetInfo(id)
            if key == "level" then return info.level end
            if key == "rarity" then return info.quality end
            return ns.Weights.Chance(tbl, id)
        end
        sort(out, function(a, b)
            local va, vb = value(a), value(b)
            if va == vb then return ns.PetCache:GetSortKey(a) < ns.PetCache:GetSortKey(b) end
            return va > vb
        end)
    end
    return out
end

function M.Rows(ids, columns)
    local rows, row = {}, nil
    for i, id in ipairs(ids) do
        if (i - 1) % columns == 0 then row = {} rows[#rows + 1] = row end
        row[#row + 1] = id
    end
    return rows
end

function M.Badge(set, id)
    local w = set:GetPetWeight(id)
    if set:HasManualWeight(id) or set:GetAutoWeight(id) == 0 then
        if w == 0 then return ns.Weights.Short(0), "never" end
        return ns.Weights.Short(w), "manual"
    end
    if w == 0 then return ns.L["auto"], "auto" end
    return ns.Weights.Short(w), "manual"
end
```

> `sortKey` z PetCache zawiera małymi literami nazwę i własną nazwę, więc wyszukiwanie po kluczu obejmuje oba pola bez wywołań API.

- [ ] **Step 5: `UI/PetPreview.lua`** — stały panel po prawej:

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
local CreateFrame = CreateFrame
ns.UI = ns.UI or {}
local Preview = {}
ns.UI.Preview = Preview

local panel, model, set, hoverID, selectedID
local angle, dragging, lastX = 0, false, nil

local function petToShow()
    return hoverID or selectedID or C_PetJournal.GetSummonedPetGUID()
end

function Preview:Attach(parent)
    panel = CreateFrame("Frame", nil, parent)
    panel:SetWidth(240)
    ns.Skin:Apply("Panel", panel, { inset = true })

    model = CreateFrame("PlayerModel", nil, panel)
    model:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 4, -4)
    model:SetPoint("TOPRIGHT", -4, -4)
    model:SetHeight(220)
    model:EnableMouse(true)
    model:EnableMouseWheel(true)
    model:SetScript("OnMouseDown", function(_, button) if button == "LeftButton" then dragging, lastX = true, GetCursorPosition() end end)
    model:SetScript("OnMouseUp", function() dragging = false end)
    model:SetScript("OnMouseWheel", function(self, delta)
        local z = (self.zoom or 0) + delta * 0.1
        self.zoom = math.max(-0.5, math.min(0.7, z))
        self:SetPortraitZoom(self.zoom)
    end)
    model:SetScript("OnUpdate", function(self, elapsed)
        if dragging then
            local x = GetCursorPosition()
            angle = angle + (x - lastX) * 0.02
            lastX = x
        else
            angle = angle + elapsed * 0.3       -- slow idle rotation
        end
        self:SetFacing(angle)
    end)

    panel.name = ns.Widgets.Label(panel, "", "GameFontNormalLarge")
    panel.name:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", model, "BOTTOMLEFT", 4, -6)
    panel.name:SetPoint("RIGHT", -6, 0)
    panel.sub = ns.Widgets.Label(panel, "", "GameFontHighlightSmall")
    panel.sub:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", panel.name, "BOTTOMLEFT", 0, -2)
    panel.sub:SetPoint("RIGHT", -6, 0)
    panel.source = ns.Widgets.Label(panel, "", "GameFontDisableSmall")
    panel.source:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", panel.sub, "BOTTOMLEFT", 0, -4)
    panel.source:SetPoint("RIGHT", -6, 0)
    panel.source:SetWordWrap(true)

    local steps = #ns.Weights.LEVELS - 1
    panel.slider = ns.Widgets.Slider(panel, 0, steps, steps, 3, function(v)
        local id = selectedID or hoverID
        if id and set then set:SetPetWeight(id, ns.Weights.LEVELS[math.floor(v + 0.5) + 1].value) end
    end, function(v) return ns.Weights.LEVELS[math.floor(v + 0.5) + 1].label end)
    panel.slider:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", panel.source, "BOTTOMLEFT", -2, -10)
    panel.slider:SetPoint("RIGHT", -6, 0)

    panel.chance = ns.Widgets.Label(panel, "", "GameFontHighlight")
    panel.chance:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", panel.slider, "BOTTOMLEFT", 2, -8)
    panel.chance:SetPoint("RIGHT", -6, 0)

    panel.summon = ns.Widgets.Button(panel, L["Summon"], function()
        local id = selectedID or hoverID
        if id then ns:SummonPet(id) end
    end)
    panel.summon:SetPoint("BOTTOMLEFT", 6, 6)
    panel.fav = ns.Widgets.Button(panel, "☆", function()
        local id = selectedID or hoverID
        if not id then return end
        local info = ns.PetCache:GetInfo(id)
        C_PetJournal.SetFavorite(id, info.isFavorite and 0 or 1)
    end)
    panel.fav:SetWidth(32)
    panel.fav:SetPoint("LEFT", panel.summon, "RIGHT", 4, 0)
    return panel
end

function Preview:ShowPet(petID, forSet)
    set = forSet or set
    if not panel then return end
    local info = petID and ns.PetCache:GetInfo(petID)
    if not info then
        panel.name:SetText(L["No pet selected"])
        panel.sub:SetText("") panel.source:SetText("") panel.chance:SetText("")
        model:ClearModel()
        return
    end
    if model.creatureID ~= info.creatureID then
        model.creatureID = info.creatureID
        model:ClearModel()
        model:SetCreature(info.creatureID)
        angle = 0
    end
    local r, g, b = C_Item.GetItemQualityColor((info.quality or 2) - 1)
    panel.name:SetText(info.name)
    panel.name:SetTextColor(r, g, b)
    panel.sub:SetText(format("%s%s · %s %d · %s", info.customName and ("«" .. info.customName .. "» ") or "",
        _G["BATTLE_PET_NAME_" .. (info.petType or 0)] or "", L["Level"], info.level, _G["BATTLE_PET_BREED_QUALITY" .. (info.quality or 2)] or ""))
    local _, _, _, _, source = C_PetJournal.GetPetInfoBySpeciesID(info.speciesID)
    panel.source:SetText(source or "")
    if set then
        local w = set:GetPetWeight(petID)
        for i, lvl in ipairs(ns.Weights.LEVELS) do
            if lvl.value == ns.Weights.Nearest(w) then panel.slider:SetValue(i - 1) end
        end
        local chance, reason = set:GetChance(petID)
        panel.chance:SetText(format(L["Chance in this set: %.1f%%"], chance * 100) .. (reason and ("\n|cffff8040" .. reason .. "|r") or ""))
    end
    panel.fav:SetText(info.isFavorite and "★" or "☆")
end

function Preview:SetHover(petID) hoverID = petID self:ShowPet(petToShow()) end
function Preview:SetSelected(petID) selectedID = petID self:ShowPet(petToShow()) end
function Preview:Refresh() self:ShowPet(petToShow()) end
function Preview:GetPanel() return panel end
```

- [ ] **Step 6: `UI/PetsTab.lua`** — pasek narzędzi, miejsce na chipy (Task 11), siatka, podgląd, dymek przy wąskim oknie:

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
local CreateFrame = CreateFrame
local TILE_W, TILE_H, PREVIEW_MIN_WIDTH = 200, 48, 760

local Tab = {}
local state = { search = "", show = "all", sort = "name" }
local set, page, grid, scrollBox, scrollBar, dataProvider, emptyText, columns = nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, 1
local floatingPreview

local QUALITY_BORDER = "Interface\\Common\\WhiteIconFrame"

local function weightMenu(owner, root, petID)
    root:CreateTitle(ns.PetCache:GetInfo(petID).name)
    for _, lvl in ipairs(ns.Weights.LEVELS) do
        root:CreateRadio(lvl.label, function() return ns.Weights.Nearest(set:GetPetWeight(petID)) == lvl.value end,
            function() set:SetPetWeight(petID, lvl.value) end)
    end
    root:CreateDivider()
    root:CreateButton(L["Summon now"], function() ns:SummonPet(petID) end)
    local info = ns.PetCache:GetInfo(petID)
    root:CreateButton(info.isFavorite and L["Remove favorite"] or L["Set favorite"], function()
        C_PetJournal.SetFavorite(petID, info.isFavorite and 0 or 1)
    end)
end

local function tile_OnClick(self, button)
    local id = self.petID
    if not id then return end
    if button == "RightButton" or IsControlKeyDown() then
        MenuUtil.CreateContextMenu(self, function(owner, root) weightMenu(owner, root, id) end)
        return
    end
    local w = set:GetPetWeight(id)
    set:SetPetWeight(id, w == 0 and ns.Weights.NORMAL or ns.Weights.NEVER)
    PlaySound(w == 0 and SOUNDKIT.IG_MAINMENU_OPTION_CHECKBOX_ON or SOUNDKIT.IG_MAINMENU_OPTION_CHECKBOX_OFF)
    ns.UI.Preview:SetSelected(id)
end

local function tile_OnEnter(self)
    if not self.petID then return end
    if floatingPreview then
        floatingPreview:ShowForCreature(ns.PetCache:GetCreatureID(self.petID), "LEFT", self, "RIGHT", 6, 0)
    else
        ns.UI.Preview:SetHover(self.petID)
    end
    if self.name:IsTruncated() then
        GameTooltip:SetOwner(self, "ANCHOR_TOP")
        GameTooltip:SetText(self.name:GetText())
        GameTooltip:Show()
    end
end

local function tile_OnLeave(self)
    GameTooltip:Hide()
    if floatingPreview then floatingPreview:ScheduleHide() else ns.UI.Preview:SetHover(nil) end
end

local function createTile(parent)
    local t = CreateFrame("Button", nil, parent)
    t:SetSize(TILE_W - 4, TILE_H - 4)
    t:RegisterForClicks("LeftButtonUp", "RightButtonUp")
    t:SetHighlightAtlas("PetList-ButtonHighlight", "ADD")
    t.bg = t:CreateTexture(nil, "BACKGROUND")
    t.bg:SetAllPoints()
    t.bg:SetAtlas("PetList-ButtonBackground")
    t.icon = t:CreateTexture(nil, "ARTWORK")
    t.icon:SetSize(36, 36)
    t.icon:SetPoint("LEFT", 4, 0)
    t.border = t:CreateTexture(nil, "OVERLAY")
    t.border:SetTexture(QUALITY_BORDER)
    t.border:SetAllPoints(t.icon)
    t.name = t:CreateFontString(nil, "OVERLAY", "GameFontNormal")
    t.name:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", t.icon, "TOPRIGHT", 6, -2)
    t.name:SetPoint("RIGHT", -40, 0)
    t.name:SetJustifyH("LEFT")
    t.name:SetWordWrap(false)
    t.sub = t:CreateFontString(nil, "OVERLAY", "GameFontHighlightSmall")
    t.sub:SetPoint("BOTTOMLEFT", t.icon, "BOTTOMRIGHT", 6, 2)
    t.sub:SetPoint("RIGHT", -40, 0)
    t.sub:SetJustifyH("LEFT")
    t.sub:SetWordWrap(false)
    t.badge = t:CreateFontString(nil, "OVERLAY", "GameFontNormalSmall")
    t.badge:SetPoint("TOPRIGHT", -6, -6)
    t:SetScript("OnClick", tile_OnClick)
    t:SetScript("OnEnter", tile_OnEnter)
    t:SetScript("OnLeave", tile_OnLeave)
    ns.Skin:Apply("SquareIcon", t.icon, t)
    return t
end

local BADGE_COLORS = { never = { 0.6, 0.6, 0.6 }, manual = { 0.4, 1, 0.4 }, auto = { 0.4, 0.8, 1 } }
-- "manual" badges follow the EllesmereUI accent when it is present
local function refreshAccent() if ns.Skin:IsActive() then BADGE_COLORS.manual = { ns.Skin:Accent() } end end
ns.Skin:OnLooksChanged(function() refreshAccent() Tab:Rebuild() end)

local function setupTile(t, petID)
    t.petID = petID
    t:SetShown(petID ~= nil)
    if not petID then return end
    local info = ns.PetCache:GetInfo(petID)
    t.icon:SetTexture(info.icon)
    local r, g, b = C_Item.GetItemQualityColor((info.quality or 2) - 1)
    t.border:SetVertexColor(r, g, b)
    t.name:SetText(info.name)
    t.sub:SetText(format("%s%d", info.customName and (info.customName .. " · ") or "", info.level))
    local text, kind = ns.PetGridModel.Badge(set, petID)
    t.badge:SetText(text or "")
    if kind then t.badge:SetTextColor(unpack(BADGE_COLORS[kind])) end
    t:SetAlpha(kind == "never" and 0.45 or 1)
    t.icon:SetDesaturated(kind == "never")
end

local function initRow(row, data)
    row.tiles = row.tiles or {}
    for i = 1, columns do
        local t = row.tiles[i]
        if not t then
            t = createTile(row)
            t:SetPoint("LEFT", (i - 1) * TILE_W, 0)
            row.tiles[i] = t
        end
        setupTile(t, data[i])
    end
    for i = columns + 1, #row.tiles do row.tiles[i]:Hide() end
end

function Tab:Rebuild()
    if not set or not page:IsVisible() then return end
    refreshAccent()
    local ids = ns.PetGridModel.Filter(set, state)
    local rows = ns.PetGridModel.Rows(ids, columns)
    dataProvider = CreateDataProvider(rows)
    scrollBox:SetDataProvider(dataProvider, ScrollBoxConstants.RetainScrollPosition)
    if ns.PetCache:NumPets() == 0 then
        emptyText:SetText(L["You have no pets yet."])
        page.openJournal:Show()
    elseif #ids == 0 then
        emptyText:SetText(format(L["No pets match \"%s\"."], state.search))
        page.openJournal:Hide()
    else
        emptyText:SetText("")
        page.openJournal:Hide()
    end
    ns.UI.Preview:ShowPet(nil, set)
    ns.UI.Preview:Refresh()
end

local function layout()
    local width = ns.UI.Main:GetContentWidth()
    local narrow = ns.UI.Main:GetFrame():GetWidth() < PREVIEW_MIN_WIDTH
    local previewPanel = ns.UI.Preview:GetPanel()
    previewPanel:SetShown(not narrow)
    if narrow and not floatingPreview then floatingPreview = ns:CreateModelPreview("PetCallGridPreview") end
    if not narrow and floatingPreview then floatingPreview:Hide() floatingPreview = nil end
    grid:SetPoint("BOTTOMRIGHT", narrow and page or previewPanel, narrow and "BOTTOMRIGHT" or "BOTTOMLEFT", narrow and -18 or -22, 0)
    local gridWidth = width - (narrow and 18 or (240 + 22))
    local newColumns = math.max(1, math.floor(gridWidth / TILE_W))
    if newColumns ~= columns then columns = newColumns end
    Tab:Rebuild()
end

function Tab:Refresh(newSet)
    set = newSet
    page.defaultWeight:GenerateMenu()
    if ns.UI.AutoRulesBar then ns.UI.AutoRulesBar:Refresh(set) end
    layout()
end

local function filterMenu(owner, root)
    local function radio(text, key, value)
        root:CreateRadio(text, function() return state[key] == value end, function() state[key] = value Tab:Rebuild() end)
    end
    root:CreateTitle(L["Show"])
    radio(ALL, "show", "all")
    radio(L["Selected"], "show", "selected")
    radio(L["Unselected"], "show", "unselected")
    root:CreateCheckbox(L["Favorites only"], function() return state.favoritesOnly end,
        function() state.favoritesOnly = not state.favoritesOnly Tab:Rebuild() end)
    local types = root:CreateButton(L["Pet Type"])
    for i = 1, C_PetJournal.GetNumPetTypes() do
        types:CreateCheckbox(_G["BATTLE_PET_NAME_" .. i], function() return state.types and state.types[i] end,
            function() state.types = state.types or {} state.types[i] = not state.types[i] or nil Tab:Rebuild() end)
    end
    root:CreateTitle(L["Sort by"])
    radio(NAME, "sort", "name")
    radio(LEVEL, "sort", "level")
    radio(RARITY, "sort", "rarity")
    radio(L["Chance"], "sort", "chance")
    root:CreateDivider()
    local bulk = root:CreateButton(L["Set weight for all visible"])
    for _, lvl in ipairs(ns.Weights.LEVELS) do
        bulk:CreateButton(lvl.label, function()
            local ids = ns.PetGridModel.Filter(set, state)
            if #ids > 20 then
                StaticPopup_Show("PETCALL_CONFIRM", format(L["Set %s for %d pets?"], lvl.label, #ids), nil,
                    { onAccept = function() set:SetPetWeights(ids, lvl.value) end })
            else
                set:SetPetWeights(ids, lvl.value)
            end
        end)
    end
end

ns.UI.Main:RegisterTab("pets", L["Pets"], function(p)
    page = p
    local search = ns.Widgets.Search(page, function(text) state.search = strlower(text) Tab:Rebuild() end)
    search:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 6, -4)
    search:SetWidth(200)

    local filterBtn = CreateFrame("DropdownButton", nil, page, "WowStyle1FilterDropdownTemplate")
    filterBtn:SetPoint("LEFT", search, "RIGHT", 8, 0)
    filterBtn:SetupMenu(filterMenu)
    ns.Skin:Apply("Dropdown", filterBtn)

    local clear = ns.Widgets.Button(page, L["Clear"], function()
        StaticPopup_Show("PETCALL_CONFIRM", L["Reset all pet weights in this set?"], nil,
            { onAccept = function() set:ClearPetWeights() end })
    end)
    clear:SetPoint("TOPRIGHT", -6, -2)

    page.defaultWeight = ns.Widgets.Dropdown(page, 150, function(owner, root)
        for _, lvl in ipairs(ns.Weights.LEVELS) do
            root:CreateRadio(lvl.label, function() return set and ns.Weights.Nearest(set.settings.defaultValue) == lvl.value end,
                function() set:SetDefaultWeight(lvl.value) end)
        end
    end)
    page.defaultWeight:SetPoint("RIGHT", clear, "LEFT", -8, 0)
    local dwLabel = ns.Widgets.Label(page, L["Other pets:"])
    dwLabel:SetPoint("RIGHT", page.defaultWeight, "LEFT", -6, 0)

    -- auto-rule chips row is filled by Task 11 (UI/AutoRulesBar.lua) at page.rulesBar
    page.rulesBar = CreateFrame("Frame", nil, page)
    page.rulesBar:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", search, "BOTTOMLEFT", -4, -6)
    page.rulesBar:SetPoint("RIGHT", -6, 0)
    page.rulesBar:SetHeight(24)

    local previewPanel = ns.UI.Preview:Attach(page)
    previewPanel:SetPoint("TOPRIGHT", page.rulesBar, "BOTTOMRIGHT", 0, -6)
    previewPanel:SetPoint("BOTTOMRIGHT", 0, 0)

    grid = CreateFrame("Frame", nil, page)
    grid:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", page.rulesBar, "BOTTOMLEFT", 0, -6)
    scrollBox = CreateFrame("Frame", nil, grid, "WowScrollBoxList")
    scrollBox:SetAllPoints()
    scrollBar = CreateFrame("EventFrame", nil, page, "MinimalScrollBar")
    scrollBar:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", grid, "TOPRIGHT", 4, 0)
    scrollBar:SetPoint("BOTTOMLEFT", grid, "BOTTOMRIGHT", 4, 0)
    local view = CreateScrollBoxListLinearView()
    view:SetElementExtent(TILE_H)
    view:SetElementInitializer("Frame", initRow)
    ScrollUtil.InitScrollBoxListWithScrollBar(scrollBox, scrollBar, view)
    ns.Skin:Apply("ScrollBar", scrollBar)

    emptyText = ns.Widgets.Label(grid, "", "GameFontDisableLarge")
    emptyText:SetPoint("CENTER")
    page.openJournal = ns.Widgets.Button(grid, L["Open Pet Journal"], function() ToggleCollectionsJournal(2) end)
    page.openJournal:SetPoint("TOP", emptyText, "BOTTOM", 0, -10)
    page.openJournal:Hide()

    page:SetScript("OnShow", layout)
    return Tab
end, 1)

local rebuildSoon = ns.Debounce(0.05, function() Tab:Rebuild() end)
ns:RegisterCallback(Tab, "PetsChanged", rebuildSoon)
ns:RegisterCallback(Tab, "SetsChanged", function(_, name) if set and (not name or name == set.name) then rebuildSoon() end end)
ns:RegisterCallback(Tab, "WindowResized", function() if page and page:IsVisible() then layout() end end)
```

> `ScrollBoxListLinearView` z `SetElementExtent` i typem ramki `"Frame"` nie wymaga szablonu XML; wiersz zawiera `columns` kafelków. Zmiana liczby kolumn przebudowuje dostawcę danych (`Rebuild`), a pula ramek ponownie inicjalizuje wiersze. Kafelki nadmiarowe są chowane w `initRow`.

Klucze do `Locales/enUS.lua`: `Pets`, `Summon`, `Summon now`, `Set favorite`, `Remove favorite`, `No pet selected`, `Chance in this set: %.1f%%`, `You have no pets yet.`, `No pets match "%s".`, `Open Pet Journal`, `Show`, `Selected`, `Unselected`, `Favorites only`, `Sort by`, `Chance`, `Set weight for all visible`, `Set %s for %d pets?`, `Clear`, `Reset all pet weights in this set?`, `Other pets:`, `auto`.

`ns:SummonPet` powstaje w Task 15 — do tego czasu w `UI/PetsTab.lua` nie dodawaj zastępstw: przyciski „Summon” zadziałają po Task 15 (w grze przed Task 15 klik w nie zgłosi błąd — to oczekiwane w trakcie prac, sprawdzane w Task 15).

- [ ] **Step 7: Weryfikacja w grze**
  1. `/pcall` → zakładka „Pety”: siatka wszystkich petów z ikonami w ramkach rzadkości; przewijanie płynne przy ~1500 petach.
  2. Klik przełącza Normalna ↔ Nigdy (kafelek przygasa); PPM i Ctrl+klik otwierają menu wag; znaczek zmienia się od razu.
  3. Wyszukiwanie po nazwie i własnej nazwie; filtr (wybrane/niewybrane/typy/ulubione/sortowanie); „Ustaw wagę dla wszystkich widocznych” z potwierdzeniem przy > 20.
  4. Panel podglądu: model obraca się sam i za myszą, kółko przybliża, suwak zmienia wagę, szansa w %.
  5. Zwężenie okna < 760 px → panel znika, podgląd pojawia się jako dymek przy kafelku.
  6. Zero błędów Lua (poza przyciskiem „Przywołaj” — patrz uwaga).

### Task 11: Reguły automatyczne w UI (chipy)

**Files:**
- Create: `UI/AutoRulesBar.lua`
- Modify: `PetCall.toc` (po `UI\PetsTab.lua`: `UI\AutoRulesBar.lua`), `Locales/enUS.lua`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `set.settings.filter`, `ns.AutoRules.*` (Task 7), `set:FiltersChanged()`, `page.rulesBar` z Task 10.
- Produces: `ns.UI.AutoRulesBar:Refresh(set)`; edytor chipa (okno `Panel` przy chipie).

- [ ] **Step 1: Implementacja** — `UI/AutoRulesBar.lua`:

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
local CreateFrame = CreateFrame
ns.UI = ns.UI or {}
local Bar = {}
ns.UI.AutoRulesBar = Bar

local bar, set, chips, editor = nil, nil, {}, nil

local function list() return set.settings.filter end
local function commit() set:FiltersChanged() Bar:Refresh(set) end

local function chipText(chip)
    local text = chip.legacy and L["Complex rule (previous version)"] or ns.AutoRules.Describe(chip.entry)
    return format("%s → %s", text, ns.Weights.Label(ns.AutoRules.Weight(chip.entry)))
end

local function closeEditor() if editor then editor:Hide() end end

local function fieldControl(parent, leaf, field)
    if field.kind == "multiChoice" then
        return ns.Widgets.Dropdown(parent, 160, function(owner, root)
            for _, v in ipairs(field.values()) do
                root:CreateCheckbox(v.label, function()
                    for _, x in ipairs(leaf.petTypes or { tonumber(leaf.petType) }) do if x == v.value then return true end end
                end, function()
                    local cur = leaf.petTypes or { tonumber(leaf.petType) }
                    local out, found = {}, false
                    for _, x in ipairs(cur) do if x == v.value then found = true else out[#out + 1] = x end end
                    if not found then out[#out + 1] = v.value end
                    if #out > 0 then ns.AutoRules.SetField(leaf, "petTypes", out) commit() end
                end)
            end
        end)
    elseif field.kind == "operator" then
        return ns.Widgets.Dropdown(parent, 60, function(owner, root)
            for _, op in ipairs(ns.AutoRules.OPERATORS) do
                root:CreateRadio(op, function() return leaf.operator == op end, function() ns.AutoRules.SetField(leaf, "operator", op) commit() end)
            end
        end)
    elseif field.kind == "number" then
        local eb = CreateFrame("EditBox", nil, parent, "InputBoxTemplate")
        eb:SetSize(40, 20)
        eb:SetAutoFocus(false)
        eb:SetNumeric(true)
        eb:SetText(tostring(leaf[field.key] or ""))
        eb:SetScript("OnEnterPressed", function(self)
            local n = math.max(field.min, math.min(field.max, tonumber(self:GetText()) or field.min))
            ns.AutoRules.SetField(leaf, field.key, tostring(n))
            self:ClearFocus()
            commit()
        end)
        ns.Skin:Apply("EditBox", eb)
        return eb
    end
end

local function openEditor(anchor, chip)
    editor = editor or CreateFrame("Frame", "PetCallRuleEditor", UIParent, "DialogBoxFrame")
    editor:SetFrameStrata("DIALOG")
    editor:SetSize(420, 120)
    editor:ClearAllPoints()
    editor:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", anchor, "BOTTOMLEFT", 0, -4)
    if editor.content then editor.content:Hide() end
    local c = CreateFrame("Frame", nil, editor)
    c:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 12, -12)
    c:SetPoint("BOTTOMRIGHT", -12, 40)
    editor.content = c
    ns.Skin:Apply("Panel", editor)
    local y = 0
    if chip.legacy then
        local t = ns.Widgets.Label(c, ns.AutoRules.Describe(chip.entry))
        t:SetPoint("TOPLEFT")
        t:SetPoint("RIGHT")
        t:SetWordWrap(true)
        local simplify = ns.Widgets.Button(c, L["Simplify"], function()
            if ns.AutoRules.Simplify(list(), chip.index) then commit() closeEditor() end
        end)
        simplify:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", t, "BOTTOMLEFT", 0, -6)
    else
        for ci, leaf in ipairs(chip.criteria) do
            local row = CreateFrame("Frame", nil, c)
            row:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 0, y)
            row:SetPoint("RIGHT")
            row:SetHeight(26)
            local label = ns.Widgets.Label(row, ns.AutoRules.Label(leaf[1]))
            label:SetPoint("LEFT")
            label:SetWidth(90)
            local prev = label
            for _, def in ipairs(ns.AutoRules.Types()) do
                if def.key == leaf[1] then
                    for _, field in ipairs(def.fields) do
                        local ctl = fieldControl(row, leaf, field)
                        ctl:SetPoint("LEFT", prev, "RIGHT", 6, 0)
                        prev = ctl
                    end
                end
            end
            local remove = ns.Widgets.Button(row, "×", function()
                ns.AutoRules.RemoveCriterion(list(), chip.index, ci) commit() closeEditor()
            end)
            remove:SetWidth(24)
            remove:SetPoint("RIGHT")
            y = y - 28
        end
        local match = ns.Widgets.Dropdown(c, 140, function(owner, root)
            root:CreateRadio(L["All criteria"], function() return chip.match == "all" end, function() ns.AutoRules.SetMatch(list(), chip.index, "all") commit() end)
            root:CreateRadio(L["Any criterion"], function() return chip.match == "any" end, function() ns.AutoRules.SetMatch(list(), chip.index, "any") commit() end)
        end)
        match:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 0, y - 4)
        local add = ns.Widgets.Dropdown(c, 140, function(owner, root)
            for _, def in ipairs(ns.AutoRules.Types()) do
                root:CreateButton(def.label, function() ns.AutoRules.AddCriterion(list(), chip.index, def.key) commit() closeEditor() end)
            end
        end)
        add:SetDefaultText(L["+ Criterion"])
        add:SetPoint("LEFT", match, "RIGHT", 8, 0)
        y = y - 30
    end
    local weight = ns.Widgets.Dropdown(c, 160, function(owner, root)
        for _, lvl in ipairs(ns.Weights.LEVELS) do
            root:CreateRadio(lvl.label, function() return ns.Weights.Nearest(ns.AutoRules.Weight(chip.entry)) == lvl.value end,
                function() ns.AutoRules.SetChipWeight(list(), chip.index, lvl.value) commit() end)
        end
    end)
    weight:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 0, y - 4)
    editor:SetHeight(-y + 90)
    editor:Show()
end

local function chipButton(i)
    local b = chips[i]
    if b then return b end
    b = CreateFrame("Button", nil, bar, "UIPanelButtonTemplate")
    b:SetHeight(20)
    b.remove = CreateFrame("Button", nil, b, "UIPanelCloseButtonNoScripts")
    b.remove:SetSize(16, 16)
    b.remove:SetPoint("RIGHT", -2, 0)
    ns.Skin:Apply("Button", b)
    chips[i] = b
    return b
end

function Bar:Refresh(newSet)
    set = newSet
    bar = bar or ns.UI.PetsTabRulesBar
    if not bar or not set then return end
    closeEditor()
    local data = set:GetAutoRules()
    local label = bar.label or ns.Widgets.Label(bar, L["Auto rules:"], "GameFontNormalSmall")
    bar.label = label
    label:SetPoint("LEFT")
    local prev = label
    for i, chip in ipairs(data) do
        local b = chipButton(i)
        b:SetText(chipText(chip))
        b:SetWidth(math.min(260, b:GetFontString():GetStringWidth() + 36))
        b:ClearAllPoints()
        b:SetPoint("LEFT", prev, "RIGHT", 6, 0)
        b:SetScript("OnClick", function(self) openEditor(self, chip) end)
        b.remove:SetScript("OnClick", function() ns.AutoRules.RemoveChip(list(), chip.index) commit() end)
        ns.Widgets.Tooltip(b, chipText(chip))
        b:Show()
        prev = b
    end
    for i = #data + 1, #chips do chips[i]:Hide() end
    bar.add = bar.add or ns.Widgets.Dropdown(bar, 110, function(owner, root)
        for _, def in ipairs(ns.AutoRules.Types()) do
            root:CreateButton(def.label, function() ns.AutoRules.AddChip(list(), def.key, ns.Weights.NORMAL) commit() end)
        end
    end)
    bar.add:SetDefaultText(L["+ Rule"])
    bar.add:ClearAllPoints()
    bar.add:SetPoint("LEFT", prev, "RIGHT", 8, 0)
end
```

W `UI/PetsTab.lua` w fabryce zakładki, po utworzeniu `page.rulesBar`, dodaj `ns.UI.PetsTabRulesBar = page.rulesBar`.

> Chipy, które nie mieszczą się w szerokości, są ucinane przez `SetClipsChildren(true)` na `bar` — ustaw to w fabryce zakładki (`page.rulesBar:SetClipsChildren(true)`); pełna lista jest w dymku każdego chipa, a przewijanie paska nie jest potrzebne przy typowej liczbie reguł (1–4).

Klucze do `Locales/enUS.lua`: `Complex rule (previous version)`, `Simplify`, `All criteria`, `Any criterion`, `+ Criterion`, `+ Rule`, `Auto rules:`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Weryfikacja w grze**
  1. Zestaw z filtrem z 1.0.x (np. `{ "favorites", 64 }`) pokazuje chip „Ulubione → Extra High Priority”.
  2. „+ Reguła” → Ulubione → chip; ulubione pety dostają znaczek `auto` (gdy ich ręczna waga jest domyślna).
  3. Edycja: dodanie kryterium „Poziom ≥ 25”, przełączenie „Wszystkie/Dowolne”, zmiana wagi, usunięcie kryterium — siatka i szanse odświeżają się od razu.
  4. Profil z głębokim drzewem filtrów → chip „Reguła złożona (poprzednia wersja)” z opisem; „Uprość” działa tylko gdy możliwe.
- [ ] **Step 3: Commit** — `git add UI/AutoRulesBar.lua UI/PetsTab.lua PetCall.toc Locales/enUS.lua && git commit -m "feat(ui): auto rule chips replacing the filters tab"`

## Kamień milowy 2 — gotowość, warunki, przywoływanie, zestawy w UI

### Task 12: Ready — warunki gotowości i powody wstrzymania

**Files:**
- Create: `Core/Ready.lua`, `tests/ready_spec.lua`
- Modify: `PetCall.toc` (po `Core\Sets.lua`, przed `Core\Commands.lua`: `Core\Ready.lua`), `tests/run.lua`, `Locales/enUS.lua`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ns.db.profile`, `ns.PetCache:NumPets()`, `ns:IsAutoSummon()` (Task 15; do tego czasu `ns.db.profile.enable`), `ns.Summon:SummonRandom()` (Task 15).
- Produces (`ns.Ready`):
  - `:SetReady(name, value)`, `:EnableCheck(name, enabled, dismiss)`, `:UpdateChecks()`, `:QueueResummon(nowait)`, `:IsReady() → bool`
  - `:GetBlockers() → { {key, label, detail} }` — tylko włączone i niespełnione warunki, w stałej kolejności `BLOCKER_ORDER`; dla wpisów przedmiotów specjalnych (`item:…`, `quest:…`) etykieta z `ns.SpecialItems:LocalizeString(key)` (Task 18; do tego czasu sam klucz)
  - `:DumpDebugState()` (dla `/pcall debugstate`)
  - Callback `ns:Fire("ReadyChanged")` przy każdej zmianie zbioru niespełnionych warunków.
- Zachowane z 1.0.x: wszystkie nazwy warunków (`addon_ready combat in_battleground in_pve resting stealthed invisible flying near_pew alive camo casting have_pets barbar thunderisle_saurok sitting feigned looting vehicle addon_enabled`), ich znaczenie, wait timer z resetem przy ruchu/upadku/braku kontroli, blokada 1,5 s po rzuceniu czaru, 3 s blokady ruchu po usiądnięciu, wykrywanie ławki po błędzie „musisz stać”, wykrywanie AFK, odpytywanie co 0,5 s (lot, zniknięcie peta).
- Zmiany: zdarzenia `UNIT_*` tylko dla `player`; `UNIT_AURA` pomija aktualizacje bez dodanych/usuniętych aur; błąd stania rozpoznawany po tekście (`ERR_MUST_BE_STANDING` / `SPELL_FAILED_NOT_STANDING`), nie po numerze.

- [ ] **Step 1: Test** — `tests/ready_spec.lua`:

```lua
local W = dofile("tests/wowstub.lua")
describe("Ready", function()
    local ns, summons
    before_each(function()
        W.reset()
        W.world.pets = { a = { species = 1, name = "A" } }
        ns = W.load()
        summons = 0
        W.login()
        W.advance(1)
        ns.Summon = ns.Summon or {}
        ns.Summon.SummonRandom = function() summons = summons + 1 return true end
    end)

    it("reports blockers with labels", function()
        W.world.combat = true
        W.fire("PLAYER_REGEN_DISABLED")
        local keys = {}
        for _, b in ipairs(ns.Ready:GetBlockers()) do keys[#keys + 1] = b.key end
        assert.is_true(table.concat(keys, ","):find("combat") ~= nil)
        assert.equal("In combat", ns.Ready:GetBlockers()[1].label)
    end)

    it("summons after the wait timer when nothing blocks", function()
        W.fire("PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED")
        W.advance(ns.db.profile.waitTimer + 1)
        assert.is_true(summons >= 1)
    end)

    it("ignores aura updates of other units and no-op aura updates", function()
        local n = 0
        ns:RegisterCallback({}, "ReadyChanged", function() n = n + 1 end)
        W.world.auras[5384] = true                 -- feign death
        W.fire("UNIT_AURA", "party1", { isFullUpdate = true })
        assert.equal(0, n)
        W.fire("UNIT_AURA", "player", {})           -- nothing added/removed
        assert.equal(0, n)
        W.fire("UNIT_AURA", "player", { addedAuras = { {} } })
        assert.equal(1, n)
    end)

    it("detects sitting on a bench from the standing error text", function()
        ns.Ready._last_summon_time = W.world.time
        W.fire("UI_ERROR_MESSAGE", 999, ERR_MUST_BE_STANDING)
        local found = false
        for _, b in ipairs(ns.Ready:GetBlockers()) do if b.key == "sitting" then found = true end end
        assert.is_true(found)
    end)

    it("captures AFK messages through C_ChatInfo", function()
        C_ChatInfo.SendChatMessage("brb", "AFK")
        assert.equal("brb", ns.Ready.afk_message)
    end)

    it("blocks with no pets", function()
        W.world.pets = {}
        ns.PetCache:Rescan()
        local found = false
        for _, b in ipairs(ns.Ready:GetBlockers()) do if b.key == "have_pets" then found = true end end
        assert.is_true(found)
    end)
end)
```

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
local GetTime, HasFullControl, InCombatLockdown, IsFalling, IsFlying, IsInInstance, IsResting, IsStealthed,
    UnitCastingInfo, UnitChannelInfo, UnitIsDeadOrGhost, UnitInVehicle, IsMounted, UnitIsAFK =
    GetTime, HasFullControl, InCombatLockdown, IsFalling, IsFlying, IsInInstance, IsResting, IsStealthed,
    UnitCastingInfo, UnitChannelInfo, UnitIsDeadOrGhost, UnitInVehicle, IsMounted, UnitIsAFK
local pairs, ipairs, next, sort, tconcat, tinsert = pairs, ipairs, next, table.sort, table.concat, table.insert

local Ready = ns:NewModule("Ready")
ns.Ready = Ready

local INVIS_SPELLS = { 66, 11392, 3680 }
local CAMO_SPELLS = { 198783, 199483 }
local FOOD_SPELLS = { 430, 433, 167152, 160598, 160599 }

local BLOCKER_ORDER = {
    "addon_enabled", "have_pets", "combat", "alive", "flying", "vehicle", "stealthed", "invisible", "camo",
    "feigned", "sitting", "casting", "looting", "barbar", "thunderisle_saurok", "in_battleground", "in_pve",
    "resting", "near_pew", "addon_ready",
}
local LABELS = {
    addon_enabled = L["Auto-summon is off"], have_pets = L["No pets"], combat = L["In combat"],
    alive = L["Dead"], flying = L["Flying"], vehicle = L["In a vehicle"], stealthed = L["Stealthed"],
    invisible = L["Invisible"], camo = L["Camouflaged"], feigned = L["Feigning death"], sitting = L["Sitting"],
    casting = L["Casting"], looting = L["Looting"], barbar = L["At the barber"], thunderisle_saurok = L["Transformed"],
    in_battleground = L["In a battleground or arena"], in_pve = L["In a dungeon or raid"],
    resting = L["Outside a city"], near_pew = L["Waiting for a zone change"], addon_ready = L["Loading"],
}

local function PlayerHasAura(id) return C_UnitAuras.GetPlayerAuraBySpellID(id) ~= nil end
local function PlayerHasAuraInList(list)
    for _, id in ipairs(list) do if PlayerHasAura(id) then return true end end
    return false
end

function Ready:OnInitialize()
    self.is_true, self.is_false, self.is_irrelevant, self.dismiss_on = {}, { addon_ready = true }, {}, {}
    self.last_spell_sent = 0

    self:RegisterEvent("COMPANION_UPDATE")
    self:RegisterEvent("UPDATE_STEALTH")
    self:RegisterEvent("PLAYER_REGEN_DISABLED")
    self:RegisterEvent("PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED")
    self:RegisterEvent("BARBER_SHOP_CLOSE")
    self:RegisterEvent("BARBER_SHOP_OPEN")
    self:RegisterEvent("LOOT_OPENED")
    self:RegisterEvent("LOOT_CLOSED")
    self:RegisterEvent("PLAYER_ALIVE")
    self:RegisterEvent("PLAYER_DEAD")
    self:RegisterEvent("PLAYER_UNGHOST")
    self:RegisterEvent("CONFIRM_XP_LOSS")
    self:RegisterEvent("ZONE_CHANGED_NEW_AREA")
    self:RegisterEvent("PLAYER_STARTED_MOVING")
    self:RegisterEvent("PLAYER_FLAGS_CHANGED")
    self:RegisterEvent("UI_ERROR_MESSAGE")
    self:RegisterUnitEvent("UNIT_AURA", "player")
    self:RegisterUnitEvent("UNIT_SPELLCAST_SENT", "player")
    for _, e in ipairs({ "UNIT_SPELLCAST_CHANNEL_START", "UNIT_SPELLCAST_CHANNEL_STOP", "UNIT_SPELLCAST_CHANNEL_UPDATE",
        "UNIT_SPELLCAST_FAILED", "UNIT_SPELLCAST_START", "UNIT_SPELLCAST_STOP", "UNIT_SPELLCAST_SUCCEEDED" }) do
        self:RegisterUnitEvent(e, "player", "SpellCastUpdate")
    end
    self:RegisterUnitEvent("UNIT_ENTERED_VEHICLE", "player")
    self:RegisterUnitEvent("UNIT_EXITED_VEHICLE", "player")

    self:UpdateChecks()
    ns:RegisterCallback(self, "ProfileChanged", function() Ready:UpdateChecks() end)
    ns:RegisterCallback(self, "PetsChanged", function() Ready:PetListUpdated() end)
    ns:RegisterCallback(self, "AutoSummonChanged", function(_, v) Ready:SetReady("addon_enabled", v) end)
end

function Ready:OnEnable()
    self:PetListUpdated()
    self.ticker = ns:NewTicker(0.5, function() Ready:FrequentCheck() end)
    self:SetReady("addon_enabled", ns.db.profile.enable)
    self:SetReady("combat", not InCombatLockdown())
    self:SetReady("resting", IsResting())
    self:SetReady("alive", not UnitIsDeadOrGhost("player"))
    self:SetReady("in_battleground", not ns.InBattlegroundOrArena())
    self:SetReady("vehicle", not UnitInVehicle("player"))
    self:SetReady("flying", not IsFlying())
    self:UNIT_AURA(nil, "player")
    self:UPDATE_STEALTH()
    self:RegisterEvent("PLAYER_MOUNT_DISPLAY_CHANGED", "CheckFlying")
    self:SetReady("addon_ready", true)
    self:PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD()
    self:RegisterEvent("PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD")

    hooksecurefunc("JumpOrAscendStart", function() Ready:SetReady("sitting", true) end)
    hooksecurefunc("SitStandOrDescendStart", function()
        if not IsFlying() and not IsMounted() then
            Ready:ToggleReady("sitting")
            Ready._sit_move_lock = Ready.is_false.sitting and (GetTime() + 3) or nil
        end
    end)
    local function onSendChat(msg, chatType)
        if chatType == "AFK" then Ready.afk_message = msg end
    end
    if C_ChatInfo and C_ChatInfo.SendChatMessage then hooksecurefunc(C_ChatInfo, "SendChatMessage", onSendChat) end
    if type(SendChatMessage) == "function" then hooksecurefunc("SendChatMessage", onSendChat) end
end

function ns.InBattlegroundOrArena()
    local _, t = IsInInstance()
    return t == "pvp" or t == "arena"
end

function ns.InInstanceOrRaid()
    local _, t = IsInInstance()
    return t == "party" or t == "raid"
end

function Ready:UpdateChecks()
    local p = ns.db.profile
    self:EnableCheck("addon_ready", true)
    self:EnableCheck("combat", true)
    self:EnableCheck("in_battleground", p.dismissInBattleground or not p.enableInBattleground, p.dismissInBattleground)
    self:EnableCheck("in_pve", p.dismissInPVE or not p.enableInPVE, p.dismissInPVE)
    self:EnableCheck("resting", p.disableOutsideCities)
    self:EnableCheck("stealthed", true, p.dismissWhenStealthed)
    self:EnableCheck("invisible", true, p.dismissWhenStealthed)
    self:EnableCheck("flying", true)
    self:EnableCheck("near_pew", p.nearPEWOnly)
    self:EnableCheck("alive", true)
    self:EnableCheck("camo", true, p.dismissWhenStealthed)
    self:EnableCheck("casting", true)
    self:EnableCheck("have_pets", true)
    self:EnableCheck("barbar", true)
    self:EnableCheck("thunderisle_saurok", true)
    self:EnableCheck("sitting", true)
    self:EnableCheck("feigned", true, p.dismissWhenStealthed)
    self:EnableCheck("looting", true)
    self:EnableCheck("vehicle", true)
    self:EnableCheck("addon_enabled", true)
    if ns.SpecialItems then ns.SpecialItems:UpdateReadyEnabled() end
    self:_Changed()
    self:_CheckSummon()
end

function Ready:EnableCheck(name, enabled, dismiss)
    local v = true
    if self.is_irrelevant[name] ~= nil then
        v = self.is_irrelevant[name]
        self.is_irrelevant[name] = nil
    elseif self.is_true[name] then
        self.is_true[name] = nil
    elseif self.is_false[name] then
        v = false
        self.is_false[name] = nil
    end
    if enabled then
        if v then self.is_true[name] = true else self.is_false[name] = true end
        self.dismiss_on[name] = dismiss and true or nil
    else
        self.is_irrelevant[name] = v
    end
end

function Ready:_Changed() ns:Fire("ReadyChanged") end

function Ready:SetReady(name, value)
    if value then
        if self.is_false[name] then
            self.is_false[name], self.is_true[name] = nil, true
            self:_Changed()
            self:_CheckSummon()
            return
        elseif self.is_true[name] then
            return
        end
    else
        if self.is_true[name] then
            self.is_true[name], self.is_false[name] = nil, true
            self:_Changed()
            self:_CheckUn(name)
            return
        elseif self.is_false[name] then
            return
        end
    end
    self.is_irrelevant[name] = not not value
end

function Ready:ToggleReady(name) self:SetReady(name, self.is_false[name] and true or false) end

function Ready:IsReady() return next(self.is_false) == nil end

function Ready:LastCheck()
    return not IsFalling() and HasFullControl() and GetTime() - self.last_spell_sent > 1.5
end

function Ready:_CheckSummon(waitFinished)
    if next(self.is_false) then return end
    if ns:GetCurrentPet() and not self.force then return end
    if waitFinished then
        if self:LastCheck() then
            self.force = false
            self._last_summon_time = GetTime()
            if ns.Summon and ns.Summon.SummonRandom then ns.Summon:SummonRandom() end
            ns:After(2, function() Ready:_CheckSummon() end)
            return
        end
    elseif self.wait_timer then
        return
    end
    self.wait_counter = 0
    self.wait_timer = ns:NewTicker(0.5, function() Ready:WaitTimerProgress() end)
end

function Ready:_CheckUn(name)
    if self.dismiss_on[name] and ns.db.profile.enable and not InCombatLockdown() and ns.Summon then
        ns.Summon:Dismiss(false, true)
    end
    if self.wait_timer then
        self.wait_timer:Cancel()
        self.wait_timer = nil
    end
end

function Ready:WaitTimerProgress()
    self.wait_counter = self.wait_counter + 0.5
    if self.wait_counter >= ns.db.profile.waitTimer then
        self.wait_timer:Cancel()
        self.wait_timer = nil
        self:_CheckSummon(true)
    elseif not self:LastCheck() then
        self.wait_counter = 0
    end
end

function Ready:QueueResummon(nowait)
    self.force = true
    self:_CheckSummon(nowait)
end

function Ready:PetListUpdated() self:SetReady("have_pets", ns.PetCache:NumPets() > 0) end
function Ready:CheckFlying() self:SetReady("flying", not IsFlying()) end

function Ready:FrequentCheck()
    self:SetReady("flying", not IsFlying())
    local petID = ns:GetCurrentPet()
    if not petID and petID ~= self._lastPet then self:_CheckSummon() end
    self._lastPet = petID
end

function Ready:PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD()
    local _, instanceType = IsInInstance()
    self:SetReady("in_pve", not (IsInGroup() and (instanceType == "party" or instanceType == "raid" or instanceType == "scenario")))
    if not self.near_pew_handle then
        self:SetReady("near_pew", true)
        self.near_pew_handle = ns:After(6 + (ns.db.profile.waitTimer or 1), function()
            Ready:SetReady("near_pew", false)
            Ready.near_pew_handle = nil
        end)
    end
end

function Ready:COMPANION_UPDATE(_, ctype) if ctype == "CRITTER" then self:_CheckSummon() end end
function Ready:BARBER_SHOP_OPEN() self:SetReady("barbar", false) end
function Ready:BARBER_SHOP_CLOSE() self:SetReady("barbar", true) end
function Ready:LOOT_OPENED() self:SetReady("looting", false) end
function Ready:LOOT_CLOSED() self:SetReady("looting", true) end
function Ready:PLAYER_ALIVE() self:SetReady("alive", not UnitIsDeadOrGhost("player")) end
function Ready:PLAYER_DEAD() self:SetReady("alive", false) end
function Ready:PLAYER_UNGHOST() self:SetReady("alive", true) end
function Ready:CONFIRM_XP_LOSS() self:SetReady("alive", true) end
function Ready:PLAYER_REGEN_DISABLED() self:SetReady("combat", false) end
function Ready:PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED() self:SetReady("combat", true) end
function Ready:ZONE_CHANGED_NEW_AREA() self:SetReady("in_battleground", not ns.InBattlegroundOrArena()) end
function Ready:UNIT_ENTERED_VEHICLE() self:SetReady("vehicle", false) end
function Ready:UNIT_EXITED_VEHICLE() self:SetReady("vehicle", true) end

function Ready:UPDATE_STEALTH()
    self:SetReady("stealthed", not (IsStealthed() and not PlayerHasAuraInList(CAMO_SPELLS)))
end

function Ready:UNIT_AURA(_, unit, info)
    if unit ~= "player" then return end
    if info and not info.isFullUpdate and not info.addedAuras and not info.removedAuraInstanceIDs then return end
    self:SetReady("invisible", not PlayerHasAuraInList(INVIS_SPELLS))
    self:SetReady("camo", not PlayerHasAuraInList(CAMO_SPELLS))
    self:SetReady("thunderisle_saurok", not PlayerHasAura(136461))
    if PlayerHasAuraInList(FOOD_SPELLS) then self:SetReady("sitting", false) end
    self:SetReady("feigned", not PlayerHasAura(5384))
end

function Ready:UNIT_SPELLCAST_SENT(event, unit)
    self.last_spell_sent = GetTime()
    self:SpellCastUpdate(event, unit)
end

function Ready:SpellCastUpdate()
    self:SetReady("casting", not (UnitCastingInfo("player") or UnitChannelInfo("player")))
end

function Ready:PLAYER_STARTED_MOVING()
    if self._sit_move_lock and GetTime() < self._sit_move_lock then return end
    self._last_summon_time = nil
    self:SetReady("sitting", true)
end

function Ready:UI_ERROR_MESSAGE(_, _, message)
    if (message == ERR_MUST_BE_STANDING or message == SPELL_FAILED_NOT_STANDING)
        and self._last_summon_time and GetTime() - self._last_summon_time < 3 then
        self:SetReady("sitting", false)
    end
end

function Ready:PLAYER_FLAGS_CHANGED()
    self:SetReady("resting", IsResting())
    if UnitIsAFK("player") and self.afk_message == nil then self:SetReady("sitting", false) end
    self.afk_message = nil
end

function Ready:GetBlockers()
    local out, seen = {}, {}
    for _, key in ipairs(BLOCKER_ORDER) do
        if self.is_false[key] then
            out[#out + 1] = { key = key, label = LABELS[key] }
            seen[key] = true
        end
    end
    for key in pairs(self.is_false) do
        if not seen[key] then
            local name = ns.SpecialItems and ns.SpecialItems:LocalizeString(key) or key
            out[#out + 1] = { key = key, label = format(L["Using %s"], name) }
        end
    end
    return out
end

function Ready:DumpDebugState()
    local ready, notReady = {}, {}
    for k in pairs(self.is_true) do ready[#ready + 1] = k end
    for k in pairs(self.is_false) do notReady[#notReady + 1] = k end
    sort(ready) sort(notReady)
    ns:Printf("Ready: %s", tconcat(ready, " "))
    ns:Printf("Not Ready: %s", tconcat(notReady, " "))
end
```

### Task 13: Silnik warunków i typy warunków

**Files:**
- Create: `Core/Conditions.lua`, `Core/ConditionTypes.lua`, `tests/conditions_spec.lua`
- Modify: `PetCall.toc` (przed `Core\Sets.lua`: `Core\Conditions.lua`, `Core\ConditionTypes.lua`), `tests/run.lua`, `Locales/enUS.lua`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ns.Events`, `ns:NewTicker`, `ns:Print`.
- Produces (`ns.Conditions`):
  - `RegisterType(def)`; `def = { key, label, group ("place"|"party"|"time"|"advanced"), fields = {…}, events = {…}, tick = seconds|nil, new() → entry, check(entry, ctx) → bool, reason(entry, ctx) → string, describe(entry) → string, merge(a, b) → entry|nil }`
  - `Types() → {def}` (w kolejności rejestracji), `GetType(key)`
  - `Context() → ctx` (stan świata jednorazowo na przebieg: `zone, subzone, mapChain{[mapID]=true}, inInstance, instanceType, delve, inGroup, inRaid, class, spec, name, minutes`)
  - `Eval(entry, ctx) → bool, reason`, `EvalList(list, ctx) → bool` (AND; pusta lista → `true`)
  - `ToModel(list) → { groups = { { rows = {entry} } } }`; `FromModel(model) → list`
  - `Normalize(list)` — w miejscu, tylko przekształcenia o identycznym znaczeniu: spłaszczenie `AND` (bez `negate`) do listy nadrzędnej; `OR` (bez `negate`) z liśćmi jednego scalanego typu → jeden liść (`def.merge`)
  - `Simplify(node) → {entry}|nil`
  - `IsLegacyNode(entry) → bool` (węzeł `AND`/`OR` w wierszu)
  - `Describe(entry) → string`
  - `NewWatcher(list, onChange) → w`; `w.value`, `w.states[entry] = { value, reason }`, `w:Refresh()`, `w:Rebuild()`, `w:Free()`
  - `Compile(entry) → fn|nil, err` (luacode; z cache po tekście kodu), `CheckLua(code) → ok, resultOrErr` (dla przycisku „Sprawdź”)
- Typy (`Core/ConditionTypes.lua`) i format zapisu (addytywny względem 1.0.x):

| `[1]` | Pola 1.0.x | Pola nowe | Uwagi |
|---|---|---|---|
| `zone` | `zoneName` | `zoneNames = {…}`, `mapIDs = {…}` | spełniony, gdy strefa/podstrefa ∈ nazwy albo mapa gracza lub jej przodek ∈ `mapIDs` |
| `specialLocation` | `loc` (`city`/`battleground`/`instance`) | `locs = {…}` z `city dungeon raid scenario delve pvp world` | `battleground`→`pvp`, `instance`→`dungeon`+`raid` |
| `groupType` | `party`, `raid` | `solo` | raid → `raid`; drużyna (nie raid) → `party`; poza grupą → `solo` |
| `specialization` | `class` (`""`/nil = dowolna), `spec` (−1 = dowolna) | `name` | „Dowolna” klasa działa |
| `timeOfDay` | — | `from`, `to` (minuty 0–1439) | przedział może przechodzić przez północ |
| `macro` | `macro` | — | `SecureCmdOptionParse(macro) ~= nil` |
| `luacode` | `code` | `disabledByImport` | ciało kodu jak w 1.0.x; zwraca funkcję |
| `AND`/`OR` | `items` | — | `negate` dozwolone na każdym wpisie |
