```lua
local W = dofile("tests/wowstub.lua")
local F = dofile("tests/fixtures/PetCall3DB_1_0.lua")
local REF = dofile("tests/fixtures/reference_1_0.lua")

local function deepcopy(t)
    if type(t) ~= "table" then return t end
    local c = {} for k, v in pairs(t) do c[k] = deepcopy(v) end return c
end

describe("Conditions", function()
    local ns, C
    before_each(function()
        W.reset()
        ns = W.load()
        W.login()
        C = ns.Conditions
    end)

    local function eval(e) return (C.Eval(e, C.Context())) end

    it("zone by name, list and map ancestry", function()
        assert.is_true(eval({ "zone", zoneName = "Stormwind City" }))
        assert.is_true(eval({ "zone", zoneName = "Trade District" }))
        assert.is_true(eval({ "zone", zoneNames = { "X", "Stormwind City" } }))
        assert.is_true(eval({ "zone", mapIDs = { 13 } }))           -- continent ancestor of 84
        assert.is_false(eval({ "zone", mapIDs = { 85 } }))
    end)

    it("location types incl. legacy values", function()
        assert.is_true(eval({ "specialLocation", loc = "city" }))
        W.world.instanceType, W.world.resting = "party", false
        assert.is_true(eval({ "specialLocation", loc = "instance" }))
        assert.is_true(eval({ "specialLocation", locs = { "dungeon" } }))
        assert.is_false(eval({ "specialLocation", locs = { "world" } }))
        W.world.instanceType, W.world.delve = "scenario", true
        assert.is_true(eval({ "specialLocation", locs = { "delve" } }))
        assert.is_false(eval({ "specialLocation", locs = { "scenario" } }))
    end)

    it("group honours party/raid/solo", function()
        local e = { "groupType", party = false, raid = true }
        assert.is_false(eval(e))
        W.world.inGroup = true
        assert.is_false(eval(e))
        W.world.inRaid = true
        assert.is_true(eval(e))
        W.world.inGroup, W.world.inRaid = false, false
        assert.is_true(eval({ "groupType", solo = true }))
    end)

    it("specialization with any class and name", function()
        assert.is_true(eval({ "specialization", class = "", spec = -1 }))
        assert.is_true(eval({ "specialization", class = "HUNTER", spec = 1 }))
        assert.is_false(eval({ "specialization", class = "WARRIOR", spec = -1 }))
        assert.is_false(eval({ "specialization", spec = -1, name = "Someone" }))
    end)

    it("time of day across midnight", function()
        W.world.gameHour, W.world.gameMinute = 23, 30
        assert.is_true(eval({ "timeOfDay", from = 22 * 60, to = 6 * 60 }))
        W.world.gameHour = 12
        assert.is_false(eval({ "timeOfDay", from = 22 * 60, to = 6 * 60 }))
    end)

    it("negate and nodes", function()
        assert.is_false(eval({ "zone", zoneName = "Stormwind City", negate = true }))
        assert.is_true(eval({ "OR", items = { { "zone", zoneName = "X" }, { "specialLocation", loc = "city" } } }))
        assert.is_false(eval({ "AND", items = { { "zone", zoneName = "X" }, { "specialLocation", loc = "city" } } }))
        assert.is_true(C.EvalList({}, C.Context()))
    end)

    it("luacode returns the function result, runs setup once, disabled when imported", function()
        local runs = 0
        _G.PC_TEST_COUNT = function() runs = runs + 1 end
        local e = { "luacode", code = "PC_TEST_COUNT()\nreturn function() return false end" }
        assert.is_false(eval(e))
        assert.is_false(eval(e))
        assert.equal(1, runs)
        local ok = C.CheckLua("return function() return true end")
        assert.is_true(ok)
        assert.is_false((C.CheckLua("this is not lua")))
        assert.is_false(eval({ "luacode", code = "return function() return true end", disabledByImport = true }))
    end)

    it("model round trip: one group and OR-of-AND groups", function()
        local list = { { "zone", zoneName = "A" }, { "groupType", party = true, raid = true } }
        local m = C.ToModel(list)
        assert.equal(1, #m.groups)
        assert.same(list, C.FromModel(m))
        local multi = { { "OR", items = { { "AND", items = { { "zone", zoneName = "A" } } }, { "zone", zoneName = "B" } } } }
        local m2 = C.ToModel(multi)
        assert.equal(2, #m2.groups)
        local back = C.FromModel(m2)
        assert.equal("OR", back[1][1])
        assert.equal(2, #back[1].items)
    end)

    it("normalize merges same-type OR and flattens AND", function()
        local list = {
            { "OR", items = { { "zone", zoneName = "A" }, { "zone", zoneName = "B" } } },
            { "AND", items = { { "specialLocation", loc = "city" } } },
        }
        C.Normalize(list)
        assert.equal(2, #list)
        assert.equal("zone", list[1][1])
        assert.same({ "A", "B" }, list[1].zoneNames)
        assert.equal("specialLocation", list[2][1])
    end)

    it("FromModel drops empty groups", function()
        local list = C.FromModel({ groups = { { rows = { { "zone", zoneName = "A" } } }, { rows = {} } } })
        assert.same({ { "zone", zoneName = "A" } }, list)
    end)

    it("simplify refuses meaning-changing rewrites", function()
        assert.is_nil(C.Simplify({ "OR", items = { { "zone", zoneName = "A" }, { "groupType", party = true } } }))
        assert.is_not_nil(C.Simplify({ "AND", items = { { "zone", zoneName = "A" } } }))
    end)

    it("equivalence with 1.0.x over all world states (normalized trees)", function()
        for wi, state in ipairs(F.worlds) do
            W.reset()
            for id, p in pairs(F.pets) do W.world.pets[id] = p end
            for k, v in pairs(state) do W.world[k] = v end
            local ns2 = W.load()
            PetCall3DB = deepcopy(F.db)
            W.login()
            for name, ref in pairs(REF[wi].sets) do
                if name ~= "$Default" then
                    local list = deepcopy(F.db.profiles.Default.sets[name].trigger or {})
                    ns2.Conditions.Normalize(list)
                    assert.equal(ref.value, ns2.Conditions.EvalList(list, ns2.Conditions.Context()), "world " .. wi .. " set " .. name)
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    it("watcher fires onChange on relevant events only", function()
        local list = { { "zone", zoneName = "Dornogal" } }
        local n = 0
        local w = C.NewWatcher(list, function() n = n + 1 end)
        assert.is_false(w.value)
        W.world.zone = "Dornogal"
        W.fire("ZONE_CHANGED_NEW_AREA")
        assert.is_true(w.value)
        assert.equal(1, n)
        W.fire("GROUP_ROSTER_UPDATE")
        assert.equal(1, n)
        w:Free()
        W.world.zone = "X"
        W.fire("ZONE_CHANGED_NEW_AREA")
        assert.equal(1, n)
    end)
end)
```

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
local ipairs, pairs, type, tinsert, loadstring, pcall, setmetatable = ipairs, pairs, type, tinsert, loadstring, pcall, setmetatable

local C = {}
ns.Conditions = C

local types, order = {}, {}

function C.RegisterType(def)
    assert(not types[def.key], "condition type exists: " .. def.key)
    types[def.key] = def
    order[#order + 1] = def
end

function C.Types() return order end
function C.GetType(key) return types[key] end

local function isNode(e) return e[1] == "AND" or e[1] == "OR" end
function C.IsLegacyNode(e) return isNode(e) end

--
-- Context
--

function C.Context()
    local ctx = {}
    ctx.zone, ctx.subzone = GetZoneText(), GetSubZoneText()
    ctx.mapChain = {}
    local mapID = C_Map.GetBestMapForUnit("player")
    local guard = 0
    while mapID and mapID ~= 0 and guard < 20 do
        ctx.mapChain[mapID] = true
        local info = C_Map.GetMapInfo(mapID)
        mapID = info and info.parentMapID
        guard = guard + 1
    end
    ctx.inInstance, ctx.instanceType = IsInInstance()
    ctx.delve = C_PartyInfo and C_PartyInfo.IsDelveInProgress and C_PartyInfo.IsDelveInProgress() or false
    ctx.resting = IsResting()
    ctx.inRaid, ctx.inGroup = IsInRaid(), IsInGroup()
    local _, class = UnitClass("player")
    ctx.class = class
    ctx.spec = C_SpecializationInfo.GetSpecialization()
    ctx.name = UnitName("player")
    local h, m = GetGameTime()
    ctx.minutes = h * 60 + m
    return ctx
end

--
-- Evaluation
--

function C.Eval(e, ctx)
    local result, reason
    if isNode(e) then
        if e[1] == "AND" then
            result = true
            for _, sub in ipairs(e.items or {}) do
                if not C.Eval(sub, ctx) then result = false break end
            end
        else
            result = false
            for _, sub in ipairs(e.items or {}) do
                if C.Eval(sub, ctx) then result = true break end
            end
        end
    else
        local def = types[e[1]]
        if not def then return false, L["Unknown condition"] end
        result = def.check(e, ctx) and true or false
        reason = def.reason and def.reason(e, ctx)
    end
    if e.negate then result = not result end
    return result, reason
end

function C.EvalList(list, ctx)
    for _, e in ipairs(list or {}) do
        if not C.Eval(e, ctx) then return false end
    end
    return true
end

function C.Describe(e)
    local text
    if isNode(e) then
        local parts = {}
        for _, sub in ipairs(e.items or {}) do parts[#parts + 1] = C.Describe(sub) end
        text = "(" .. table.concat(parts, e[1] == "AND" and (" " .. L["and"] .. " ") or (" " .. L["or"] .. " ")) .. ")"
    else
        local def = types[e[1]]
        text = def and def.describe(e) or L["Unknown condition"]
    end
    if e.negate then text = L["not"] .. " " .. text end
    return text
end

--
-- Model
--

function C.ToModel(list)
    list = list or {}
    local groups = {}
    local only = list[1]
    if #list == 1 and only[1] == "OR" and not only.negate then
        for _, item in ipairs(only.items or {}) do
            if item[1] == "AND" and not item.negate then
                groups[#groups + 1] = { rows = item.items or {} }
            else
                groups[#groups + 1] = { rows = { item } }
            end
        end
    else
        groups[1] = { rows = list }
    end
    return { groups = groups }
end

function C.FromModel(model)
    -- an empty group would evaluate to true and make the set always active
    local groups = {}
    for _, g in ipairs(model.groups) do
        if #g.rows > 0 then groups[#groups + 1] = g end
    end
    if #groups <= 1 then
        local out = {}
        for _, r in ipairs(groups[1] and groups[1].rows or {}) do out[#out + 1] = r end
        return out
    end
    local items = {}
    for _, g in ipairs(groups) do
        local rows = {}
        for _, r in ipairs(g.rows) do rows[#rows + 1] = r end
        items[#items + 1] = { "AND", items = rows }
    end
    return { { "OR", items = items } }
end

local function mergeLeaves(items)
    local first = items[1]
    local def = first and not isNode(first) and types[first[1]]
    if not def or not def.merge then return nil end
    local acc = first
    for i = 2, #items do
        local e = items[i]
        if isNode(e) or e[1] ~= first[1] or e.negate or acc.negate then return nil end
        acc = def.merge(acc, e)
        if not acc then return nil end
    end
    return acc
end

function C.Simplify(node)
    if not isNode(node) or node.negate then return nil end
    local items = node.items or {}
    if #items == 1 then return { items[1] } end
    if node[1] == "AND" then
        -- splicing an AND into an AND context never changes the meaning
        local out = {}
        for _, e in ipairs(items) do out[#out + 1] = e end
        return out
    end
    local merged = mergeLeaves(items)
    return merged and { merged } or nil
end

-- Rewrites only where the meaning stays identical: splices AND into the AND
-- context, merges same-type OR leaves, unwraps single-child nodes. A single
-- top-level OR of AND groups is the multi-group shape and stays; its groups
-- are normalized in turn.
function C.Normalize(list)
    local i = 1
    while i <= #list do
        local e = list[i]
        local replacement = isNode(e) and C.Simplify(e)
        if replacement then
            table.remove(list, i)
            for k = #replacement, 1, -1 do tinsert(list, i, replacement[k]) end
        else
            i = i + 1
        end
    end
    local only = list[1]
    if #list == 1 and only[1] == "OR" and not only.negate then
        for _, item in ipairs(only.items or {}) do
            if item[1] == "AND" and not item.negate then C.Normalize(item.items) end
        end
    end
    return list
end

--
-- Lua code
--

local compiled = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })   -- entry -> { code, fn, err, obj }

local function compile(entry)
    local c = compiled[entry]
    if c and c.code == entry.code then return c end
    c = { code = entry.code, events = {} }
    compiled[entry] = c
    local code = entry.code or ""
    if code == "" then c.err = L["No code"] return c end
    local chunk, err = loadstring("return function(self) " .. code .. "\nend", "PetCall condition")
    if not chunk then c.err = err return c end
    local okSetup, setup = pcall(chunk)
    if not okSetup then c.err = setup return c end
    c.obj = { RegisterEvent = function(_, event) c.events[#c.events + 1] = event end }
    local okFn, fn = pcall(setup, c.obj)
    if not okFn then c.err = fn return c end
    if type(fn) ~= "function" then c.err = L["Lua code must return a function."] return c end
    c.fn = fn
    return c
end

function C.Compile(entry)
    local c = compile(entry)
    return c.fn, c.err, c
end

function C.CheckLua(code)
    local c = compile({ code = code })
    if not c.fn then return false, c.err end
    local ok, res = pcall(c.fn, c.obj)
    if not ok then return false, res end
    return true, res
end

--
-- Watchers
--

local tickers = {}   -- seconds -> { ticker, watchers = {} }

local function subscribeTick(w, seconds)
    local t = tickers[seconds]
    if not t then
        t = { watchers = {} }
        t.ticker = ns:NewTicker(seconds, function()
            for watcher in pairs(t.watchers) do watcher:Refresh() end
        end)
        tickers[seconds] = t
    end
    t.watchers[w] = true
end

local function unsubscribeTicks(w)
    for seconds, t in pairs(tickers) do
        t.watchers[w] = nil
        if not next(t.watchers) then
            t.ticker:Cancel()
            tickers[seconds] = nil
        end
    end
end

local Watcher = {}
Watcher.__index = Watcher

local function collect(list, out)
    for _, e in ipairs(list) do
        if isNode(e) then collect(e.items or {}, out)
        else out[#out + 1] = e end
    end
    return out
end

function Watcher:Rebuild()
    ns.Events.UnregisterAll(self)
    unsubscribeTicks(self)
    local events = {}
    for _, e in ipairs(collect(self.list, {})) do
        local def = types[e[1]]
        if def then
            for _, ev in ipairs(def.events or {}) do events[ev] = true end
            if def.tick then subscribeTick(self, def.tick) end
            if e[1] == "luacode" then
                local _, _, c = C.Compile(e)
                for _, ev in ipairs(c.events) do events[ev] = true end
            end
        end
    end
    for ev in pairs(events) do
        ns.Events.Register(self, ev, function() self:Refresh() end)
    end
    self:Refresh(true)
end

function Watcher:Refresh(silent)
    local ctx = C.Context()
    local changed = false
    local states = {}
    for _, e in ipairs(collect(self.list, {})) do
        local v, reason = C.Eval(e, ctx)
        states[e] = { value = v, reason = reason }
        local old = self.states[e]
        if not old or old.value ~= v or old.reason ~= reason then changed = true end
    end
    for _, e in ipairs(self.list) do
        if isNode(e) then states[e] = { value = (C.Eval(e, ctx)) } end
    end
    self.states = states
    local value = C.EvalList(self.list, ctx)
    if value ~= self.value then changed = true end
    self.value = value
    if changed and not silent then self.onChange(self) end
end

function Watcher:Free()
    ns.Events.UnregisterAll(self)
    unsubscribeTicks(self)
    self.freed = true
end

function C.NewWatcher(list, onChange)
    local w = setmetatable({ list = list, onChange = onChange, states = {}, value = true }, Watcher)
    w:Rebuild()
    return w
end
```

> `Normalize` rozwija każdy węzeł, który `Simplify` potrafi zastąpić bez zmiany znaczenia, z jednym wyjątkiem: pojedynczy `OR` grup na najwyższym poziomie zostaje (to jest kształt „wiele grup”). Test „equivalence with 1.0.x” pilnuje znaczenia na wszystkich światach fixture'a.

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
local C = ns.Conditions
local ipairs, tinsert, format = ipairs, tinsert, format

local ZONE_EVENTS = { "ZONE_CHANGED", "ZONE_CHANGED_INDOORS", "ZONE_CHANGED_NEW_AREA", "PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD" }

local function contains(list, v)
    for _, x in ipairs(list or {}) do if x == v then return true end end
    return false
end

local function union(a, b)
    local out = {}
    for _, x in ipairs(a or {}) do if not contains(out, x) then out[#out + 1] = x end end
    for _, x in ipairs(b or {}) do if not contains(out, x) then out[#out + 1] = x end end
    return out
end

local function mapName(id)
    local info = C_Map.GetMapInfo(id)
    return info and info.name or tostring(id)
end

local function zoneNames(e)
    local names = {}
    if e.zoneName and e.zoneName ~= "" then names[1] = e.zoneName end
    for _, n in ipairs(e.zoneNames or {}) do names[#names + 1] = n end
    return names
end

C.RegisterType({
    key = "zone", label = L["Place"], group = "place",
    fields = { { key = "places", kind = "multiPlace" } },
    events = ZONE_EVENTS,
    new = function() return { "zone", mapIDs = { C_Map.GetBestMapForUnit("player") } } end,
    check = function(e, ctx)
        for _, n in ipairs(zoneNames(e)) do
            if n == ctx.zone or n == ctx.subzone then return true end
        end
        for _, id in ipairs(e.mapIDs or {}) do
            if ctx.mapChain[id] then return true end
        end
        return false
    end,
    reason = function(_, ctx) return format(L["You are in: %s"], ctx.subzone ~= "" and (ctx.zone .. " – " .. ctx.subzone) or ctx.zone) end,
    describe = function(e)
        local parts = zoneNames(e)
        for _, id in ipairs(e.mapIDs or {}) do parts[#parts + 1] = mapName(id) end
        return L["Place"] .. ": " .. table.concat(parts, ", ")
    end,
    merge = function(a, b)
        local names = union(zoneNames(a), zoneNames(b))
        return { "zone", zoneNames = names, mapIDs = union(a.mapIDs, b.mapIDs) }
    end,
})

local LOC_LEGACY = { city = { "city" }, battleground = { "pvp" }, instance = { "dungeon", "raid" } }
local LOC_VALUES = {
    { value = "city", label = L["City or rest area"] }, { value = "dungeon", label = L["Dungeon"] },
    { value = "raid", label = L["Raid"] }, { value = "scenario", label = L["Scenario"] },
    { value = "delve", label = L["Delve"] }, { value = "pvp", label = L["Battleground or arena"] },
    { value = "world", label = L["Open world"] },
}
local function locs(e) return e.locs or LOC_LEGACY[e.loc] or {} end
local function locLabel(v) for _, x in ipairs(LOC_VALUES) do if x.value == v then return x.label end end return v end
local function currentLoc(ctx)
    if ctx.delve then return "delve" end
    local t = ctx.instanceType
    if t == "party" then return "dungeon" end
    if t == "raid" then return "raid" end
    if t == "scenario" then return "scenario" end
    if t == "pvp" or t == "arena" then return "pvp" end
    return "world"
end

C.RegisterType({
    key = "specialLocation", label = L["Place type"], group = "place",
    fields = { { key = "locs", kind = "multiChoice", values = function() return LOC_VALUES end } },
    events = { "ZONE_CHANGED", "ZONE_CHANGED_INDOORS", "ZONE_CHANGED_NEW_AREA", "PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD", "PLAYER_FLAGS_CHANGED", "PLAYER_UPDATE_RESTING" },
    new = function() return { "specialLocation", locs = { "city" } } end,
    check = function(e, ctx)
        local cur = currentLoc(ctx)
        for _, v in ipairs(locs(e)) do
            if v == "city" and ctx.resting then return true end
            if v == cur then return true end
        end
        return false
    end,
    reason = function(_, ctx) return format(L["You are in: %s"], locLabel(ctx.resting and "city" or currentLoc(ctx))) end,
    describe = function(e)
        local parts = {}
        for _, v in ipairs(locs(e)) do parts[#parts + 1] = locLabel(v) end
        return L["Place type"] .. ": " .. table.concat(parts, ", ")
    end,
    merge = function(a, b) return { "specialLocation", locs = union(locs(a), locs(b)) } end,
})

local GROUP_VALUES = { { value = "solo", label = L["Solo"] }, { value = "party", label = PARTY }, { value = "raid", label = RAID } }
C.RegisterType({
    key = "groupType", label = L["Group"], group = "party",
    fields = { { key = "group", kind = "flags", values = function() return GROUP_VALUES end } },
    events = { "GROUP_ROSTER_UPDATE", "PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD" },
    new = function() return { "groupType", party = true, raid = true } end,
    check = function(e, ctx)
        if ctx.inRaid then return e.raid == true end
        if ctx.inGroup then return e.party == true end
        return e.solo == true
    end,
    reason = function(_, ctx) return ctx.inRaid and RAID or (ctx.inGroup and PARTY or L["Solo"]) end,
    describe = function(e)
        local parts = {}
        for _, v in ipairs(GROUP_VALUES) do if e[v.value] then parts[#parts + 1] = v.label end end
        return L["Group"] .. ": " .. table.concat(parts, ", ")
    end,
    merge = function(a, b)
        return { "groupType", solo = a.solo or b.solo or nil, party = a.party or b.party or nil, raid = a.raid or b.raid or nil }
    end,
})

C.RegisterType({
    key = "specialization", label = L["Character"], group = "party",
    fields = { { key = "class", kind = "class" }, { key = "spec", kind = "spec" }, { key = "name", kind = "text", optional = true } },
    events = { "PLAYER_SPECIALIZATION_CHANGED", "PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD" },
    new = function()
        local _, class = UnitClass("player")
        return { "specialization", class = class, spec = C_SpecializationInfo.GetSpecialization() or -1 }
    end,
    check = function(e, ctx)
        if e.class and e.class ~= "" and e.class ~= ctx.class then return false end
        if e.spec and e.spec ~= -1 and e.spec ~= ctx.spec then return false end
        if e.name and e.name ~= "" and e.name ~= ctx.name then return false end
        return true
    end,
    reason = function(_, ctx)
        local _, specName = C_SpecializationInfo.GetSpecializationInfo(ctx.spec or 0)
        return format("%s · %s", LOCALIZED_CLASS_NAMES_MALE[ctx.class] or ctx.class, specName or "?")
    end,
    describe = function(e)
        local class = (e.class and e.class ~= "") and (LOCALIZED_CLASS_NAMES_MALE[e.class] or e.class) or L["Any class"]
        local spec = L["Any specialization"]
        if e.spec and e.spec ~= -1 then spec = format("#%d", e.spec) end
        local s = format("%s: %s, %s", L["Character"], class, spec)
        if e.name and e.name ~= "" then s = s .. ", " .. e.name end
        return s
    end,
})

local function fmtTime(min) return format("%02d:%02d", math.floor(min / 60), min % 60) end
C.RegisterType({
    key = "timeOfDay", label = L["Time of day"], group = "time",
    fields = { { key = "range", kind = "timeRange" } },
    tick = 30,
    new = function() return { "timeOfDay", from = 20 * 60, to = 6 * 60 } end,
    check = function(e, ctx)
        local from, to, now = e.from or 0, e.to or 0, ctx.minutes
        if from <= to then return now >= from and now < to end
        return now >= from or now < to
    end,
    reason = function(_, ctx) return format(L["Server time: %s"], fmtTime(ctx.minutes)) end,
    describe = function(e) return format("%s: %s–%s", L["Time of day"], fmtTime(e.from or 0), fmtTime(e.to or 0)) end,
})

C.RegisterType({
    key = "macro", label = L["Macro conditional"], group = "advanced",
    fields = { { key = "macro", kind = "text", placeholder = "[outdoors,nomounted]" } },
    tick = 0.5,
    events = { "MODIFIER_STATE_CHANGED", "PLAYER_TARGET_CHANGED", "UPDATE_SHAPESHIFT_FORM", "ACTIONBAR_PAGE_CHANGED" },
    new = function() return { "macro", macro = "" } end,
    check = function(e)
        if not e.macro or e.macro == "" then return false end
        return SecureCmdOptionParse(e.macro) ~= nil
    end,
    describe = function(e) return L["Macro conditional"] .. ": " .. (e.macro or "") end,
})

local reported = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
C.RegisterType({
    key = "luacode", label = L["Lua code"], group = "advanced",
    fields = { { key = "code", kind = "code" } },
    new = function() return { "luacode", code = "-- self:RegisterEvent(\"EVENT_NAME\")\nreturn function()\n    return true\nend\n" } end,
    check = function(e)
        if e.disabledByImport then return false end
        local fn, err, c = C.Compile(e)
        if not fn then
            if reported[c] == nil then reported[c] = true ns:Printf(L["Lua condition error: %s"], tostring(err)) end
            return false
        end
        local ok, res = pcall(fn, c.obj)
        if not ok then
            if reported[c] == nil then reported[c] = true ns:Printf(L["Lua condition error: %s"], tostring(res)) end
            return false
        end
        return res and true or false
    end,
    reason = function(e)
        if e.disabledByImport then return L["Imported code is disabled until you review it."] end
        local fn, err = C.Compile(e)
        return fn and nil or tostring(err)
    end,
    describe = function() return L["Lua code"] end,
})
```

### Task 14: Zestawy z warunkami — watchery, normalizacja, aktywny zestaw

**Files:**
- Modify: `Core/Sets.lua`, `tests/sets_spec.lua`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ns.Conditions.NewWatcher/Normalize/ToModel/FromModel`.
- Produces (rozszerzenie `set`): `set:GetConditionModel() → model`, `set:SetConditionModel(model)`, `set:GetConditionStates() → { [entry] = {value, reason} }`, `set:ConditionsChanged()` (po edycji pól wiersza w miejscu), `set:ApplyTemplate(key)` (`"city"|"instance"|"here"|"spec"|"group"`); callback `ns:Fire("ConditionStateChanged", name)`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implementacja** — w `Core/Sets.lua`:

W `newSet` przed utworzeniem watchera:

```lua
    if not set:IsDefault() then
        set.settings.trigger = set.settings.trigger or {}
        ns.Conditions.Normalize(set.settings.trigger)
        set.watcher = ns.Conditions.NewWatcher(set.settings.trigger, function()
            ns:Fire("ConditionStateChanged", set.name)
            Sets:Update()
        end)

```

(i usuń poprzedni warunek `if ns.Conditions and not set:IsDefault() then … end`).

Nowe metody:

```lua
function SetMixin:GetConditionModel() return ns.Conditions.ToModel(self.settings.trigger) end

function SetMixin:SetConditionModel(model)
    local list = ns.Conditions.FromModel(model)
    local t = self.settings.trigger
    wipe(t)
    for i, e in ipairs(list) do t[i] = e end
    self:ConditionsChanged()
end

function SetMixin:ConditionsChanged()
    if self.watcher then self.watcher:Rebuild() end
    ns:Fire("ConditionStateChanged", self.name)
    changed(self)
end

function SetMixin:GetConditionStates() return self.watcher and self.watcher.states or {} end

local TEMPLATES = {
    city = function() return { { "specialLocation", locs = { "city" } } } end,
    instance = function() return { { "specialLocation", locs = { "dungeon", "raid" } } } end,
    here = function() return { { "zone", mapIDs = { C_Map.GetBestMapForUnit("player") } } } end,
    spec = function()
        local _, class = UnitClass("player")
        return { { "specialization", class = class, spec = C_SpecializationInfo.GetSpecialization() or -1 } }
    end,
    group = function() return { { "groupType", party = true, raid = true } } end,
}

function SetMixin:ApplyTemplate(key)
    local t = self.settings.trigger
    for _, e in ipairs(TEMPLATES[key]()) do t[#t + 1] = e end
    self:ConditionsChanged()
end
```

W `Sets:Reload()` po zwolnieniu watcherów nic więcej — `newSet` utworzy nowe przy pierwszym `Get`. W `SetMixin:Delete()` watcher jest już zwalniany (Task 8).

- [ ] **Step 3: `Core/Summon.lua`**

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
local C_PetJournal, InCombatLockdown, tinsert = C_PetJournal, InCombatLockdown, tinsert

local Summon = ns:NewModule("Summon")
ns.Summon = Summon

local RECENT_MAX = 5

function ns:GetCurrentPet() return C_PetJournal.GetSummonedPetGUID() end
function ns:IsAutoSummon() return ns.db.profile.enable end

function ns:SetAutoSummon(v)
    v = v and true or false
    if ns.db.profile.enable == v then return end
    ns.db.profile.enable = v
    ns:Fire("AutoSummonChanged", v)
end

local function remember(petID)
    local r = ns.db.global.recentPets
    for i = #r, 1, -1 do if r[i] == petID then table.remove(r, i) end end
    tinsert(r, 1, petID)
    while #r > RECENT_MAX do table.remove(r) end
end

function ns:GetRecentPets()
    local out = {}
    for _, id in ipairs(ns.db.global.recentPets) do
        if ns.PetCache:Contains(id) then out[#out + 1] = id end
    end
    return out
end

local function doSummon(petID)
    if ns.db.profile.verbose then
        local info = ns.PetCache:GetInfo(petID)
        ns:Printf(L["SUMMONING_MSG"], info and (info.customName or info.name) or "?")
    end
    C_PetJournal.SummonPetByGUID(petID)
    remember(petID)
    ns.SwitchTimer:Start()
end

function Summon:SummonRandom()
    local set = ns.Sets:GetCurrent()
    local petID = set and set:PickPet()
    if not petID or ns:GetCurrentPet() == petID then return false end
    if (C_PetJournal.GetPetCooldownByGUID(petID) or 0) > 0 then return false end
    doSummon(petID)
    return true
end

function ns:SummonPet(petID)
    if not petID or ns:GetCurrentPet() == petID then return end
    doSummon(petID)
end

function Summon:Dismiss(disable, verify)
    local cur = ns:GetCurrentPet()
    if cur then
        C_PetJournal.SummonPetByGUID(cur)
        if verify then
            if self.verify then self.verify:Cancel() end
            self.verify = ns:NewTicker(0.3, function()
                if ns:GetCurrentPet() == cur then
                    if not InCombatLockdown() then C_PetJournal.SummonPetByGUID(cur) end
                else
                    Summon.verify:Cancel()
                    Summon.verify = nil
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    if disable then ns:SetAutoSummon(false) end
end

function Summon:OnImmediateSet(set)
    if not set:Contains(ns:GetCurrentPet()) then ns.Ready:QueueResummon() end
end

-- Compatibility API (Bindings.xml and 1.x macros call these on the global)
function ns:ResummonPet(enable, nowait)
    if enable then ns:SetAutoSummon(true) end
    ns.Ready:QueueResummon(nowait)
end
function ns:DismissPet(disable) Summon:Dismiss(disable) end
function ns:TogglePet()
    if ns:GetCurrentPet() then Summon:Dismiss(true) else ns:ResummonPet(true, true) end
end
function ns:OpenOptions() ns:OpenWindow() end

function ns:SummonAnother() ns:ResummonPet(true, true) end
function ns:Dismiss() Summon:Dismiss(true) end
function ns:Toggle() ns:TogglePet() end

function Summon:OnInitialize()
    hooksecurefunc(C_PetJournal, "SetPetLoadOutInfo", function(slot)
        if ns.db.profile.overrideSetLoadout and slot == 1 then ns:ResummonPet() end
    end)
end
```

`ns:OpenWindow` jest zdefiniowany w `UI/MainWindow.lua`; w testach (bez UI) `OpenOptions` nie jest wołane.

`Core/SwitchTimer.lua` (port 1.0.x):

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local SwitchTimer = ns:NewModule("SwitchTimer")
ns.SwitchTimer = SwitchTimer

function SwitchTimer:OnInitialize()
    self:RegisterEvent("PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD")
    self:RegisterEvent("ZONE_CHANGED_NEW_AREA")
end

function SwitchTimer:Start(noRestart)
    if self.timer then
        if noRestart then return end
        self.timer:Cancel()
        self.timer = nil
    end
    local cfg = ns.db.profile.autoSwitch
    if cfg.timer then
        self.timer = ns:After(cfg.timerValue, function() SwitchTimer:Fire() end)
    end
end

function SwitchTimer:Stop()
    if self.timer then self.timer:Cancel() self.timer = nil end
end

function SwitchTimer:Fire()
    self.timer = nil
    if ns.db.profile.autoSwitch.citiesOnly and not IsResting() then return self:Start() end
    ns:ResummonPet()
end

function SwitchTimer:PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD()
    if ns:GetCurrentPet() then self:Start() end
end

function SwitchTimer:ZONE_CHANGED_NEW_AREA()
    if ns.db.profile.autoSwitch.onZone then
        ns:After(1, function()
            local zone = GetZoneText()
            if zone ~= SwitchTimer.lastZone then
                ns.Ready:QueueResummon()
                SwitchTimer.lastZone = zone
            end
        end)
    end
end
```

`SUMMONING_MSG` już jest w `Locales/enUS.lua`.

- [ ] **Step 1: `UI/SetList.lua`**

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
local CreateFrame = CreateFrame
ns.UI = ns.UI or {}
local SetList = {}
ns.UI.SetList = SetList

local COLLAPSE_BELOW = 700
local panel, rail, rows, newBox = nil, nil, {}, nil
local STATUS = {
    active = { "●", 0.3, 1, 0.3, L["Active now"] },
    waiting = { "○", 0.8, 0.8, 0.8, L["Conditions not met"] },
    empty = { "●", 1, 0.55, 0.1, L["No pets to choose from"] },
    noConditions = { "●", 1, 0.55, 0.1, L["No conditions: this set is always active"] },
    disabled = { "◌", 0.5, 0.5, 0.5, L["Disabled"] },
}

local function setMenu(owner, root, set)
    if not set:IsDefault() then
        root:CreateButton(set.settings.enabled and L["Disable"] or L["Enable"], function() set:SetEnabled(not set.settings.enabled) end)
        root:CreateButton(L["Rename"], function() ns.UI.Main:SelectSet(set.name) ns.UI.Main:SelectTab("settings") end)
    end
    if ns.UI.Share then root:CreateButton(L["Export"], function() ns.UI.Share:ShowExport(set.name) end) end
    if not set:IsDefault() then
        root:CreateButton(DELETE, function()
            StaticPopup_Show("PETCALL_CONFIRM", format(L["Delete \"%s\"? This cannot be undone."], set.name), nil,
                { onAccept = function() set:Delete() end })
        end)
    end
end

local function row(i)
    if rows[i] then return rows[i] end
    local r = CreateFrame("Button", nil, panel)
    r:SetHeight(24)
    r:RegisterForClicks("LeftButtonUp", "RightButtonUp")
    r:SetHighlightAtlas("PetList-ButtonHighlight", "ADD")
    r.selected = r:CreateTexture(nil, "BACKGROUND")
    r.selected:SetAllPoints()
    r.selected:SetAtlas("PetList-ButtonSelect")
    r.dot = r:CreateFontString(nil, "OVERLAY", "GameFontNormal")
    r.dot:SetPoint("LEFT", 6, 0)
    r.name = r:CreateFontString(nil, "OVERLAY", "GameFontHighlight")
    r.name:SetPoint("LEFT", r.dot, "RIGHT", 6, 0)
    r.name:SetPoint("RIGHT", -34, 0)
    r.name:SetJustifyH("LEFT")
    r.name:SetWordWrap(false)
    r.prio = r:CreateFontString(nil, "OVERLAY", "GameFontNormalSmall")
    r.prio:SetPoint("RIGHT", -6, 0)
    r:SetScript("OnClick", function(self, button)
        if button == "RightButton" then
            MenuUtil.CreateContextMenu(self, function(o, root) setMenu(o, root, self.set) end)
        else
            ns.UI.Main:SelectSet(self.set.name)
        end
    end)
    ns.Widgets.Tooltip(r, function(self) return self.set:DisplayName() end, function(self) return STATUS[self.set:GetStatus()][5] end)
    rows[i] = r
    return r
end

function SetList:Refresh()
    if not panel then return end
    local sets = ns.Sets:List()
    local selected = ns.UI.Main:GetSelectedSet()
    for i, set in ipairs(sets) do
        local r = row(i)
        r.set = set
        r:ClearAllPoints()
        r:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 0, -28 - (i - 1) * 26)
        r:SetPoint("RIGHT")
        local st = STATUS[set:GetStatus()]
        r.dot:SetText(st[1])
        r.dot:SetTextColor(st[2], st[3], st[4])
        r.name:SetText(set:DisplayName() .. (set.settings.enabled and "" or (" (" .. L["off"] .. ")")))
        r.name:SetFontObject(set.settings.enabled and "GameFontHighlight" or "GameFontDisable")
        r.prio:SetText(set:IsDefault() and "" or (ns.Weights.Short(set:GetPriority()) or ""))
        r.selected:SetShown(selected == set)
        r.selected:SetVertexColor(ns.Skin:Accent())
        r:Show()
    end
    for i = #sets + 1, #rows do rows[i]:Hide() end
    rail.count:SetText(#sets)
end

function SetList:SetCollapsed(collapsed, user)
    local f = ns.UI.Main:GetFrame()
    panel:SetShown(not collapsed)
    rail:SetShown(collapsed)
    f.left:SetWidth(collapsed and 28 or 190)
    if user then ns.db.global.window.setsCollapsed = collapsed end
end

local function autoCollapse()
    local user = ns.db.global.window.setsCollapsed
    local f = ns.UI.Main:GetFrame()
    if f:GetWidth() < COLLAPSE_BELOW then return SetList:SetCollapsed(true) end
    if user ~= nil then return SetList:SetCollapsed(user) end
    SetList:SetCollapsed(#ns.Sets:List() <= 1)
end

ns:RegisterCallback(SetList, "WindowBuilt", function(_, frame)
    panel = CreateFrame("Frame", nil, frame.left)
    panel:SetAllPoints()
    local title = ns.Widgets.Label(panel, L["Sets"], "GameFontNormal")
    title:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 6, -6)
    local collapse = CreateFrame("Button", nil, panel)
    collapse:SetSize(20, 20)
    collapse:SetPoint("TOPRIGHT", -2, -2)
    collapse:SetNormalAtlas("common-icon-backarrow")
    collapse:SetScript("OnClick", function() SetList:SetCollapsed(true, true) end)
    ns.Widgets.Tooltip(collapse, L["Collapse"])

    newBox = CreateFrame("EditBox", nil, panel, "InputBoxTemplate")
    newBox:SetHeight(20)
    newBox:SetPoint("BOTTOMLEFT", 8, 32)
    newBox:SetPoint("BOTTOMRIGHT", -4, 32)
    newBox:SetAutoFocus(false)
    newBox:Hide()
    newBox:SetScript("OnEscapePressed", function(self) self:SetText("") self:Hide() end)
    newBox:SetScript("OnEnterPressed", function(self)
        local set, ok, err = ns.Sets:Create(self:GetText())
        if not ok then
            UIErrorsFrame:AddMessage(err, 1, 0.3, 0.3)
            PlaySound(SOUNDKIT.IG_PLAYER_INVITE_DECLINE)
            return
        end
        self:SetText("") self:Hide()
        ns.UI.Main:SelectSet(set.name)
        ns.UI.Main:SelectTab("when")
    end)
    ns.Skin:Apply("EditBox", newBox)

    local new = ns.Widgets.Button(panel, L["+ New"], function() newBox:Show() newBox:SetFocus() end)
    new:SetPoint("BOTTOMLEFT", 4, 4)
    local import = ns.Widgets.Button(panel, L["Import"], function() if ns.UI.Share then ns.UI.Share:ShowImport() end end)
    import:SetPoint("LEFT", new, "RIGHT", 4, 0)

    rail = CreateFrame("Button", nil, frame.left)
    rail:SetAllPoints()
    rail:Hide()
    rail.label = ns.Widgets.Label(rail, L["Sets"], "GameFontNormalSmall")
    rail.label:SetPoint("TOP", 0, -8)
    rail.count = ns.Widgets.Label(rail, "", "GameFontHighlightSmall")
    rail.count:SetPoint("TOP", rail.label, "BOTTOM", 0, -4)
    rail:SetScript("OnClick", function() SetList:SetCollapsed(false, true) end)
    ns.Widgets.Tooltip(rail, L["Show sets"])
    autoCollapse()
end)

local refresh = function() SetList:Refresh() end
for _, cb in ipairs({ "SetsChanged", "CurrentSetChanged", "ConditionStateChanged", "SelectedSetChanged", "PetsChanged", "WindowShown" }) do
    ns:RegisterCallback(SetList, cb, refresh)
end
ns:RegisterCallback(SetList, "WindowResized", autoCollapse)
ns.Skin:OnLooksChanged(function() SetList:Refresh() end)
```

> Rejestracja wielu callbacków tą samą funkcją na tym samym właścicielu jest poprawna: `ns:RegisterCallback` trzyma osobną listę per nazwa.

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
ns.UI = ns.UI or {}
local Bar = {}
ns.UI.StatusBar = Bar

local bar

function Bar:Refresh()
    if not bar or not bar:IsVisible() then return end
    local set = ns.Sets:GetCurrent()
    local pet = ns:GetCurrentPet()
    local info = pet and ns.PetCache:GetInfo(pet)
    bar.left:SetText(format("%s: %s · %s: %s", L["Active"], set and set:DisplayName() or NONE,
        L["Pet"], info and (info.customName or info.name) or L["none"]))
    local blockers = ns.Ready:GetBlockers()
    if #blockers == 0 then
        bar.mid:SetText("|cff40ff40" .. L["Ready"] .. "|r")
    else
        local labels = {}
        for i = 1, math.min(3, #blockers) do labels[i] = blockers[i].label end
        bar.mid:SetText("|cffffb040" .. L["Paused by"] .. ":|r " .. table.concat(labels, ", ") .. (#blockers > 3 and " …" or ""))
    end
    bar.blockers = blockers
    bar.auto:SetText(ns:IsAutoSummon() and L["Auto-summon: ON"] or L["Auto-summon: OFF"])
    bar.auto:SetWidth(bar.auto:GetFontString():GetStringWidth() + 24)
end

ns:RegisterCallback(Bar, "WindowBuilt", function(_, frame)
    bar = frame.status
    bar.left = ns.Widgets.Label(bar, "", "GameFontHighlightSmall")
    bar.left:SetPoint("LEFT")
    bar.left:SetWidth(260)
    bar.left:SetWordWrap(false)
    bar.auto = ns.Widgets.Button(bar, "", function() ns:SetAutoSummon(not ns:IsAutoSummon()) end)
    bar.auto:SetPoint("RIGHT")
    bar.another = ns.Widgets.Button(bar, L["Summon another"], function() ns:SummonAnother() end)
    bar.another:SetPoint("RIGHT", bar.auto, "LEFT", -4, 0)
    bar.mid = ns.Widgets.Label(bar, "", "GameFontHighlightSmall")
    bar.mid:SetPoint("LEFT", bar.left, "RIGHT", 8, 0)
    bar.mid:SetPoint("RIGHT", bar.another, "LEFT", -8, 0)
    bar.mid:SetWordWrap(false)
    local hit = CreateFrame("Frame", nil, bar)
    hit:SetAllPoints(bar.mid)
    hit:EnableMouse(true)
    ns.Widgets.Tooltip(hit, L["Why is no pet summoned?"], function()
        if not bar.blockers or #bar.blockers == 0 then return L["Nothing is blocking PetCall."] end
        local lines = {}
        for _, b in ipairs(bar.blockers) do lines[#lines + 1] = "• " .. b.label end
        return table.concat(lines, "\n")
    end)
    bar:SetScript("OnShow", function() Bar:Refresh() end)
end)

local refresh = ns.Debounce(0.1, function() Bar:Refresh() end)
for _, cb in ipairs({ "ReadyChanged", "CurrentSetChanged", "PetsChanged", "AutoSummonChanged", "SummonedPetChanged", "WindowShown" }) do
    ns:RegisterCallback(Bar, cb, refresh)
end
```

```lua
        ns:Fire("SummonedPetChanged")
        self:_CheckSummon()
    end
end
```

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
local Tab = {}
local page, set

local function priorityMenu(owner, root)
    for _, lvl in ipairs(ns.Weights.LEVELS) do
        if lvl.value > 0 then
            root:CreateRadio(lvl.label, function() return set and ns.Weights.Nearest(set:GetPriority()) == lvl.value end,
                function() set:SetPriority(lvl.value) end)
        end
    end
end

function Tab:Refresh(newSet)
    set = newSet
    local default = set:IsDefault()
    page.title:SetText(set:DisplayName())
    for _, w in ipairs(page.editable) do w:SetShown(not default) end
    page.defaultNote:SetShown(default)
    page.enabled:SetChecked(set.settings.enabled)
    page.immediate:SetChecked(set.settings.immediate and true or false)
    page.priority:GenerateMenu()
    page.name:SetText(set.name)
    page.export:ClearAllPoints()
    if default then
        page.export:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", page.defaultNote, "BOTTOMLEFT", 0, -16)
    else
        page.export:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", page.nameLabel, "BOTTOMLEFT", -4, -24)
    end
end

ns.UI.Main:RegisterTab("settings", L["Set settings"], function(p)
    page = p
    page.editable = {}
    page.title = ns.Widgets.Label(page, "", "GameFontNormalLarge")
    page.title:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 8, -8)

    page.enabled = ns.Widgets.Checkbox(page, L["Enabled"], function() return set and set.settings.enabled end, function(v) set:SetEnabled(v) end)
    page.enabled:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", page.title, "BOTTOMLEFT", -2, -12)
    tinsert(page.editable, page.enabled)

    local prioLabel = ns.Widgets.Label(page, L["Priority"])
    prioLabel:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", page.enabled, "BOTTOMLEFT", 4, -14)
    page.priority = ns.Widgets.Dropdown(page, 200, priorityMenu)
    page.priority:SetPoint("LEFT", prioLabel, "RIGHT", 10, 0)
    local prioHelp = ns.Widgets.Label(page, L["When several sets match, the one with the highest priority is used."], "GameFontDisableSmall")
    prioHelp:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", prioLabel, "BOTTOMLEFT", 0, -6)
    for _, w in ipairs({ prioLabel, page.priority, prioHelp }) do tinsert(page.editable, w) end

    page.immediate = ns.Widgets.Checkbox(page, L["Summon immediately when this set becomes active"],
        function() return set and set.settings.immediate end, function(v) set:SetImmediate(v) end)
    page.immediate:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", prioHelp, "BOTTOMLEFT", -4, -12)
    tinsert(page.editable, page.immediate)

    local nameLabel = ns.Widgets.Label(page, L["Name"])
    nameLabel:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", page.immediate, "BOTTOMLEFT", 4, -18)
    page.name = CreateFrame("EditBox", nil, page, "InputBoxTemplate")
    page.name:SetSize(200, 20)
    page.name:SetAutoFocus(false)
    page.name:SetPoint("LEFT", nameLabel, "RIGHT", 10, 0)
    ns.Skin:Apply("EditBox", page.name)
    local rename = ns.Widgets.Button(page, L["Rename"], function()
        local ok, err = set:Rename(page.name:GetText())
        if not ok then UIErrorsFrame:AddMessage(err, 1, 0.3, 0.3) PlaySound(SOUNDKIT.IG_PLAYER_INVITE_DECLINE) end
        page.name:ClearFocus()
    end)
    rename:SetPoint("LEFT", page.name, "RIGHT", 8, 0)
    page.name:SetScript("OnEnterPressed", function() rename:Click() end)
    for _, w in ipairs({ nameLabel, page.name, rename }) do tinsert(page.editable, w) end

    page.nameLabel = nameLabel
    page.export = ns.Widgets.Button(page, L["Export"], function() if ns.UI.Share then ns.UI.Share:ShowExport(set.name) end end)
    local export = page.export
    local delete = ns.Widgets.Button(page, L["Delete set"], function()
        StaticPopup_Show("PETCALL_CONFIRM", format(L["Delete \"%s\"? This cannot be undone."], set.name), nil,
            { onAccept = function() set:Delete() end })
    end)
    delete:SetPoint("LEFT", export, "RIGHT", 8, 0)
    tinsert(page.editable, delete)

    page.defaultNote = ns.Widgets.Label(page, L["The default set is used whenever no other set is active. It cannot be renamed, disabled or deleted."], "GameFontHighlight")
    page.defaultNote:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", page.title, "BOTTOMLEFT", 0, -12)
    page.defaultNote:SetPoint("RIGHT", -8, 0)
    page.defaultNote:SetWordWrap(true)
    return Tab
end, 3)

ns:RegisterCallback(Tab, "SetsChanged", function() if page and page:IsVisible() and set and not set.deleted then Tab:Refresh(set) end end)
```

### Task 17: Zakładka Kiedy — edytor warunków

**Files:**
- Create: `UI/WhenTab.lua`
- Modify: `PetCall.toc` (po `UI\SetSettingsTab.lua`: `UI\WhenTab.lua`), `Locales/enUS.lua`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `set:GetConditionModel/SetConditionModel/ConditionsChanged/GetConditionStates/ApplyTemplate/ConditionsMet/HasConditions`, `ns.Conditions.Types/GetType/Describe/Simplify/IsLegacyNode/CheckLua`, callback `ConditionStateChanged`.
- Produces: zakładka `"when"` (`L["When"]`, kolejność 2).

Edytor buduje się ze schematów pól (`def.fields[i].kind`):

| `kind` | Kontrolka | Zapis |
|---|---|---|
| `multiPlace` | chipy nazw (`zoneName`, `zoneNames`) i map (`mapIDs`) z × + przycisk „+ obecne” (dodaje `C_Map.GetBestMapForUnit("player")` do `mapIDs`) | modyfikuje listy, `ConditionsChanged()` |
| `multiChoice` | lista rozwijana z polami wyboru (`values()`) | lista wartości w `field.key` |
| `flags` | trzy pola wyboru z `values()` | `entry[value] = true/nil` |
| `class` | lista: „Dowolna” + klasy z `CLASS_SORT_ORDER` | `class` (`""` = dowolna) |
| `spec` | lista: „Dowolna” + 1–4 z nazwami z `C_SpecializationInfo.GetSpecializationInfo(i)` | `spec` (−1 = dowolna) |
| `text` | pole tekstowe (Enter zapisuje) | `field.key` |
| `timeRange` | dwa pola `HH:MM` | `from`, `to` w minutach |
| `code` | wielowierszowe pole w ramce przewijanej (`ScrollFrame` + `EditBox` multiline), przycisk „Sprawdź”, wynik/błąd pod polem; gdy `disabledByImport` — czerwony pasek z „Włącz” | `code`, `disabledByImport = nil` |

- [ ] **Step 1: Implementacja** — `UI/WhenTab.lua`:

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
local CreateFrame = CreateFrame
local Tab = {}
local page, scroll, content, set, model
local pool = {}          -- reusable row/group frames are rebuilt on each Refresh (sets have few rows)

local TEMPLATES = {
    { key = "city", label = L["In a city"] }, { key = "instance", label = L["In a raid or dungeon"] },
    { key = "here", label = L["Here (current place)"] }, { key = "spec", label = L["On my current specialization"] },
    { key = "group", label = L["In a group"] },
}

local function save() set:SetConditionModel(model) end
local function changedInPlace() set:ConditionsChanged() end

local function clear()
    for _, f in ipairs(pool) do f:Hide() f:SetParent(nil) end
    wipe(pool)
end

local function track(f) pool[#pool + 1] = f return f end

local function chip(parent, text, onRemove)
    local b = track(CreateFrame("Button", nil, parent, "UIPanelButtonTemplate"))
    b:SetText(text .. "  ×")
    b:SetHeight(20)
    b:SetWidth(b:GetFontString():GetStringWidth() + 20)
    b:SetScript("OnClick", onRemove)
    ns.Skin:Apply("Button", b)
    return b
end

local function mapName(id) local i = C_Map.GetMapInfo(id) return i and i.name or tostring(id) end

local function buildField(row, e, field, prev)
    local function place(w, gap) w:SetPoint("LEFT", prev, "RIGHT", gap or 6, 0) prev = w return w end
    if field.kind == "multiPlace" then
        local names = {}
        if e.zoneName and e.zoneName ~= "" then names[#names + 1] = { text = e.zoneName, remove = function() e.zoneName = nil end } end
        for i, n in ipairs(e.zoneNames or {}) do names[#names + 1] = { text = n, remove = function() table.remove(e.zoneNames, i) end } end
        for i, id in ipairs(e.mapIDs or {}) do names[#names + 1] = { text = mapName(id), remove = function() table.remove(e.mapIDs, i) end } end
        for _, n in ipairs(names) do place(chip(row, n.text, function() n.remove() changedInPlace() Tab:Refresh(set) end)) end
        place(track(ns.Widgets.Button(row, L["+ current"], function()
            e.mapIDs = e.mapIDs or {}
            local id = C_Map.GetBestMapForUnit("player")
            if id then tinsert(e.mapIDs, id) changedInPlace() Tab:Refresh(set) end
        end)))
    elseif field.kind == "multiChoice" then
        local values = field.values()
        local dd = track(ns.Widgets.Dropdown(row, 200, function(owner, root)
            for _, v in ipairs(values) do
                root:CreateCheckbox(v.label, function()
                    local cur = e[field.key] or (e[1] == "specialLocation" and ({ city = { "city" }, battleground = { "pvp" }, instance = { "dungeon", "raid" } })[e.loc]) or {}
                    for _, x in ipairs(cur) do if x == v.value then return true end end
                end, function()
                    local cur = e[field.key] or (e[1] == "specialLocation" and ({ city = { "city" }, battleground = { "pvp" }, instance = { "dungeon", "raid" } })[e.loc]) or {}
                    local out, found = {}, false
                    for _, x in ipairs(cur) do if x == v.value then found = true else out[#out + 1] = x end end
                    if not found then out[#out + 1] = v.value end
                    e[field.key], e.loc = out, nil
                    changedInPlace()
                end)
            end
        end))
        place(dd)
    elseif field.kind == "flags" then
        for _, v in ipairs(field.values()) do
            local cb = track(ns.Widgets.Checkbox(row, v.label, function() return e[v.value] end,
                function(on) e[v.value] = on or nil changedInPlace() end))
            place(cb, 8)
            prev = cb.text
        end
    elseif field.kind == "class" then
        place(track(ns.Widgets.Dropdown(row, 150, function(owner, root)
            root:CreateRadio(L["Any class"], function() return not e.class or e.class == "" end, function() e.class = "" changedInPlace() end)
            for _, c in ipairs(CLASS_SORT_ORDER) do
                root:CreateRadio(LOCALIZED_CLASS_NAMES_MALE[c] or c, function() return e.class == c end, function() e.class = c changedInPlace() end)
            end
        end)))
    elseif field.kind == "spec" then
        place(track(ns.Widgets.Dropdown(row, 170, function(owner, root)
            root:CreateRadio(L["Any specialization"], function() return not e.spec or e.spec == -1 end, function() e.spec = -1 changedInPlace() end)
            for i = 1, 4 do
                local _, name = C_SpecializationInfo.GetSpecializationInfo(i)
                if name then
                    root:CreateRadio(format("#%d %s", i, name), function() return e.spec == i end, function() e.spec = i changedInPlace() end)
                end
            end
        end)))
    elseif field.kind == "text" then
        local eb = track(CreateFrame("EditBox", nil, row, "InputBoxTemplate"))
        eb:SetSize(field.optional and 120 or 220, 20)
        eb:SetAutoFocus(false)
        eb:SetText(e[field.key] or "")
        eb:SetScript("OnEnterPressed", function(self) e[field.key] = self:GetText() self:ClearFocus() changedInPlace() end)
        eb:SetScript("OnEditFocusLost", function(self) if (e[field.key] or "") ~= self:GetText() then e[field.key] = self:GetText() changedInPlace() end end)
        if field.placeholder then ns.Widgets.Tooltip(eb, field.placeholder) end
        ns.Skin:Apply("EditBox", eb)
        place(eb, 10)
    elseif field.kind == "timeRange" then
        local function timeBox(key)
            local eb = track(CreateFrame("EditBox", nil, row, "InputBoxTemplate"))
            eb:SetSize(50, 20)
            eb:SetAutoFocus(false)
            eb:SetText(format("%02d:%02d", math.floor((e[key] or 0) / 60), (e[key] or 0) % 60))
            eb:SetScript("OnEnterPressed", function(self)
                local h, m = self:GetText():match("^(%d%d?):(%d%d)$")
                h, m = tonumber(h), tonumber(m)
                if h and m and h < 24 and m < 60 then e[key] = h * 60 + m changedInPlace() else PlaySound(SOUNDKIT.IG_PLAYER_INVITE_DECLINE) end
                self:ClearFocus()
            end)
            ns.Skin:Apply("EditBox", eb)
            return eb
        end
        place(timeBox("from"), 10)
        local dash = track(ns.Widgets.Label(row, "–"))
        place(dash, 4)
        place(timeBox("to"), 4)
    elseif field.kind == "code" then
        -- code editor sits under the row; the row grows
        row.codeEditor = true
    end
    return prev
end

local function codeEditor(parent, e, anchor)
    local box = track(CreateFrame("Frame", nil, parent, "InsetFrameTemplate"))
    box:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", anchor, "BOTTOMLEFT", 24, -4)
    box:SetPoint("RIGHT", -8, 0)
    box:SetHeight(120)
    local sf = CreateFrame("ScrollFrame", nil, box, "ScrollFrameTemplate")
    sf:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 6, -6)
    sf:SetPoint("BOTTOMRIGHT", -26, 6)
    local eb = CreateFrame("EditBox", nil, sf)
    eb:SetMultiLine(true)
    eb:SetFontObject("ChatFontNormal")
    eb:SetWidth(400)
    eb:SetAutoFocus(false)
    eb:SetText(e.code or "")
    sf:SetScrollChild(eb)
    local result = track(ns.Widgets.Label(parent, "", "GameFontHighlightSmall"))
    result:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", box, "BOTTOMLEFT", 0, -4)
    result:SetPoint("RIGHT", -8, 0)
    local check = track(ns.Widgets.Button(parent, L["Check"], function()
        e.code = eb:GetText()
        local ok, res = ns.Conditions.CheckLua(e.code)
        result:SetText(ok and ("|cff40ff40" .. L["OK, current result:"] .. "|r " .. tostring(res)) or ("|cffff4040" .. tostring(res) .. "|r"))
        changedInPlace()
    end))
    check:SetPoint("TOPRIGHT", box, "BOTTOMRIGHT", 0, -2)
    if e.disabledByImport then
        local warn = track(ns.Widgets.Label(parent, "|cffff4040" .. L["Imported code is disabled until you review it."] .. "|r"))
        warn:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", result, "BOTTOMLEFT", 0, -6)
        local enable = track(ns.Widgets.Button(parent, L["Review done – enable"], function()
            e.disabledByImport = nil changedInPlace() Tab:Refresh(set)
        end))
        enable:SetPoint("LEFT", warn, "RIGHT", 8, 0)
        return 190
    end
    return 160
end

local function stateDot(row, e)
    local st = set:GetConditionStates()[e]
    local dot = track(ns.Widgets.Label(row, st and st.value and "●" or "○", "GameFontNormal"))
    dot:SetTextColor(st and st.value and 0.3 or 0.8, st and st.value and 1 or 0.8, st and st.value and 0.3 or 0.8)
    local hit = track(CreateFrame("Frame", nil, row))
    hit:SetAllPoints(dot)
    hit:EnableMouse(true)
    ns.Widgets.Tooltip(hit, st and st.value and L["Met right now"] or L["Not met right now"], st and st.reason)
    return dot
end

local function buildRow(parent, group, index, e, y)
    local row = track(CreateFrame("Frame", nil, parent))
    row:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 8, y)
    row:SetPoint("RIGHT", -8, 0)
    row:SetHeight(26)
    local dot = stateDot(row, e)
    dot:SetPoint("LEFT")
    local height = 28
    if ns.Conditions.IsLegacyNode(e) then
        local text = track(ns.Widgets.Label(row, L["Complex rule (previous version)"] .. ": " .. ns.Conditions.Describe(e), "GameFontHighlightSmall"))
        text:SetPoint("LEFT", dot, "RIGHT", 6, 0)
        text:SetPoint("RIGHT", -120, 0)
        text:SetWordWrap(true)
        local simplified = ns.Conditions.Simplify(e)
        local simplify = track(ns.Widgets.Button(row, L["Simplify"], function()
            table.remove(group.rows, index)
            for k = #simplified, 1, -1 do tinsert(group.rows, index, simplified[k]) end
            save() Tab:Refresh(set)
        end))
        simplify:SetPoint("RIGHT", -28, 0)
        simplify:SetEnabled(simplified ~= nil)
        ns.Widgets.Tooltip(simplify, simplified and L["Rewrite as simple conditions with the same meaning."] or L["This rule cannot be simplified without changing its meaning."])
        height = 40
    else
        local def = ns.Conditions.GetType(e[1])
        local label = track(ns.Widgets.Label(row, def.label, "GameFontNormal"))
        label:SetPoint("LEFT", dot, "RIGHT", 6, 0)
        label:SetWidth(120)
        local prev = label
        for _, field in ipairs(def.fields) do prev = buildField(row, e, field, prev) end
        local negate = track(ns.Widgets.Checkbox(row, L["not"], function() return e.negate end, function(v) e.negate = v or nil changedInPlace() end))
        negate:SetPoint("RIGHT", -60, 0)
        if row.codeEditor then height = 28 + codeEditor(parent, e, row) end
    end
    local remove = track(ns.Widgets.Button(row, "×", function()
        table.remove(group.rows, index)
        save() Tab:Refresh(set)
    end))
    remove:SetWidth(24)
    remove:SetPoint("RIGHT")
    return height
end

local function addMenu(group)
    return function(owner, root)
        local groups = { place = L["Place"], party = L["Group and character"], time = L["Time"], advanced = L["Advanced"] }
        local sub = {}
        for _, def in ipairs(ns.Conditions.Types()) do
            sub[def.group] = sub[def.group] or root:CreateButton(groups[def.group])
            sub[def.group]:CreateButton(def.label, function()
                tinsert(group.rows, def.new())
                save() Tab:Refresh(set)
            end)
        end
    end
end

local render

function Tab:Refresh(newSet)
    set = newSet
    if not page or set:IsDefault() then return end
    model = set:GetConditionModel()
    render()
end

-- Draws `model`. An empty extra group lives only in this local model until a
-- condition is added to it (FromModel drops empty groups on save).
function render()
    clear()
    local y = -8
    local header = track(ns.Widgets.Label(content, "", "GameFontNormal"))
    header:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 8, y)
    local met = set:ConditionsMet()
    header:SetText(format("%s   %s", #model.groups > 1 and L["Use this set when ANY of these groups is met:"] or L["Use this set when ALL conditions are met:"],
        met and "|cff40ff40● " .. L["active"] .. "|r" or "|cffcccccc○ " .. L["inactive"] .. "|r"))
    y = y - 24
    if not set:HasConditions() then
        local note = track(ns.Widgets.Label(content, "|cffffb040" .. L["Without conditions this set is always active."] .. "|r"))
        note:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 8, y)
        y = y - 22
        local prev
        for _, t in ipairs(TEMPLATES) do
            local b = track(ns.Widgets.Button(content, t.label, function() set:ApplyTemplate(t.key) Tab:Refresh(set) end))
            if prev then b:SetPoint("LEFT", prev, "RIGHT", 6, 0) else b:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 8, y) end
            prev = b
        end
        y = y - 30
        if #model.groups[1].rows == 0 then
            local add = track(ns.Widgets.Dropdown(content, 180, addMenu(model.groups[1])))
            add:SetDefaultText(L["+ Add condition"])
            add:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 8, y)
            y = y - 30
        end
    end
    for gi, group in ipairs(model.groups) do
        if #group.rows == 0 and gi == 1 and not set:HasConditions() then break end
        if gi > 1 then
            local orLabel = track(ns.Widgets.Label(content, L["OR"], "GameFontNormalLarge"))
            orLabel:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 12, y)
            y = y - 24
        end
        local card = track(CreateFrame("Frame", nil, content, "InsetFrameTemplate"))
        card:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 4, y)
        card:SetPoint("RIGHT", -4, 0)
        ns.Skin:Apply("Inset", card)
        local cy = -6
        for ri, e in ipairs(group.rows) do cy = cy - buildRow(card, group, ri, e, cy) end
        local add = track(ns.Widgets.Dropdown(card, 180, addMenu(group)))
        add:SetDefaultText(L["+ Add condition"])
        add:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 8, cy - 2)
        if #model.groups > 1 then
            local removeGroup = track(ns.Widgets.Button(card, L["Remove group"], function()
                table.remove(model.groups, gi) save() Tab:Refresh(set)
            end))
            removeGroup:SetPoint("LEFT", add, "RIGHT", 8, 0)
        end
        cy = cy - 32
        card:SetHeight(-cy + 4)
        y = y + cy - 10
    end
    if set:HasConditions() then
        local orLink = track(ns.Widgets.Button(content, L["+ Or another set of conditions"], function()
            tinsert(model.groups, { rows = {} }) render()
        end))
        orLink:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 8, y)
        y = y - 30
    end
    content:SetHeight(-y + 10)
end

ns.UI.Main:RegisterTab("when", L["When"], function(p)
    page = p
    scroll = CreateFrame("ScrollFrame", nil, page, "ScrollFrameTemplate")
    scroll:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 0, -2)
    scroll:SetPoint("BOTTOMRIGHT", -24, 2)
    content = CreateFrame("Frame", nil, scroll)
    content:SetSize(10, 10)
    scroll:SetScrollChild(content)
    scroll:SetScript("OnSizeChanged", function(self, w) content:SetWidth(w) end)
    ns.Skin:Apply("ScrollBar", scroll.ScrollBar)
    return Tab
end, 2)

local refreshSoon = ns.Debounce(0.1, function()
    if page and page:IsVisible() and set and not set.deleted then Tab:Refresh(set) end
end)
ns:RegisterCallback(Tab, "ConditionStateChanged", function(_, name) if set and name == set.name then refreshSoon() end end)
```

Klucze do `Locales/enUS.lua`: `In a city`, `In a raid or dungeon`, `Here (current place)`, `On my current specialization`, `In a group`, `+ current`, `Check`, `OK, current result:`, `Review done – enable`, `Met right now`, `Not met right now`, `Rewrite as simple conditions with the same meaning.`, `This rule cannot be simplified without changing its meaning.`, `Group and character`, `Time`, `Advanced`, `Use this set when ANY of these groups is met:`, `Use this set when ALL conditions are met:`, `active`, `inactive`, `Without conditions this set is always active.`, `+ Add condition`, `OR`, `Remove group`, `+ Or another set of conditions`, `When`.

Port `SpecialItems.lua` 1.0.15 (po hotfixie 1.0.14) z tymi różnicami: brak Ace3 i AceConfig (opcje przechodzą do `UI/Settings.lua`), `BAG_UPDATE` → `BAG_UPDATE_DELAYED`, rejestracja przez własną ramkę z `RegisterUnitEvent` dla `UNIT_SPELLCAST_SUCCEEDED`, timer przez `ns:After`.

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
local GetItemCount, GetItemInfo, IsEquippedItem = C_Item.GetItemCount, C_Item.GetItemInfo, C_Item.IsEquippedItem
local GetZoneText, ipairs, pairs, tonumber, strsub, setmetatable, rawset, sort =
    GetZoneText, ipairs, pairs, tonumber, strsub, setmetatable, rawset, table.sort

local SI = ns:NewModule("SpecialItems")
ns.SpecialItems = SI

local MAP_NAME = setmetatable({}, { __index = function(t, id)
    local info = C_Map.GetMapInfo(id)
    rawset(t, id, info and info.name)
    return info and info.name
end })

local ENTRIES = {}
local function bagItem(id) ENTRIES["item:" .. id] = { events = { "BAG_UPDATE_DELAYED" }, check = function() return GetItemCount(id) > 0 end } end
for _, id in ipairs({ 18597, 18598, 30803, 31815, 31880, 31881, 32834, 34971, 36936, 46396, 46397, 46831 }) do bagItem(id) end
ENTRIES["item:12185"] = { events = { "PLAYER_EQUIPMENT_CHANGED", "EQUIPMENT_SWAP_FINISHED" }, check = function() return IsEquippedItem(12185) end }
ENTRIES["item:46362"] = { events = { "BAG_UPDATE_DELAYED", "ZONE_CHANGED", "ZONE_CHANGED_NEW_AREA" }, check = function()
    local z = GetZoneText()
    return GetItemCount(46362) > 0 and (z == MAP_NAME[201] or z == MAP_NAME[161] or z == MAP_NAME[261])
end }
ENTRIES["item:68646"] = { events = { "BAG_UPDATE_DELAYED", "ZONE_CHANGED", "ZONE_CHANGED_NEW_AREA" }, check = function()
    return GetItemCount(68646) > 0 and GetZoneText() == MAP_NAME[281]
end }
ENTRIES["item:71137"] = { unitEvents = { "UNIT_SPELLCAST_SUCCEEDED" }, check = function(self, event, unit, castGUID, spellID)
    if event == "UNIT_SPELLCAST_SUCCEEDED" and spellID == 100959 then
        if self.timer then self.timer:Cancel() end
        self.timer = ns:After(180, function()
            self.timer = nil
            SI:Update("item:71137", false)
        end)
        return true
    end
    return self.timer ~= nil
end }
local function questEntry(...)
    local ids = { ... }
    return { quest = true, check = function()
        for _, q in ipairs(ids) do if SI.quests[q] then return true end end
        return false
    end }
end
ENTRIES["quest:11878"] = questEntry(11878)
ENTRIES["quest:26831"] = questEntry(26831)
ENTRIES["quest:25371"] = questEntry(25371)
ENTRIES["quest:35396"] = questEntry(25371, 35317, 35331, 34965)

local defaults = { profile = { enable = true, items = { ["*"] = true } }, global = { localeCache = { ["*"] = {} } } }

function SI:OnInitialize()
    self.db = ns.DB:Namespace("SpecialItems", defaults)
    self.quests = {}
    self.byEvent = {}
    self:RegisterEvent("QUEST_LOG_UPDATE", "UpdateQuestList")
    self:RegisterEvent("QUEST_ACCEPTED", "UpdateQuestList")
    self:RegisterEvent("QUEST_REMOVED", "UpdateQuestList")
    self:RegisterEvent("PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD", "CheckAll")
    self:RegisterEvent("GET_ITEM_INFO_RECEIVED")
    for key, e in pairs(ENTRIES) do
        e.key = key
        for _, ev in ipairs(e.events or {}) do self:Subscribe(ev, e, false) end
        for _, ev in ipairs(e.unitEvents or {}) do self:Subscribe(ev, e, true) end
        local id = key:match("^item:(%d+)$")
        if id and not GetItemInfo(tonumber(id)) then C_Item.RequestLoadItemDataByID(tonumber(id)) end
    end
    ns:RegisterCallback(self, "ProfileChanged", function() SI:UpdateReadyEnabled() end)
end

function SI:Subscribe(event, entry, unit)
    if not self.byEvent[event] then
        self.byEvent[event] = {}
        local handler = function(_, ev, ...) for _, x in ipairs(SI.byEvent[ev]) do SI:Update(x.key, x.check(x, ev, ...)) end end
        if unit then self:RegisterUnitEvent(event, "player", handler) else self:RegisterEvent(event, handler) end
    end
    tinsert(self.byEvent[event], entry)
end

function SI:OnEnable()
    self:UpdateQuestList()
    self:UpdateReadyEnabled()
    self:CheckAll()
end

function SI:Update(key, value) ns.Ready:SetReady(key, not value) end

function SI:CheckAll()
    for key, e in pairs(ENTRIES) do self:Update(key, e.check(e, "Init")) end
end

function SI:UpdateQuestList()
    wipe(self.quests)
    for i = 1, C_QuestLog.GetNumQuestLogEntries() do
        local info = C_QuestLog.GetInfo(i)
        if info and not info.isHeader then self.quests[info.questID] = true end
    end
    for key, e in pairs(ENTRIES) do
        if e.quest then self:Update(key, e.check(e)) end
    end
end

function SI:IsEnabled() return self.db.profile.enable end
function SI:SetEnabled(v) self.db.profile.enable = v and true or false self:UpdateReadyEnabled() end
function SI:SetItemEnabled(key, v) self.db.profile.items[key] = v and true or false self:UpdateReadyEnabled() end

function SI:UpdateReadyEnabled()
    if not self.db then return end
    for key in pairs(ENTRIES) do
        ns.Ready:EnableCheck(key, self.db.profile.enable and self.db.profile.items[key])
    end
    ns.Ready:_Changed()
end

function SI:LocalizeString(key)
    local cache = self.db.global.localeCache[GetLocale()]
    if cache[key] then return cache[key] end
    local name
    local kind, id = key:match("^(%a+):(%d+)$")
    id = tonumber(id)
    if kind == "item" then
        name = GetItemInfo(id)
        if not name then C_Item.RequestLoadItemDataByID(id) end
    elseif kind == "quest" then
        name = C_QuestLog.GetTitleForQuestID(id)
    end
    if name then cache[key] = name return name end
    return key
end

function SI:GET_ITEM_INFO_RECEIVED(_, itemID, success)
    if success and ENTRIES["item:" .. tostring(itemID)] then ns:Fire("SpecialItemsChanged") end
end

function SI:List()
    local out = {}
    for key in pairs(ENTRIES) do
        out[#out + 1] = { key = key, name = self:LocalizeString(key), enabled = self.db.profile.items[key] and true or false }
    end
    sort(out, function(a, b) return a.name < b.name end)
    return out
end
```

```lua
local W = dofile("tests/wowstub.lua")
-- Hand-built AceSerializer rev 1 payload, as exported by PetCall 1.0.10+:
-- { enabled=true, priority=8, defaultValue=0, pets={[39]=64,[40]=0.125},
--   trigger={ {"specialLocation", loc="city"}, {"luacode", code="return function() return true end"}, {"bogus"} },
--   filter={ {"favorites", 8} } }
local V1 = "PetCall:1:^1^T^Senabled^B^Spriority^N8^SdefaultValue^N0"
    .. "^Spets^T^N39^N64^N40^F1^f-3^t"
    .. "^Strigger^T^N1^T^N1^SspecialLocation^Sloc^Scity^t"
    .. "^N2^T^N1^Sluacode^Scode^Sreturn~`function()~`return~`true~`end^t"
    .. "^N3^T^N1^Sbogus^t^t"
    .. "^Sfilter^T^N1^T^N1^Sfavorites^N2^N8^t^t^t^^"

describe("Share", function()
    local ns
    before_each(function()
        W.reset()
        W.world.pets = { a = { species = 39, name = "Squirrel" }, b = { species = 40, name = "Cat" } }
        ns = W.load()
        W.login()
        W.advance(1)
    end)

    it("decodes AceSerializer data", function()
        local ok, v = ns.LegacyDeserialize("^1^T^Sa^N1^Sb^F1^f-3^Sc^b^t^^")
        assert.is_true(ok)
        assert.same({ a = 1, b = 0.125, c = false }, v)
        assert.is_false((ns.LegacyDeserialize("garbage")))
    end)

    it("imports a 1.0 string with sanitising", function()
        local ok, name, info = ns.Share:Import(V1, "Imported")
        assert.is_true(ok)
        assert.equal("Imported", name)
        local s = ns.Sets:Get("Imported")
        assert.equal(8, s.settings.priority)
        assert.equal(64, s:GetPetWeight("a"))
        assert.equal(0.125, s:GetPetWeight("b"))
        assert.equal(2, #s.settings.trigger)              -- "bogus" dropped
        assert.is_true(s.settings.trigger[2].disabledByImport)
        assert.is_true(info.hasLuaCode)
        assert.equal(1, info.dropped)
        assert.same({ { "favorites", 8 } }, s.settings.filter)
    end)

    it("round-trips the new format", function()
        local set = ns.Sets:Create("Src")
        set:SetPetWeight("a", 8)
        set:ApplyTemplate("city")
        local str = ns.Share:Export("Src")
        assert.equal("PetCall:2:", str:sub(1, 10))
        local ok = ns.Share:Import(str, "Copy")
        assert.is_true(ok)
        local copy = ns.Sets:Get("Copy")
        assert.equal(8, copy:GetPetWeight("a"))
        assert.equal("specialLocation", copy.settings.trigger[1][1])
    end)

    it("rejects bad input and duplicate names", function()
        assert.is_false((ns.Share:Import("hello", "X")))
        assert.is_false((ns.Share:Import(V1, "")))
        ns.Sets:Create("Taken")
        assert.is_false((ns.Share:Import(V1, "Taken")))
    end)

    it("never executes imported code during import", function()
        _G.PC_IMPORT_RAN = false
        local evil = V1:gsub("return~`function%(%)~`return~`true~`end", "PC_IMPORT_RAN=true~`return~`function()~`return~`true~`end")
        ns.Share:Import(evil, "Evil")
        W.advance(5)
        assert.is_false(_G.PC_IMPORT_RAN)
    end)
end)
```

Jeśli test dekodowania nie przejdzie na tym stringu, wygeneruj `V1` prawdziwym `AceSerializer:Serialize` w `tools/reference_1_0.lua` (dopisz tam eksport zestawu z fixture'a) i wklej wynik — test ma odzwierciedlać prawdziwy format, nie nasze wyobrażenie o nim.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run → FAIL**
- [ ] **Step 3: `Core/LegacyDeserialize.lua`** (na podstawie AceSerializer-3.0, licencja BSD — zachowaj informację w komentarzu):

```lua
-- Decoder for the AceSerializer-3.0 rev 1 format (PetCall 1.x export strings).
-- Derived from AceSerializer-3.0 (Ace3, BSD licence).
local _, ns = ...

local gsub, gmatch, strchar, strbyte, tonumber, error, pcall = string.gsub, string.gmatch, string.char, string.byte, tonumber, error, pcall

local function unescape(esc)
    if esc < "~\122" then return strchar(strbyte(esc, 2, 2) - 64) end
    if esc == "~\122" then return "\030" end
    if esc == "~\123" then return "\127" end
    if esc == "~\124" then return "\126" end
    if esc == "~\125" then return "\94" end
    error("bad escape")
end

local function number(s)
    if s == "1.#INF" or s == "inf" then return math.huge end
    if s == "-1.#INF" or s == "-inf" then return -math.huge end
    return tonumber(s)
end

local value

function value(iter, single, ctl, data)
    if not single then ctl, data = iter() end
    if not ctl then error("missing terminator") end
    if ctl == "^^" then return end
    local res
    if ctl == "^S" then
        res = gsub(data, "~.", unescape)
    elseif ctl == "^N" then
        res = number(data) or error("bad number")
    elseif ctl == "^F" then
        local ctl2, e = iter()
        if ctl2 ~= "^f" then error("bad float") end
        local m, ex = tonumber(data), tonumber(e)
        if not (m and ex) then error("bad float") end
        res = m * (2 ^ ex)
    elseif ctl == "^B" then
        res = true
    elseif ctl == "^b" then
        res = false
    elseif ctl == "^Z" then
        res = nil
    elseif ctl == "^T" then
        res = {}
        while true do
            ctl, data = iter()
            if ctl == "^t" then break end
            local k = value(iter, true, ctl, data)
            if k == nil then error("bad table") end
            ctl, data = iter()
            local v = value(iter, true, ctl, data)
            if v == nil then error("bad table") end
            res[k] = v
        end
    else
        error("bad control code")
    end
    if single then return res end
    return res, value(iter)
end

function ns.LegacyDeserialize(str)
    str = gsub(str or "", "[%c ]", "")
    local iter = gmatch(str, "(^.)([^^]*)")
    local ctl = iter()
    if ctl ~= "^1" then return false, "not AceSerializer data" end
    return pcall(value, iter)
end
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `Core/Share.lua`**

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
local type, pairs, ipairs, tostring = type, pairs, ipairs, tostring

local Share = {}
ns.Share = Share

local V1, V2 = "PetCall:1:", "PetCall:2:"

function Share:Export(name)
    local set = ns.Sets:Get(name)
    if not set then return nil, L["Set not found."] end
    local st = set.settings
    local pets = {}
    for petID, w in pairs(st.pets) do
        local species = ns.PetCache:GetSpeciesID(petID)
        if species and type(w) == "number" and (pets[species] == nil or w > pets[species]) then pets[species] = w end
    end
    local data = { enabled = st.enabled, priority = st.priority, defaultValue = st.defaultValue,
        immediate = st.immediate, pets = pets, filter = st.filter, trigger = st.trigger }
    local cbor = C_EncodingUtil.SerializeCBOR(data)
    return V2 .. C_EncodingUtil.EncodeBase64(C_EncodingUtil.CompressString(cbor))
end

local function decode(str)
    str = strtrim(str or "")
    if str:sub(1, #V2) == V2 then
        local ok, data = pcall(function()
            local raw = C_EncodingUtil.DecodeBase64(str:sub(#V2 + 1))
            return C_EncodingUtil.DeserializeCBOR(C_EncodingUtil.DecompressString(raw))
        end)
        if ok and type(data) == "table" then return data end
        return nil, L["The import string is damaged."]
    elseif str:sub(1, #V1) == V1 then
        local ok, data = ns.LegacyDeserialize(str:sub(#V1 + 1))
        if ok and type(data) == "table" then return data end
        return nil, L["The import string is damaged."]
    end
    return nil, L["This is not a PetCall export string."]
end

local function sanitize(list, isKnown, info)
    local out = {}
    if type(list) ~= "table" then return out end
    for _, e in ipairs(list) do
        if type(e) == "table" and type(e[1]) == "string" then
            if e[1] == "AND" or e[1] == "OR" then
                e.items = sanitize(e.items, isKnown, info)
                out[#out + 1] = e
            elseif isKnown(e[1]) then
                if e[1] == "luacode" then
                    e.disabledByImport = true
                    info.hasLuaCode = true
                end
                out[#out + 1] = e
            else
                info.dropped = info.dropped + 1
            end
        else
            info.dropped = info.dropped + 1
        end
    end
    return out
end

local function knownFilter(key)
    for _, def in ipairs(ns.AutoRules.Types()) do if def.key == key then return true end end
    return false
end
local function knownCondition(key) return ns.Conditions.GetType(key) ~= nil end

function Share:Import(str, name)
    local data, err = decode(str)
    if not data then return false, err end
    local okName, nameErr = ns.Sets:ValidateName(name)
    if not okName then return false, nameErr end
    local info = { hasLuaCode = false, dropped = 0 }
    local set = ns.Sets:Create(name)
    local st = set.settings
    st.enabled = data.enabled ~= false
    st.priority = type(data.priority) == "number" and data.priority or 1
    st.defaultValue = type(data.defaultValue) == "number" and data.defaultValue or 0
    st.immediate = data.immediate and true or nil
    st.filter = sanitize(data.filter, knownFilter, info)
    st.trigger = sanitize(data.trigger, knownCondition, info)
    wipe(st.pets)
    if type(data.pets) == "table" then
        for _, petID in ns.PetCache:IteratePetIDs() do
            local w = data.pets[ns.PetCache:GetSpeciesID(petID)]
            if type(w) == "number" then st.pets[petID] = w end
        end
    end
    -- rebuild the watcher on the new trigger table
    if set.watcher then set.watcher:Free() end
    ns.Conditions.Normalize(st.trigger)
    set.watcher = ns.Conditions.NewWatcher(st.trigger, function()
        ns:Fire("ConditionStateChanged", set.name)
        ns.Sets:Update()
    end)
    set:ConditionsChanged()
    return true, set.name, info
end
```

```lua
    ButtonFrameTemplate_HideButtonBar(dialog)

    dialog:SetMovable(true)
    dialog:EnableMouse(true)
    dialog:RegisterForDrag("LeftButton")
    dialog:SetScript("OnDragStart", dialog.StartMoving)
    dialog:SetScript("OnDragStop", dialog.StopMovingOrSizing)

    ns.Skin:Apply("CloseButton", dialog.CloseButton)

    dialog.hint:SetPoint("RIGHT", -8, 0)

    sf:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 8, -28)
    sf:SetPoint("RIGHT", -28, 0)
```
