```lua
    dialog.text:SetFontObject("ChatFontNormal")
    dialog.text:SetWidth(460)
    dialog.text:SetAutoFocus(false)
    dialog.text:SetScript("OnEscapePressed", function() dialog:Hide() end)
    sf:SetScrollChild(dialog.text)

    dialog.nameLabel = ns.Widgets.Label(dialog.Inset, L["Set name:"])
    dialog.nameLabel:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", sf, "BOTTOMLEFT", 0, -12)
    dialog.name = CreateFrame("EditBox", nil, dialog.Inset, "InputBoxTemplate")
    dialog.name:SetSize(220, 20)
    dialog.name:SetAutoFocus(false)
    dialog.name:SetPoint("LEFT", dialog.nameLabel, "RIGHT", 10, 0)
    ns.Skin:Apply("EditBox", dialog.name)

    dialog.status = ns.Widgets.Label(dialog.Inset, "", "GameFontHighlightSmall")
    dialog.status:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", dialog.nameLabel, "BOTTOMLEFT", 0, -10)
    dialog.status:SetPoint("RIGHT", -8, 0)
    dialog.status:SetWordWrap(true)

    dialog.import = ns.Widgets.Button(dialog.Inset, L["Import"], function()
        local ok, result, info = ns.Share:Import(dialog.text:GetText(), dialog.name:GetText())
        if not ok then
            dialog.status:SetText("|cffff4040" .. tostring(result) .. "|r")
            return
        end
        dialog:Hide()
        ns.UI.Main:Show()
        ns.UI.Main:SelectSet(result)
        if info.hasLuaCode then
            ns:Print(L["The imported set contains Lua code. It is disabled until you review it in the When tab."])
            ns.UI.Main:SelectTab("when")
        end
    end)
    dialog.import:SetPoint("BOTTOMRIGHT", -8, 8)
    dialog.name:SetScript("OnEnterPressed", function() dialog.import:Click() end)
end

function Share:ShowExport(name)
    if not dialog then build() end
    local str, err = ns.Share:Export(name)
    dialog:SetTitle(format(L["Export: %s"], ns.Sets:Get(name):DisplayName()))
    dialog.hint:SetText(L["All text is selected – press Ctrl+C to copy."])
    dialog.text:SetText(str or err)
    for _, w in ipairs({ dialog.nameLabel, dialog.name, dialog.status, dialog.import }) do w:Hide() end
    dialog:Show()
    dialog.text:SetFocus()
    dialog.text:HighlightText()
end

function Share:ShowImport()
    if not dialog then build() end
    dialog:SetTitle(L["Import set"])
    dialog.hint:SetText(L["Paste a PetCall export string below:"])
    dialog.text:SetText("")
    dialog.name:SetText("")
    dialog.status:SetText("")
    for _, w in ipairs({ dialog.nameLabel, dialog.name, dialog.status, dialog.import }) do w:Show() end
    dialog:Show()
    dialog.text:SetFocus()
end
```

```lua
local W = dofile("tests/wowstub.lua")
describe("Migrate", function()
    it("prompts when PetLeash has sets and PetCall has none, and migrates", function()
        W.reset()
        PetLeash3DB = { profiles = { Default = { sets = { City = { name = "City" } } } } }
        local ns = W.load()

        assert.equal("PETCALL_MIGRATE_PETLEASH", W.world.popup)

        PetLeash3DB = { profiles = { Default = { sets = { City = {} } } } }

        W.load()
        W.login()
        assert.is_nil(W.world.popup)
    end)
    it("prints once after the reload", function()
        W.reset()
        PetCall3DB = { _petleashMigrated = true }
        W.load()
        W.login()
        assert.is_nil(PetCall3DB._petleashMigrated)
        assert.is_true(W.world.printed[#W.world.printed]:find("migrated") ~= nil)
    end)
end)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `Core/Migrate.lua`**

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
local Migrate = ns:NewModule("Migrate")
ns.Migrate = Migrate

local function hasUserSets(db)
    if type(db) ~= "table" or type(db.profiles) ~= "table" then return false end
    for _, profile in pairs(db.profiles) do
        if type(profile.sets) == "table" then
            for key in pairs(profile.sets) do
                if key ~= "$Default" then return true end
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end

function Migrate:HasPetLeashData()
    return type(PetLeash3DB) == "table" and type(PetLeash3DB.profiles) == "table" and next(PetLeash3DB.profiles) ~= nil
end

StaticPopupDialogs.PETCALL_MIGRATE_PETLEASH = {
    text = L["MIGRATE_PETLEASH_PROMPT"], button1 = YES, button2 = NO,
    OnAccept = function() Migrate:Run() end,
    timeout = 0, whileDead = true, hideOnEscape = true,
}

function Migrate:Prompt()
    if not self:HasPetLeashData() then return ns:Print(L["No PetLeash data found."]) end
    StaticPopup_Show("PETCALL_MIGRATE_PETLEASH")
end

function Migrate:Run()
    PetCall3DB = CopyTable(PetLeash3DB)
    PetCall3DB._petleashMigrated = true
    PetLeash3DB = nil
    ReloadUI()
end

function Migrate:OnEnable()
    if ns.db.sv._petleashMigrated then
        ns.db.sv._petleashMigrated = nil
        ns:Print(L["MIGRATED_FROM_PETLEASH"])
    elseif self:HasPetLeashData() and not hasUserSets(ns.db.sv) then
        StaticPopup_Show("PETCALL_MIGRATE_PETLEASH")
    end
end
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ns.db.profile`, `ns.Ready:UpdateChecks()`, `ns.SwitchTimer:Start()`, `ns.SpecialItems:*`, `ns.UI.Main:SetScale`, `ns.DB` (profile), `ns.UI.Broker:SetMinimapShown(v)` (Task 22 — do tego czasu wywołanie warunkowe).

Zmień w spec §10 zdanie o sekcji „O dodatku” na: „Podkategoria „O dodatku” (canvas): …” i w §16 wiersz „Panel About” na „Ustawienia › O dodatku (podkategoria)”.

- [ ] **Step 1: `UI/Settings.lua`**

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
ns.UI = ns.UI or {}
local S = {}
ns.UI.Settings = S

local category, layout

local function onChanged() ns.Ready:UpdateChecks() end

local function proxy(key, varType, label, default, get, set, tooltip, kind, options)
    local setting = Settings.RegisterProxySetting(category, "PETCALL_" .. key, varType, label, default, get, set)
    if kind == "slider" then
        Settings.CreateSlider(category, setting, options, tooltip)
    elseif kind == "dropdown" then
        Settings.CreateDropdown(category, setting, options, tooltip)
    else
        Settings.CreateCheckbox(category, setting, tooltip)
    end
    return setting
end

local function profileBool(key, label, tooltip, after)
    return proxy(key, Settings.VarType.Boolean, label, ns.DB.defaults[key],
        function() return ns.db.profile[key] end,
        function(v) ns.db.profile[key] = v onChanged() if after then after(v) end end, tooltip)
end

-- three-state "where" lists map onto the 1.x pair of booleans
local function whereSetting(key, label, tooltip, enableKey, dismissKey)
    local function get()
        local p = ns.db.profile
        if p[dismissKey] then return 3 end
        return p[enableKey] and 1 or 2
    end
    local function set(v)
        local p = ns.db.profile
        p[enableKey], p[dismissKey] = v == 1, v == 3
        onChanged()
    end
    local function options()
        local c = Settings.CreateControlTextContainer()
        c:Add(1, L["Summon"])
        c:Add(2, L["Don't summon"])
        c:Add(3, L["Dismiss"])
        return c:GetData()
    end
    proxy(key, Settings.VarType.Number, label, 1, get, set, tooltip, "dropdown", options)
end

local function header(text) layout:AddInitializer(CreateSettingsListSectionHeaderInitializer(text)) end

local function build()
    category, layout = Settings.RegisterVerticalLayoutCategory("PetCall")

    layout:AddInitializer(CreateSettingsButtonInitializer(L["PetCall window"], L["Open"], function()
        SettingsPanel:Hide()
        ns:OpenWindow()
    end, L["Pick pets, create sets and set their conditions."], true))

    header(GENERAL)
    proxy("enable", Settings.VarType.Boolean, L["Auto-summon"], true,
        function() return ns:IsAutoSummon() end, function(v) ns:SetAutoSummon(v) end,
        L["Automatically summon a companion pet when entering a new area or when idle."])
    profileBool("nearPEWOnly", L["Only Summon After Zoning"], L["Only summon a pet when entering a new area or zone. When disabled, a pet is also summoned after standing still for the configured wait time."])
    do
        local opts = Settings.CreateSliderOptions(1, 30, 0.5)
        opts:SetLabelFormatter(MinimalSliderWithSteppersMixin.Label.Right, function(v) return format(L["%.1f s"], v) end)
        proxy("waitTimer", Settings.VarType.Number, L["Wait Time (Seconds)"], 3,
            function() return ns.db.profile.waitTimer end, function(v) ns.db.profile.waitTimer = v end,
            L["Seconds of inactivity before PetCall summons a pet."], "slider", opts)
    end
    profileBool("verbose", L["Chat messages"], L["Print a message in the chat window each time a pet is summoned."])
    proxy("minimap", Settings.VarType.Boolean, L["Show Minimap Button"], false,
        function() return not ns.db.profile.minimap.hide end,
        function(v) ns.db.profile.minimap.hide = not v if ns.UI.Broker then ns.UI.Broker:SetMinimapShown(v) end end,
        L["Show the PetCall icon on the minimap."])
    profileBool("overrideSetLoadout", L["Override Pet Battle Loadout"], L["Re-summon your PetCall pet after the game auto-summons one via the Pet Battle Loadout screen."])
    do
        local opts = Settings.CreateSliderOptions(0.8, 1.3, 0.05)
        opts:SetLabelFormatter(MinimalSliderWithSteppersMixin.Label.Right, function(v) return format("%d%%", v * 100 + 0.5) end)
        proxy("windowScale", Settings.VarType.Number, L["PetCall window scale"], 1,
            function() return ns.db.global.window.scale or 1 end, function(v) ns.UI.Main:SetScale(v) end,
            L["Scale of the PetCall window on top of the game's UI scale."], "slider", opts)
    end

    header(L["Where"])
    whereSetting("bg", L["Battlegrounds and arenas"], L["What PetCall does in battlegrounds and arenas."], "enableInBattleground", "dismissInBattleground")
    whereSetting("pve", L["Dungeons and raids"], L["What PetCall does in dungeons and raids."], "enableInPVE", "dismissInPVE")
    profileBool("disableOutsideCities", L["Only Enable in Cities"], L["Restrict pet summoning to city areas only. Your pet will be dismissed when you leave a city."])
    profileBool("dismissWhenStealthed", L["Dismiss When Stealthed or Invisible"], L["Automatically dismiss your pet when you enter stealth or use an invisibility effect."])

    header(L["Auto Switch Pet"])
    proxy("autoSwitchTimer", Settings.VarType.Boolean, L["Enable Timed Auto Switch"], false,
        function() return ns.db.profile.autoSwitch.timer end,
        function(v) ns.db.profile.autoSwitch.timer = v if v then ns.SwitchTimer:Start() else ns.SwitchTimer:Stop() end end,
        L["Automatically switch to a different random pet from your selection after a set amount of time."])
    do
        local opts = Settings.CreateSliderOptions(1, 60, 1)
        opts:SetLabelFormatter(MinimalSliderWithSteppersMixin.Label.Right, function(v) return format(L["%d min"], v) end)
        proxy("autoSwitchMinutes", Settings.VarType.Number, L["Switch every"], 30,
            function() return math.floor(ns.db.profile.autoSwitch.timerValue / 60 + 0.5) end,
            function(v) ns.db.profile.autoSwitch.timerValue = v * 60 ns.SwitchTimer:Start() end,
            L["How often to switch to a different pet."], "slider", opts)
    end
    proxy("autoSwitchCities", Settings.VarType.Boolean, L["Only use Timed Auto Switch in cities"], false,
        function() return ns.db.profile.autoSwitch.citiesOnly end, function(v) ns.db.profile.autoSwitch.citiesOnly = v end,
        L["Restrict timed pet switching to city areas."])
    proxy("autoSwitchZone", Settings.VarType.Boolean, L["Auto Switch when changing maps"], false,
        function() return ns.db.profile.autoSwitch.onZone end, function(v) ns.db.profile.autoSwitch.onZone = v end,
        L["Switch to a different random pet each time you travel to a new map or zone."])

    header(L["Special Items"])
    proxy("specialItems", Settings.VarType.Boolean, ENABLE, true,
        function() return ns.SpecialItems:IsEnabled() end, function(v) ns.SpecialItems:SetEnabled(v) end,
        L["EXPLAIN_SPECIAL_ITEMS"])
    for _, item in ipairs(ns.SpecialItems:List()) do
        local key = item.key
        local setting = Settings.RegisterProxySetting(category, "PETCALL_SI_" .. key:gsub(":", "_"), Settings.VarType.Boolean,
            item.name, true,
            function() return ns.SpecialItems.db.profile.items[key] and true or false end,
            function(v) ns.SpecialItems:SetItemEnabled(key, v) end)
        Settings.CreateCheckbox(category, setting)
    end

    Settings.RegisterAddOnCategory(category)
    S.category = category
    ns.UI.Profiles:Register(category)
    S:RegisterAbout(category)
end

function S:RegisterAbout(parent)
    local f = CreateFrame("Frame")
    f:Hide()
    f:SetScript("OnShow", function(self)
        if self.built then return end
        self.built = true
        local y = -16
        local function line(text, font, indent)
            local fs = ns.Widgets.Label(self, text, font)
            fs:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 16 + (indent or 0), y)
            fs:SetPoint("RIGHT", -16, 0)
            fs:SetWordWrap(true)
            y = y - fs:GetStringHeight() - 6
        end
        local version, _, _, toc = GetBuildInfo()
        line("PetCall " .. ns.version, "GameFontNormalLarge")
        line(format(L["WoW %s · Interface %d"], version, toc), "GameFontDisableSmall")
        y = y - 8
        line(L["What's new"], "GameFontNormal")
        for _, note in ipairs(ns.releaseNotes or {}) do line("• " .. note, "GameFontHighlightSmall", 8) end
        y = y - 8
        line(L["Commands"], "GameFontNormal")
        for _, c in ipairs(ns.Commands:HelpLines()) do line(c, "GameFontHighlightSmall", 8) end
        y = y - 8
        line(L["Key bindings"], "GameFontNormal")
        for _, b in ipairs({ "PETCALL_SUMMON", "PETCALL_DESUMMON", "PETCALL_TOGGLE", "PETCALL_CONFIG" }) do
            line(format("%s: %s", _G["BINDING_NAME_" .. b], GetBindingKey(b) or L["not bound"]), "GameFontHighlightSmall", 8)
        end
        y = y - 8
        line(L["Credits"], "GameFontNormal")
        line("Cryzer — PetCall 2.0", "GameFontHighlightSmall", 8)
        line("End — PetLeash", "GameFontHighlightSmall", 8)
        line(L["Translations by the WoW community."], "GameFontHighlightSmall", 8)
    end)
    Settings.RegisterCanvasLayoutSubcategory(parent, f, L["About"])
end

local pendingOpen
function ns:OpenSettings()
    if InCombatLockdown() then
        if not pendingOpen then
            pendingOpen = {}
            ns:Print(L["Settings will open after combat."])
            ns.Events.Register(pendingOpen, "PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED", function()
                ns.Events.Unregister(pendingOpen, "PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED")
                pendingOpen = nil
                Settings.OpenToCategory(category:GetID())
            end)
        end
        return
    end
    Settings.OpenToCategory(category:GetID())
end

local loader = {}
ns.Events.Register(loader, "PLAYER_LOGIN", function()
    ns.Events.Unregister(loader, "PLAYER_LOGIN")
    build()
end)

-- 1.x key binding names (Bindings.xml)
BINDING_HEADER_PETCALL = "PetCall"
BINDING_NAME_PETCALL_SUMMON = L["Summon Another Pet"]
BINDING_NAME_PETCALL_DESUMMON = L["Dismiss Pet"]
BINDING_NAME_PETCALL_TOGGLE = L["Toggle Non-Combat Pet"]
BINDING_NAME_PETCALL_CONFIG = L["Open PetCall"]
```

> Ustawienia są budowane po `PLAYER_LOGIN`, bo potrzebują wczytanej bazy danych i listy przedmiotów. Po zmianie profilu Blizzard odczytuje wartości przez gettery proxy przy każdym otwarciu panelu, więc nie trzeba ich przebudowywać.
>
> `ns.releaseNotes`: w `ReleaseNotes.lua` zamień `local addon_name, addon = ...` / `addon.releaseNotes` na `local _, ns = ...` / `ns.releaseNotes` i to samo w generatorze w `.github/workflows/release.yml` (dwie linie `printf`). `ReleaseNotes.lua` w TOC na końcu listy.

- [ ] **Step 2: `UI/Profiles.lua`**

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
ns.UI = ns.UI or {}
local P = {}
ns.UI.Profiles = P

local frame

local function refresh()
    if not frame then return end
    frame.current:GenerateMenu()
    frame.copy:GenerateMenu()
    frame.delete:GenerateMenu()
end

local function profileMenu(onPick, skipCurrent)
    return function(owner, root)
        for _, name in ipairs(ns.DB:List()) do
            if not (skipCurrent and name == ns.DB:Current()) then
                root:CreateRadio(name, function() return not skipCurrent and name == ns.DB:Current() end, function() onPick(name) end)
            end
        end
    end
end

local function report(ok, err) if not ok and err then UIErrorsFrame:AddMessage(err, 1, 0.3, 0.3) end refresh() end

function P:Register(parent)
    frame = CreateFrame("Frame")
    frame:Hide()
    local y = -16
    local function row(label, control)
        local l = ns.Widgets.Label(frame, label)
        l:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 16, y)
        l:SetWidth(180)
        control:SetPoint("LEFT", l, "RIGHT", 10, 0)
        y = y - 36
        return control
    end
    frame.current = row(L["Current profile"], ns.Widgets.Dropdown(frame, 220, profileMenu(function(n) report(ns.DB:Set(n)) end)))
    local new = CreateFrame("EditBox", nil, frame, "InputBoxTemplate")
    new:SetSize(200, 20)
    new:SetAutoFocus(false)
    new:SetScript("OnEnterPressed", function(self) report(ns.DB:Set(self:GetText())) self:SetText("") self:ClearFocus() end)
    row(L["New profile (Enter)"], new)
    frame.copy = row(L["Copy from"], ns.Widgets.Dropdown(frame, 220, profileMenu(function(n)
        StaticPopup_Show("PETCALL_CONFIRM", format(L["Overwrite the current profile with \"%s\"?"], n), nil, { onAccept = function() report(ns.DB:Copy(n)) end })
    end, true)))
    frame.delete = row(L["Delete profile"], ns.Widgets.Dropdown(frame, 220, profileMenu(function(n)
        StaticPopup_Show("PETCALL_CONFIRM", format(L["Delete profile \"%s\"?"], n), nil, { onAccept = function() report(ns.DB:Delete(n)) end })
    end, true)))
    local reset = ns.Widgets.Button(frame, L["Reset current profile"], function()
        StaticPopup_Show("PETCALL_CONFIRM", L["Reset the current profile to defaults?"], nil, { onAccept = function() ns.DB:Reset() refresh() end })
    end)
    reset:SetPoint("TOPLEFT", 16, y)
    frame:SetScript("OnShow", refresh)
    Settings.RegisterCanvasLayoutSubcategory(parent, frame, L["Profiles"])
end

ns:RegisterCallback(P, "ProfileChanged", refresh)
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `LibStub("LibDataBroker-1.1")`, `LibStub("LibDBIcon-1.0")`, `ns:GetCurrentPet`, `ns.Sets:GetCurrent`, `ns.Ready:GetBlockers`, `ns:IsAutoSummon`, `ns:TogglePet`, `ns:SummonAnother`, `ns:Dismiss`, `ns:GetRecentPets`, `ns:SummonPet`, `ns:CreateModelPreview`.
- Produces: `ns.UI.Broker:FillTooltip(tt)`, `:OnClick(frame, button)`, `:SetMinimapShown(v)`; `ns.UI.Menu:Open(anchor)`; `ns.Commands:HelpLines() → {string}`; globale `PetCall_OnAddonCompartmentClick/Enter/Leave`.

- [ ] **Step 1: `UI/Menu.lua`**

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
ns.UI = ns.UI or {}
local Menu = {}
ns.UI.Menu = Menu

local PAGE = 25
local preview

local function iterUtf8(s) return s:gmatch("([%z\1-\127\194-\244][\128-\191]*)") end

-- shortest distinguishing prefixes for a page label "Ab – Ca"
local function prefix(a, b)
    if not b then return iterUtf8(a)() end
    local ia, ib, r = iterUtf8(a), iterUtf8(b), ""
    local ca, cb = ia(), ib()
    while ca and cb do
        if ca ~= cb then return r .. ca end
        r = r .. ca
        ca, cb = ia(), ib()
    end
    return r
end

local function petName(id) local i = ns.PetCache:GetInfo(id) return i and (i.customName or i.name) or "?" end

local function addPet(root, id)
    local info = ns.PetCache:GetInfo(id)
    if not info then return end
    local b = root:CreateButton(format("|T%s:16|t %s", info.icon or 0, petName(id)), function() ns:SummonPet(id) end)
    b:SetOnEnter(function(frame)
        preview = preview or ns:CreateModelPreview("PetCallMenuPreview")
        preview:ShowForCreature(info.creatureID, "LEFT", frame, "RIGHT", 8, 0)
    end)
    b:SetOnLeave(function() if preview then preview:ScheduleHide() end end)
end

function Menu:Build(owner, root)
    root:CreateTitle("PetCall")
    root:CreateButton(L["Summon another"], function() ns:SummonAnother() end)
    root:CreateButton(L["Dismiss pet"], function() ns:Dismiss() end)
    root:CreateCheckbox(L["Auto-summon"], function() return ns:IsAutoSummon() end, function() ns:SetAutoSummon(not ns:IsAutoSummon()) end)
    local set = ns.Sets:GetCurrent()
    root:CreateTitle(format("%s: %s", L["Active"], set and set:DisplayName() or NONE))
    local recent = ns:GetRecentPets()
    if #recent > 0 then
        local r = root:CreateButton(L["Recently summoned"])
        for _, id in ipairs(recent) do addPet(r, id) end
    end
    local all = root:CreateButton(L["All pets"])
    local ids = {}
    for _, id in ns.PetCache:IteratePetIDs() do ids[#ids + 1] = id end
    table.sort(ids, function(a, b) return strlower(petName(a)) < strlower(petName(b)) end)
    for start = 1, #ids, PAGE do
        local stop = math.min(start + PAGE - 1, #ids)
        local first, last = petName(ids[start]), petName(ids[stop])
        local label = format("%s – %s", prefix(first, start > 1 and petName(ids[start - 1]) or nil),
            prefix(last, stop < #ids and petName(ids[stop + 1]) or nil))
        local page = all:CreateButton(label)
        page:SetScrollMode(20 * 26)
        for i = start, stop do addPet(page, ids[i]) end
    end
    root:CreateDivider()
    root:CreateButton(L["Open PetCall"], function() ns:OpenWindow() end)
    root:CreateButton(L["Settings"], function() ns:OpenSettings() end)
end

function Menu:Open(anchor)
    MenuUtil.CreateContextMenu(anchor, function(owner, root) Menu:Build(owner, root) end)
end
```

> `prefix` dla etykiety zakresu bierze najkrótszy prefiks odróżniający pierwszego peta strony od ostatniego z poprzedniej strony (i analogicznie koniec), jak `BrokerMenu_PetRangeString` z 1.0.x.

- [ ] **Step 2: `UI/Broker.lua`**

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
ns.UI = ns.UI or {}
local Broker = {}
ns.UI.Broker = Broker

local LDB = LibStub("LibDataBroker-1.1")
local LDBIcon = LibStub("LibDBIcon-1.0")
local ICON = "Interface\\AddOns\\PetCall\\Media\\icon"
local obj

function Broker:FillTooltip(tt)
    tt:AddLine("PetCall")
    tt:AddDoubleLine(L["Auto-summon"], ns:IsAutoSummon() and ("|cff40ff40" .. L["On"] .. "|r") or ("|cffff4040" .. L["Off"] .. "|r"))
    local set = ns.Sets:GetCurrent()
    tt:AddDoubleLine(L["Active set"], set and set:DisplayName() or NONE, nil, nil, nil, 1, 1, 1)
    local pet = ns:GetCurrentPet()
    local info = pet and ns.PetCache:GetInfo(pet)
    tt:AddDoubleLine(L["Pet"], info and (info.customName or info.name) or L["none"], nil, nil, nil, 1, 1, 1)
    local blockers = ns.Ready:GetBlockers()
    if #blockers > 0 then
        local labels = {}
        for _, b in ipairs(blockers) do labels[#labels + 1] = b.label end
        tt:AddLine(L["Paused by"] .. ": " .. table.concat(labels, ", "), 1, 0.7, 0.25, true)
    end
    tt:AddLine(format(L["You have %d pets"], ns.PetCache:NumPets()), 0.6, 0.6, 0.6)
    tt:AddLine(" ")
    tt:AddLine("|cff69b950" .. L["Left-Click:"] .. "|r " .. L["Toggle Non-Combat Pet"], 1, 1, 1)
    tt:AddLine("|cff69b950" .. L["Right-Click:"] .. "|r " .. L["Pet Menu"], 1, 1, 1)
    tt:AddLine("|cff69b950" .. L["Ctrl + Click:"] .. "|r " .. L["Open PetCall"], 1, 1, 1)
end

function Broker:OnClick(frame, button)
    if IsControlKeyDown() then
        ns:OpenWindow()
    elseif button == "RightButton" then
        ns.UI.Menu:Open(frame)
    else
        ns:TogglePet()
    end
end

local function update()
    if not obj then return end
    local pet = ns:GetCurrentPet()
    local info = pet and ns.PetCache:GetInfo(pet)
    obj.text = info and (info.customName or info.name) or "PetCall"
    local on = ns:IsAutoSummon()
    obj.iconR, obj.iconG, obj.iconB = 1, on and 1 or 0.3, on and 1 or 0.3
end

function Broker:SetMinimapShown(v)
    if v then LDBIcon:Show("PetCall") else LDBIcon:Hide("PetCall") end
end

local loader = {}
ns.Events.Register(loader, "PLAYER_LOGIN", function()
    ns.Events.Unregister(loader, "PLAYER_LOGIN")
    obj = LDB:NewDataObject("PetCall", {
        type = "data source", label = "PetCall", text = "PetCall", icon = ICON,
        OnClick = function(frame, button) Broker:OnClick(frame, button) end,
        OnTooltipShow = function(tt) Broker:FillTooltip(tt) end,
    })
    LDBIcon:Register("PetCall", obj, ns.db.profile.minimap)
    update()
end)

for _, cb in ipairs({ "SummonedPetChanged", "AutoSummonChanged", "PetsChanged", "CurrentSetChanged" }) do
    ns:RegisterCallback(Broker, cb, update)
end
ns:RegisterCallback(Broker, "ProfileChanged", function()
    LDBIcon:Refresh("PetCall", ns.db.profile.minimap)
    update()
end)

function PetCall_OnAddonCompartmentClick(_, button) Broker:OnClick(AddonCompartmentFrame, button) end
function PetCall_OnAddonCompartmentEnter(_, frame)
    GameTooltip:SetOwner(frame, "ANCHOR_LEFT")
    Broker:FillTooltip(GameTooltip)
    GameTooltip:Show()
end
function PetCall_OnAddonCompartmentLeave() GameTooltip:Hide() end
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `Core/Commands.lua`** — pełna wersja (zastępuje minimalną z Task 9):

```lua
local _, ns = ...

local L = ns.L
local Commands = ns:NewModule("Commands")
ns.Commands = Commands

local list = {
    { "config", L["Open the PetCall window"], function() ns:OpenWindow() end },
    { "settings", L["Open the settings"], function() ns:OpenSettings() end },
    { "resummon", L["Summon another pet; turns auto-summon on"], function() ns:ResummonPet(true, true) end },
    { "dismiss", L["Dismiss your pet; turns auto-summon off"], function() ns:DismissPet(true) end },
    { "desummon", nil, function() ns:DismissPet(true) end },
    { "togglePet", L["Toggle your pet and auto-summon"], function() ns:TogglePet() end },
    { "enable", L["Toggle auto-summon"], function() ns:SetAutoSummon(not ns:IsAutoSummon()) end },
    { "dismissWhenStealthed", L["Toggle dismissing in stealth"], function()
        ns.db.profile.dismissWhenStealthed = not ns.db.profile.dismissWhenStealthed
        ns.Ready:UpdateChecks()
        ns:Printf("%s: %s", L["Dismiss When Stealthed or Invisible"], ns.db.profile.dismissWhenStealthed and L["On"] or L["Off"])
    end },
    { "migrate", L["Import PetLeash data"], function() ns.Migrate:Prompt() end },
    { "debugstate", nil, function()
        ns:Printf("%s: %s", L["Auto-summon"], ns:IsAutoSummon() and L["On"] or L["Off"])
        ns.Ready:DumpDebugState()
    end },
    { "help", L["List commands"], function()
        for _, line in ipairs(Commands:HelpLines()) do ns:Print(line) end
    end },
}

local byName = {}
for _, c in ipairs(list) do byName[strlower(c[1])] = c end

function Commands:HelpLines()
    local out = { "/pcall, /petcall — " .. L["Open the PetCall window"] }
    for _, c in ipairs(list) do
        if c[2] then out[#out + 1] = format("/pcall %s — %s", c[1], c[2]) end
    end
    return out
end

function Commands:Handle(input)
    input = strtrim(input or "")
    if input == "" then return ns:OpenWindow() end
    local cmd = input:match("^(%S+)")
    local c = byName[strlower(cmd)]
    if c then return c[3]() end
    ns:Printf(L["Unknown command: %s. Type /pcall help."], cmd)
end

function Commands:OnInitialize()
    SLASH_PETCALL1, SLASH_PETCALL2 = "/petcall", "/pcall"
    SlashCmdList.PETCALL = function(msg) Commands:Handle(msg) end
end
```

```lua
local W = dofile("tests/wowstub.lua")
describe("Commands", function()
    it("dispatches 1.x subcommands case-insensitively", function()
        W.reset()
        W.world.pets = { a = { species = 1, name = "A" } }
        local ns = W.load()
        W.login()
        W.advance(1)
        ns.OpenWindow = function() W.world.opened = true end
        SlashCmdList.PETCALL("")
        assert.is_true(W.world.opened)
        SlashCmdList.PETCALL("ENABLE")
        assert.is_false(ns:IsAutoSummon())
        SlashCmdList.PETCALL("dismissWhenStealthed")
        assert.is_true(ns.db.profile.dismissWhenStealthed)
        SlashCmdList.PETCALL("nope")
        assert.is_true(W.world.printed[#W.world.printed]:find("Unknown command") ~= nil)
    end)
end)
```
